if type(protectgui) ~= "function" and _syn and type(_syn.protect_gui) == "function" then
    protectgui = _syn.protect_gui
end

if type(getcustomasset) ~= "function" then
    if type(getexecutorasset) == "function" then
        getcustomasset = getexecutorasset
    elseif type(getsynasset) == "function" then
        getcustomasset = getsynasset
    elseif _syn and type(_syn.getcustomasset) == "function" then
        getcustomasset = _syn.getcustomasset
    elseif _syn and type(_syn.getsynasset) == "function" then
        getcustomasset = _syn.getsynasset
    end
end

if type(readfile) ~= "function" and _syn and type(_syn.readfile) == "function" then readfile = _syn.readfile end
if type(writefile) ~= "function" and _syn and type(_syn.writefile) == "function" then writefile = _syn.writefile end
if type(delfile) ~= "function" and _syn and type(_syn.delfile) == "function" then delfile = _syn.delfile end

    if type(get_signal_cons) == "function" then
        getconnections = get_signal_cons
    elseif type(getconnects) == "function" then
        getconnections = getconnects
    elseif _syn and type(_syn.get_signal_cons) == "function" then
        getconnections = _syn.get_signal_cons
    end
end

function _xluCompatGuiParent()
    if type(gethui) == "function" then
        local ok, parent = pcall(gethui)
        if ok and parent then return parent end
    end
    local okCore, core = pcall(function() return CoreGui end)
    if okCore and core then return core end
    return nil
end

LP = Players.LocalPlayer
PlayerGui = LP:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")

-- Meridian Duels -- Adapt speed method doesn't spoof velocity, so these
-- are now plain passthroughs (no hookmetamethod). Kept only so Auto
-- Left/Right/Infinite Jump (which call them) don't break - their own
-- feature logic is untouched.
local spoofedVelocity = Vector3.zero
local function getRealVelocity(root)
    return root and root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
end
local function writeRealVelocity(root, value)
    if root then root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = value end
end

function getUndetectedGuiParent()

    if type(gethui) == "function" then
        local ok, parent = pcall(gethui)
        if ok and parent then return parent end
    end

    if protectgui then
        return game:GetService("CoreGui")
    end

    local cg = game:GetService("CoreGui")
    if cg then return cg end

    return PlayerGui
end

function safeParentGui(gui)
    pcall(function()
        if type(gethui) == "function" then
            local ok, parent = pcall(gethui)
            if ok and parent then
                gui.Parent = parent
                return
            end
        end
        if protectgui then
            gui.Parent = game:GetService("CoreGui")
            pcall(function() protectgui(gui) end)
            return
        end
        gui.Parent = game:GetService("CoreGui")
    end)

    if not gui.Parent then
        pcall(function() gui.Parent = PlayerGui end)
    end
end

function doAutoTPDown(force)
    local char = LP.Character
    if not char then return end
    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hrp then return end
    local hum2 = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not hum2 then return end
    if not force then
        if hum2.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air then return end
        if not (hrp.Position.Y >= (tonumber(autoTPHeight) or 20)) then return end
    end
    local yaw = select(2, hrp.CFrame:ToEulerAnglesYXZ())
    hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(hrp.Position.X, -7.00, hrp.Position.Z) * CFrame.Angles(0, yaw, 0)
    _G._akaiBypassSpoof = true
    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
    hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
    pcall(function() hrp.Velocity = Vector3.zero end)
    pcall(function() hrp.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero end)
end
local function _clearAutoTPConnection()
if autoTPConn then
pcall(function() autoTPConn:Disconnect() end)
pcall(function() task.cancel(autoTPConn) end)
autoTPConn = nil
end
end
local function startAutoTP()
autoTPEnabled = true
_clearAutoTPConnection()
autoTPLastRun = 0
autoTPConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not autoTPEnabled then
_clearAutoTPConnection()
return
end
local now = tick()
if now - autoTPLastRun < 0.1 then return end
autoTPLastRun = now
pcall(function() doAutoTPDown(false) end)
end)
if setAutoTPVisual then setAutoTPVisual(true) end
end
local function stopAutoTP()
autoTPEnabled = false
_clearAutoTPConnection()
if setAutoTPVisual then setAutoTPVisual(false) end
end
local function runTPFloor()
pcall(function() doAutoTPDown(true) end)
end
local function toggleAutoTP(on)
if on then
startAutoTP()
else
stopAutoTP()
end
do end
end
function _G.MeridianStopAutoTPForAction()
if autoTPEnabled then
stopAutoTP()
pcall(function() if setAutoTPVisual then setAutoTPVisual(false) end end)
do end
end
end
local dropBrainrotActive = false
local _adaptDropConns = {}

local _xDropLastInvoke = 0
local _xDropConn = nil
local function stopDropBrainrot()
    dropBrainrotActive = false
    if _xDropConn then
        pcall(function() _xDropConn:Disconnect() end)
        _xDropConn = nil
    end
    for _, t in ipairs(_adaptDropConns) do
        if type(t) == "thread" then pcall(task.cancel, t)
        elseif typeof(t) == "RBXScriptConnection" then pcall(function() t:Disconnect() end) end
    end
    _adaptDropConns = {}
    local c = LP.Character
    if c then
        local root = c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if root then
            _G._akaiBypassSpoof = true
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end
    end
end

_G.MeridianDropAscendDuration = _G.MeridianDropAscendDuration or 0.2
_G.MeridianDropAscendSpeed    = _G.MeridianDropAscendSpeed    or 150

-- Meridian Duels -- STAND DROP: adapted 1-to-1 from the reference
-- "Stand Drop" (brainrot fling) implementation. Disables collision on
-- other players' characters and violently flings the root part's
-- velocity for a very short burst, then restores it - separate
-- mechanism from Jump Drop (which ascends then raycasts back down).
-- Meridian Duels -- 1-to-1 with the original RitualHub source's
-- runStandDrop(): same disableOtherCollisions loop, same fling-thread
-- structure, and crucially the same root.Velocity property (not
-- AssemblyLinearVelocity) since that's what the original actually uses.
local function runStandDrop()
    if dropBrainrotActive then return end
    dropBrainrotActive = true
    local colConn = RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
        if not dropBrainrotActive then return end
        for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if plr ~= LP and plr.Character then
                for _, part in ipairs(plr.Character:GetChildren()) do
                    if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                        part.CanCollide = false
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
    table.insert(_adaptDropConns, colConn)
    local flingThread = coroutine.create(function()
        while dropBrainrotActive do
            RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
            local c = LP.Character
            local root = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not root then break end
            _G._akaiBypassSpoof = true
            local vel = root.Velocity
            _G._akaiBypassSpoof = true
            root.Velocity = vel * 10000 + Vector3.new(0, 10000, 0)
            RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
            if root and root.Parent then
                _G._akaiBypassSpoof = true
                root.Velocity = vel
            end
            RunService.Stepped:Wait()
            if root and root.Parent then
                _G._akaiBypassSpoof = true
                root.Velocity = vel + Vector3.new(0, 0.1, 0)
            end
        end
    end)
    table.insert(_adaptDropConns, flingThread)
    coroutine.resume(flingThread)
    task.delay(0.1, function()
        dropBrainrotActive = false
        for _, c in ipairs(_adaptDropConns) do
            if typeof(c) == "RBXScriptConnection" then
                c:Disconnect()
            elseif type(c) == "thread" then
                pcall(coroutine.close, c)
            end
        end
        _adaptDropConns = {}
    end)
end

-- Meridian Duels -- JUMP DROP (fast variant): ported from the 7up
-- reference's runJumpDrop(). Runs on RenderStepped instead of
-- Heartbeat so it wins the frame race against the walk/carry speed
-- mover (which runs on Heartbeat) instead of getting its upward
-- velocity overwritten mid-ascent. Also kills any LinearVelocity /
-- BodyVelocity / VectorForce on the root before bursting and keeps
-- re-disabling LinearVelocity every frame during ascent, same as the
-- reference. Names kept as Main's own (dropBrainrotActive, _xDropConn,
-- _G.MeridianDropAscendDuration/Speed) so runDrop()'s dispatcher and
-- every button/UI hookup elsewhere in the file needs no changes.
local function runJumpDrop()
    if dropBrainrotActive then return end
    if _G.MeridianStopAutoTPForAction then _G.MeridianStopAutoTPForAction() end
    local char = LP.Character
    if not char then return end
    local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not root then return end
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

    dropBrainrotActive = true
    local startTime = tick()

    -- Destroy _akaiLV entirely so RenderStepped speed engine
    -- can't fight the upward burst. Re-built after landing.
    if _akaiLV and _akaiLV.Parent then
        pcall(function() _akaiLV.PlaneVelocity = Vector2.zero end)
        pcall(function() _akaiLV:Destroy() end)
        _akaiLV = nil
    end
    if _akaiAtt and _akaiAtt.Parent then
        pcall(function() _akaiAtt:Destroy() end)
        _akaiAtt = nil
    end

    pcall(function()
        if hum then
            hum:Move(Vector3.zero, false)
            hum.AutoRotate = true
        end
        for _, inst in ipairs(root:GetChildren()) do
            if inst:IsA("LinearVelocity") or inst:IsA("BodyVelocity") or inst:IsA("VectorForce") then
                inst:Destroy()
            end
        end
    end)

    if _xDropConn then pcall(function() _xDropConn:Disconnect() end); _xDropConn = nil end
    _xDropConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        local currentChar = LP.Character
        local currentRoot = currentChar and currentChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not currentChar or not currentRoot then
            if _xDropConn then pcall(function() _xDropConn:Disconnect() end); _xDropConn = nil end
            dropBrainrotActive = false
            return
        end
        if not dropBrainrotActive then
            if _xDropConn then pcall(function() _xDropConn:Disconnect() end); _xDropConn = nil end
            return
        end

        pcall(function()
            for _, inst in ipairs(currentRoot:GetChildren()) do
                if inst:IsA("LinearVelocity") then
                    inst.Enabled = false
                    if inst.PlaneVelocity ~= nil then
                        inst.PlaneVelocity = Vector2.zero
                    end
                end
            end
        end)

        if tick() - startTime >= (_G.MeridianDropAscendDuration or 0.2) then
            if _xDropConn then pcall(function() _xDropConn:Disconnect() end); _xDropConn = nil end
            local rayParams = RaycastParams.new()
            rayParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = {currentChar}
            rayParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
            local rayResult = workspace:Raycast(currentRoot.Position, Vector3.new(0, -2000, 0), rayParams)
            if rayResult then
                local humNow = currentChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                local offset = (humNow and humNow.HipHeight or 2) + (currentRoot.Size.Y / 2)
                currentRoot.CFrame = CFrame.new(currentRoot.Position.X, rayResult.Position.Y + offset, currentRoot.Position.Z)
                _G._akaiBypassSpoof = true
                currentRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                currentRoot.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                if currentRoot.Velocity then currentRoot.Velocity = Vector3.zero end
            end
            dropBrainrotActive = false
            -- Re-build speed LV after landing
            task.defer(function()
                local c = LP.Character
                if c then pcall(setupLinearVelocity, c) end
            end)
            return
        end

        local up = tonumber(_G.MeridianDropAscendSpeed) or 150
        _G._akaiBypassSpoof = true
        currentRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, up, 0)
        currentRoot.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        if currentRoot.Velocity then
            currentRoot.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, up, 0)
        end
    end)
end

-- Meridian Duels -- dispatcher: routes to Stand or Jump drop based on
-- the Drop Mode selector (_G.MeridianDropMode). Previously this always
-- ran the Jump Drop body directly regardless of what the GUI selector
-- showed - the Stand option existed visually but never actually did
-- anything different.
function runDrop()
    if _G.MeridianDropMode == "Stand" then
        runStandDrop()
    else
        runJumpDrop()
    end
end

function runDropBrainrot()
runDrop()
end

local infJumpEnabled = false
local antiRagdollEnabled = false
local antiRagdollConn = nil
_G.MeridianAntiRagdollMode = "Zombie Hub"

_G.MeridianAntiVoidEnabled = _G.MeridianAntiVoidEnabled == true
_G.MeridianAntiVoidConnection = _G.MeridianAntiVoidConnection or nil
_G.MeridianAntiVoidSafeCFrame = _G.MeridianAntiVoidSafeCFrame or nil

function _G.MeridianAntiVoidSaveSafe()
    local char = LP.Character
    local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hum or not root or hum.Health <= 0 then return end
    if hum.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air then
        _G.MeridianAntiVoidSafeCFrame = root.CFrame
    end
end

function _G.MeridianAntiVoidStop()
    if _G.MeridianAntiVoidConnection then
        pcall(function() _G.MeridianAntiVoidConnection:Disconnect() end)
        _G.MeridianAntiVoidConnection = nil
    end
end

function _G.MeridianAntiVoidSet(on)
    _G.MeridianAntiVoidEnabled = on == true
    _G.MeridianAntiVoidStop()
    if not _G.MeridianAntiVoidEnabled then return end

    _G.MeridianAntiVoidSaveSafe()

    _G.MeridianAntiVoidConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        local char = LP.Character
        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not _G.MeridianAntiVoidEnabled or not hum or not root or hum.Health <= 0 then return end

        local voidY = Workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight + 30
        if hum.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air and root.Position.Y > voidY + 12 then
            _G.MeridianAntiVoidSafeCFrame = root.CFrame
        elseif root.Position.Y <= voidY and _G.MeridianAntiVoidSafeCFrame then
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            root.CFrame = _G.MeridianAntiVoidSafeCFrame + Vector3.new(0, 4, 0)
            hum.PlatformStand = false
            hum.Sit = false
            pcall(function() hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp) end)
        end
    end)
end

local AnimationPackList = {"OFF","Zombie","Ninja","Knight","Elder","Levitate","Astronaut","Pirate","Toy","Vampire","Werewolf","Rthro","Stylish","Adidas Aura","Adidas Community","Adidas Sports","Amazon Unboxed","Bubbly","Cartoon","Catwalk Glam","Levitation","Mage","NFL","No Boundaries","Robot","Superhero","Wicked Popular"}
local AnimationPackIndex = 1
local OriginalAnims = {}
local animationPackValueLabel = nil
local refreshAnimationPackRow = nil

_G.MeridianAnimationSystem = _G.MeridianAnimationSystem or {
    conn = nil,
    original = nil,
    activePack = "OFF",
}

function _G.MeridianAnimGetAnimate(char)
    char = char or LP.Character
    return char and char:FindFirstChild("Animate") or nil
end

function _G.MeridianAnimReadOriginal(char)
    if _G.MeridianAnimationSystem.original then return end
    local animate = _G.MeridianAnimGetAnimate(char)
    if not animate then return end

    local function g(obj)
        return obj and obj.AnimationId or nil
    end

    _G.MeridianAnimationSystem.original = {
        idle1 = g(animate.idle and animate.idle:FindFirstChild("Animation1")),
        idle2 = g(animate.idle and animate.idle:FindFirstChild("Animation2")),
        walk = g(animate.walk and animate.walk:FindFirstChild("WalkAnim")),
        run = g(animate.run and animate.run:FindFirstChild("RunAnim")),
        jump = g(animate.jump and animate.jump:FindFirstChild("JumpAnim")),
        fall = g(animate.fall and animate.fall:FindFirstChild("FallAnim")),
        climb = g(animate.climb and animate.climb:FindFirstChild("ClimbAnim")),
        swim = g(animate.swim and animate.swim:FindFirstChild("Swim")),
        swimidle = g(animate.swimidle and animate.swimidle:FindFirstChild("SwimIdle")),
    }
end

function _G.MeridianAnimGetPack(packName)
    return AnimationPacks[packName]
end

function _G.MeridianAnimApplyPack(char, packName)
    local animate = _G.MeridianAnimGetAnimate(char)
    local pack = _G.MeridianAnimGetPack(packName)
    if not animate or not pack then return false end

    local function s(obj, id)
        if obj and id then
            obj.AnimationId = tostring(id)
        end
    end

    s(animate.idle and animate.idle:FindFirstChild("Animation1"), pack.idle and pack.idle[1] and pack.idle[1][1])
    s(animate.idle and animate.idle:FindFirstChild("Animation2"), pack.idle and pack.idle[2] and pack.idle[2][1])
    s(animate.walk and animate.walk:FindFirstChild("WalkAnim"), pack.walk)
    s(animate.run and animate.run:FindFirstChild("RunAnim"), pack.run)
    s(animate.jump and animate.jump:FindFirstChild("JumpAnim"), pack.jump)
    s(animate.fall and animate.fall:FindFirstChild("FallAnim"), pack.fall)
    s(animate.climb and animate.climb:FindFirstChild("ClimbAnim"), pack.climb)
    s(animate.swim and animate.swim:FindFirstChild("Swim"), pack.swim)
    s(animate.swimidle and animate.swimidle:FindFirstChild("SwimIdle"), pack.swimidle)

    return true
end

function _G.MeridianAnimRestore(char)
    local animate = _G.MeridianAnimGetAnimate(char)
    local o = _G.MeridianAnimationSystem.original
    if not animate or not o then return end

    local function s(obj,id)
        if obj and id then obj.AnimationId = id end
    end

    s(animate.idle and animate.idle:FindFirstChild("Animation1"), o.idle1)
    s(animate.idle and animate.idle:FindFirstChild("Animation2"), o.idle2)
    s(animate.walk and animate.walk:FindFirstChild("WalkAnim"), o.walk)
    s(animate.run and animate.run:FindFirstChild("RunAnim"), o.run)
    s(animate.jump and animate.jump:FindFirstChild("JumpAnim"), o.jump)
    s(animate.fall and animate.fall:FindFirstChild("FallAnim"), o.fall)
    s(animate.climb and animate.climb:FindFirstChild("ClimbAnim"), o.climb)
    s(animate.swim and animate.swim:FindFirstChild("Swim"), o.swim)
    s(animate.swimidle and animate.swimidle:FindFirstChild("SwimIdle"), o.swimidle)
end

function _G.MeridianAnimStop()
    if _G.MeridianAnimationSystem.conn then
        pcall(function() _G.MeridianAnimationSystem.conn:Disconnect() end)
        _G.MeridianAnimationSystem.conn = nil
    end

    _G.MeridianAnimRestore(LP.Character)
    _G.MeridianAnimationSystem.activePack = "OFF"
end

function _G.MeridianAnimStart(packName)
    if packName == "OFF" then
        _G.MeridianAnimStop()
        return
    end

    if _G.MeridianAnimationSystem.conn then
        pcall(function() _G.MeridianAnimationSystem.conn:Disconnect() end)
        _G.MeridianAnimationSystem.conn = nil
    end

    local char = LP.Character
    if not char then return end

    local pack = _G.MeridianAnimGetPack(packName)
    if not pack then return end

    _G.MeridianAnimReadOriginal(char)
    _G.MeridianAnimationSystem.activePack = packName

    task.spawn(function()
        local list = {}
        for _, v in pairs(pack) do
            if type(v) == "string" then
                table.insert(list, v)
            elseif type(v) == "table" then
                for _, a in ipairs(v) do
                    if type(a) == "table" and a[1] then
                        table.insert(list, tostring(a[1]))
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        pcall(function()
            game:GetService("ContentProvider"):PreloadAsync(list)
        end)
    end)

    _G.MeridianAnimApplyPack(char, packName)

    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum then
        for _, track in ipairs(hum:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
            pcall(function() track:Stop(0) end)
        end
        pcall(function()
            hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
        end)
    end

    _G.MeridianAnimationSystem.conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if selectedAnimationPack == "OFF" then return end
        local c = LP.Character
        if c then
            _G.MeridianAnimApplyPack(c, selectedAnimationPack)
        end
    end)
end

function syncAnimationPackIndex()
    local found = 1
    for i, name in ipairs(AnimationPackList) do
        if name == selectedAnimationPack then
            found = i
            break
        end
    end
    AnimationPackIndex = found
end

function applyAnimationPack(packName)
    selectedAnimationPack = packName or "OFF"
    syncAnimationPackIndex()

    if selectedAnimationPack == "OFF" then
        _G.MeridianAnimStop()
    else
        _G.MeridianAnimStart(selectedAnimationPack)
    end

    if refreshAnimationPackRow then
        pcall(refreshAnimationPackRow)
    end
end

enableUnwalk = function()
    selectedAnimationPack = "OFF"
    _G.MeridianAnimStop()
end

disableUnwalk = function()
end

enableHitHarderAnim = function()
    applyAnimationPack("Zombie")
end

disableHitHarderAnim = function()
    applyAnimationPack("OFF")
end

resetAnimations = function()
    applyAnimationPack("OFF")
end

stopCurrentAnimations = function(char)
    local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not hum then return end
    for _, track in ipairs(hum:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
        pcall(function() track:Stop(0) end)
    end
end

getAnimate = function(char)
    return _G.MeridianAnimGetAnimate(char)
end

setAnimId = function(obj,id)
    if obj and id then obj.AnimationId = tostring(id) end
end

reloadAnimate = function(animate)
    if not animate then return end
    pcall(function()
        animate.Disabled = true
        task.wait()
        animate.Disabled = false
    end)
end

backupAnimations = function(char)
    _G.MeridianAnimReadOriginal(char)
end

LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
    task.wait(0.5)
    _G.MeridianAnimationSystem.original = nil
    if selectedAnimationPack and selectedAnimationPack ~= "OFF" then
        _G.MeridianAnimStart(selectedAnimationPack)
    end
end)

-- ── VYNX SPLATTER RAGDOLL (1:1) ─────────────────────────────────
-- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- ANTI-RAGDOLL — 7UP Duels dual-mode engine (V1 task-loop + V2 Heartbeat)
-- V1: arHeartbeatLoop task + boost-exit + BallSocketConstraint destroy
-- V2: Heartbeat full constraint/Motor6D/velocity reset (arV2ResetCharacter)
-- Controlled by _G.MeridianAntiRagdollMode ("V1" | "V2")
-- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
local _arMode          = nil   -- "v1" | "v2" | nil
local _arConn          = nil   -- Heartbeat conn (V2 only)
local _arRagdollConns  = {}    -- RenderStepped + CharacterAdded (V1 only)
local _arCached        = {}    -- { character, humanoid, root }
local _arIsBoosting    = false
local AR_BOOST_SPEED   = 400
local AR_DEFAULT_SPEED = 16

local function _arV2ResetCharacter(char)
    local hum  = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hum or not root or hum.Health <= 0 then return end
    pcall(function()
        hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
        hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
        root.Velocity              = Vector3.zero
        root.RotVelocity           = Vector3.zero
        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity  = Vector3.zero
        root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        hum.PlatformStand = false
        hum.Sit           = false
        hum.AutoRotate    = true
        hum.JumpPower  = hum.JumpPower  > 0 and hum.JumpPower  or 50
        hum.WalkSpeed  = hum.WalkSpeed  > 0 and hum.WalkSpeed  or AR_DEFAULT_SPEED
        for _, obj in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
            if obj:IsA("Motor6D") then
                obj.Enabled = true
            elseif obj:IsA("BallSocketConstraint") or obj:IsA("HingeConstraint") then
                obj.Enabled = true
            elseif obj:IsA("BasePart") then
                obj.CanCollide               = true
                obj.AssemblyLinearVelocity   = Vector3.zero
                obj.AssemblyAngularVelocity  = Vector3.zero
            end
        end
        if workspace.CurrentCamera then workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = hum end
    end)
end

local function _arCacheChar()
    local char = LP.Character
    if not char then return false end
    local hum  = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hum or not root then return false end
    _arCached = { character = char, humanoid = hum, root = root }
    return true
end

local function _arDisconnectAll()
    for _, c in ipairs(_arRagdollConns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
    _arRagdollConns = {}
end

local function _arIsRagdolled()
    if not _arCached.humanoid then return false end
    local st = _arCached.humanoid:GetState()
    if st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
    or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
    or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown then return true end
    local endTime = LP:GetAttribute("RagdollEndTime")
    if endTime and (endTime - workspace:GetServerTimeNow()) > 0 then return true end
    return false
end

local function _arForceExitRagdoll()
    if not _arCached.humanoid or not _arCached.root then return end
    pcall(function() LP:SetAttribute("RagdollEndTime", workspace:GetServerTimeNow()) end)
    for _, d in ipairs(_arCached.character:GetDescendants()) do
        if d:IsA("BallSocketConstraint")
        or (d:IsA("Attachment") and d.Name:find("RagdollAttachment")) then
            d:Destroy()
        end
    end
    if not _arIsBoosting then
        _arIsBoosting = true
        _arCached.humanoid.WalkSpeed = AR_BOOST_SPEED
    end
    if _arCached.humanoid.Health > 0 then
        _arCached.humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
    end
    _arCached.root.Anchored = false
end

local function _arV1Loop()
    while _arMode == "v1" do
        task.wait()
        local ragdolled = _arIsRagdolled()
        if ragdolled then
            _arForceExitRagdoll()
        elseif _arIsBoosting and not ragdolled then
            _arIsBoosting = false
            if _arCached.humanoid then
                _arCached.humanoid.WalkSpeed = AR_DEFAULT_SPEED
            end
        end
    end
end

local function startAntiRagdoll()
    local requestedMode = (_G.MeridianAntiRagdollMode == "V2") and "v2" or "v1"
    if _arMode == requestedMode then return end

    -- Teardown previous
    _arMode = nil
    if _arConn then _arConn:Disconnect(); _arConn = nil end
    if _arIsBoosting and _arCached.humanoid then
        _arCached.humanoid.WalkSpeed = AR_DEFAULT_SPEED
    end
    _arIsBoosting = false
    _arDisconnectAll()
    _arCached = {}

    antiRagdollEnabled = true
    if not _arCacheChar() then return end
    _arMode = requestedMode

    if requestedMode == "v2" then
        _arConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if _arMode ~= "v2" or not antiRagdollEnabled then return end
            local char = LP.Character
            local hum  = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if not hum then return end
            local st = hum:GetState()
            if st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
            or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
            or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown
            or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead
            or hum.PlatformStand == true
            or hum.Sit == true then
                _arV2ResetCharacter(char)
            end
        end)
        return
    end

    -- V1: RenderStepped cam lock + CharacterAdded respawn
    local camConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if cam and _arCached.humanoid then
            cam.CameraSubject = _arCached.humanoid
        end
    end)
    table.insert(_arRagdollConns, camConn)

    local respawnConn = LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
        _arIsBoosting = false
        task.wait(0.5)
        _arCacheChar()
    end)
    table.insert(_arRagdollConns, respawnConn)

    task.spawn(_arV1Loop)
end

local function stopAntiRagdoll()
    _arMode = nil
    if _arConn then _arConn:Disconnect(); _arConn = nil end
    if _arIsBoosting and _arCached.humanoid then
        _arCached.humanoid.WalkSpeed = AR_DEFAULT_SPEED
    end
    _arIsBoosting    = false
    antiRagdollEnabled = false
    _arDisconnectAll()
    _arCached = {}
end

local function setAntiRagdoll(on)
    if on then
        startAntiRagdoll()
    else
        stopAntiRagdoll()
    end
end

local _xIJBoosting   = false
local _xIJLastBoost  = 0
local _xIJForce      = 55
local _xIJFrames     = 2
local _xIJCooldown   = 0.12
local _xInfJumpState = {holdPressed=false, holdActive=false, controllerActive=false, mobilePressed=false, mobileActive=false, hooked={}}

local function _xApplyInfJumpBoost(root)
    if not infJumpEnabled then return end
    if not root or _xIJBoosting then return end
    local now = tick()
    if now - _xIJLastBoost < _xIJCooldown then return end
    _xIJLastBoost  = now
    _xIJBoosting   = true

    -- bypass spoof so Y write actually reaches the physics engine
    _G._akaiBypassSpoof = true
    local cur = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity  -- reads real value via bypass
    _G._akaiBypassSpoof = true
    root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(cur.X, _xIJForce, cur.Z)
    local frames = 0
    local conn
    conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if frames < _xIJFrames then
            frames = frames + 1
            _G._akaiBypassSpoof = true
            local cv = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
            _G._akaiBypassSpoof = true
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(cv.X, _xIJForce + frames * 0.01, cv.Z)
        else
            if conn then conn:Disconnect() end
            _xIJBoosting = false
        end
    end)
end

UserInputService.JumpRequest:Connect(function()
    if infJumpEnabled then
        local char = LP.Character
        local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if root then _xApplyInfJumpBoost(root) end
    end
end)

UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(inp, gpe)
    if gpe then return end
    if UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then return end
    if inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard and inp.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Space then
        _xInfJumpState.holdPressed = true
        task.delay(0.12, function()
            if _xInfJumpState.holdPressed and infJumpEnabled then
                _xInfJumpState.holdActive = true
            end
        end)
    elseif inp.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.ButtonA and inp.UserInputType.Name:match("^Gamepad") then
        _xInfJumpState.controllerActive = true
    end
end)

UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(inp)
    if inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard and inp.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Space then
        _xInfJumpState.holdPressed = false
        _xInfJumpState.holdActive = false
    end
    if inp.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.ButtonA and inp.UserInputType.Name:match("^Gamepad") then
        _xInfJumpState.controllerActive = false
    end
end)

local function _xHookInfJumpMobileButton(obj)
    if not obj or obj.Name ~= "JumpButton" or not obj:IsA("GuiButton") or _xInfJumpState.hooked[obj] then return end
    _xInfJumpState.hooked[obj] = true
    obj.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch or not infJumpEnabled then return end
        _xInfJumpState.mobilePressed = true
        task.delay(0.12, function()
            if _xInfJumpState.mobilePressed and infJumpEnabled then
                _xInfJumpState.mobileActive = true
            end
        end)
    end)
    obj.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            _xInfJumpState.mobilePressed = false
            _xInfJumpState.mobileActive = false
        end
    end)
    obj.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_, parent)
        if not parent then
            _xInfJumpState.hooked[obj] = nil
            _xInfJumpState.mobilePressed = false
            _xInfJumpState.mobileActive = false
        end
    end)
end

task.spawn(function()
    local pg = LP:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 10)
    if pg then
        for _, d in ipairs(pg:GetDescendants()) do _xHookInfJumpMobileButton(d) end
        pg.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj) task.defer(_xHookInfJumpMobileButton, obj) end)
    end
end)

local _xHoldIJConn = nil
local function _xStartHoldIJ()
    if _xHoldIJConn then _xHoldIJConn:Disconnect() end
    _xHoldIJConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not infJumpEnabled then return end
        local S = _xInfJumpState
        if not (S.holdActive or S.mobileActive or S.controllerActive) then return end
        local char = LP.Character
        local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        _G._akaiBypassSpoof = true
        local realY = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y
        if realY < 30 then
            _xApplyInfJumpBoost(root)
        end
    end)
end
_xStartHoldIJ()

setInfJumpInternal = function(on)
    infJumpEnabled = on and true or false
    if not infJumpEnabled then
        _xInfJumpState.holdPressed = false
        _xInfJumpState.holdActive = false
        _xInfJumpState.controllerActive = false
        _xInfJumpState.mobilePressed = false
        _xInfJumpState.mobileActive = false
        _xIJBoosting = false
    end
end

_G.MeridianAntiResetEnabled = true

function _G.MeridianApplyAntiReset()
    pcall(function()
        StarterGui:SetCore("ResetButtonCallback", false)
    end)
end

function _G.MeridianRestoreReset()

end

function _G.MeridianSetAntiReset(on)

    _G.MeridianAntiResetEnabled = true
    task.spawn(function()
        for _ = 1, 12 do
            _G.MeridianApplyAntiReset()
            task.wait(0.25)
        end
    end)
end

_G.MeridianSetAntiReset(true)

_G.MeridianAntiDieHealthConn = _G.MeridianAntiDieHealthConn or nil
_G.MeridianAntiDieCharConn = _G.MeridianAntiDieCharConn or nil
_G.MeridianAntiDieMotorConn = _G.MeridianAntiDieMotorConn or nil

-- Ported from source: re-enables any disabled Motor6D joints and corrects
-- the Humanoid out of Ragdoll/FallingDown every frame, so a fling/ragdoll
-- can't actually take hold. Runs globally (always-on, not tied to a
-- specific toggle), same as the health-restore hooks below.
-- Insta Reset override: while _G.MeridianResetCooldown is true (a reset is
-- actively in progress), this loop -- and the health-restore hooks below --
-- skip entirely, since Insta Reset intentionally drops Health to 0 and
-- changes Humanoid state as its own reset mechanism; Anti-Die would
-- otherwise immediately fight that and break the reset.
function _G.MeridianStartAntiDieMotorFix()
    if _G.MeridianAntiDieMotorConn then return end
    _G.MeridianAntiDieMotorConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if _G.MeridianResetCooldown then return end
        local char = LP.Character
        if not char then return end
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not hum then return end

        for _, obj in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
            if obj:IsA("Motor6D") and not obj.Enabled then
                obj.Enabled = true
            end
        end

        local st = hum:GetState()
        if st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown then
            hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
        end
    end)
end

function _G.MeridianStopAntiDieMotorFix()
    if _G.MeridianAntiDieMotorConn then
        pcall(function() _G.MeridianAntiDieMotorConn:Disconnect() end)
        _G.MeridianAntiDieMotorConn = nil
    end
end

function _G.MeridianAntiDieWatch(char)
    if _G.MeridianAntiDieHealthConn then
        pcall(function() _G.MeridianAntiDieHealthConn:Disconnect() end)
        _G.MeridianAntiDieHealthConn = nil
    end

    local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not hum then return end

    -- Disable the Dead/Ragdoll/FallingDown states outright, instead of
    -- only restoring health after the fact. Restoring health alone still
    -- lets the humanoid briefly enter those states first, which is what
    -- was triggering a "reset" (dropping carried items, canceling steal,
    -- etc.) even though health bounced back a moment later.
    pcall(function() hum.BreakJointsOnDeath = false end)
    pcall(function() hum.RequiresNeck = false end)
    pcall(function() hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, false) end)
    pcall(function() hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll, false) end)
    pcall(function() hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown, false) end)
    pcall(function() if hum.MaxHealth <= 0 or hum.MaxHealth ~= hum.MaxHealth then hum.MaxHealth = 100 end end)

    _G.MeridianAntiDieHealthConn = hum:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Health"):Connect(function()
        if _G.MeridianResetCooldown then return end
        if hum.Health < hum.MaxHealth then
            pcall(function()
                hum.Health = hum.MaxHealth
            end)
        end
    end)

    if _G.MeridianAntiDieDiedConn then
        pcall(function() _G.MeridianAntiDieDiedConn:Disconnect() end)
        _G.MeridianAntiDieDiedConn = nil
    end
    _G.MeridianAntiDieDiedConn = hum.Died:Connect(function()
        if _G.MeridianResetCooldown then return end
        task.wait()
        pcall(function()
            hum.Health = hum.MaxHealth
        end)
    end)

    if _G.MeridianAntiDieStateConn then
        pcall(function() _G.MeridianAntiDieStateConn:Disconnect() end)
        _G.MeridianAntiDieStateConn = nil
    end
    _G.MeridianAntiDieStateConn = hum.StateChanged:Connect(function(_, new)
        if _G.MeridianResetCooldown then return end
        if new == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead or new == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll then
            pcall(function() hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp) end)
            if hum.Health < hum.MaxHealth then
                pcall(function() hum.Health = hum.MaxHealth end)
            end
        end
    end)

    -- Guard against vital parts (Head/HumanoidRootPart/Humanoid) being
    -- removed outright (void/reset TP death), re-applying the state
    -- protections the moment a new Humanoid shows up.
    if _G.MeridianAntiDieChildRemovedConn then
        pcall(function() _G.MeridianAntiDieChildRemovedConn:Disconnect() end)
        _G.MeridianAntiDieChildRemovedConn = nil
    end
    _G.MeridianAntiDieChildRemovedConn = char.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(obj)
        if _G.MeridianResetCooldown then return end
        if obj and (obj.Name == "Head" or obj.Name == "HumanoidRootPart" or obj.Name == "Humanoid") then
            task.defer(function()
                local h = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if h then
                    pcall(function() h:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, false) end)
                    pcall(function() h:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll, false) end)
                    pcall(function() h:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown, false) end)
                    if h.Health < h.MaxHealth then h.Health = h.MaxHealth end
                end
            end)
        end
    end)

    _G.MeridianStartAntiDieMotorFix()
end

if _G.MeridianAntiDieCharConn then
    pcall(function() _G.MeridianAntiDieCharConn:Disconnect() end)
end
_G.MeridianAntiDieCharConn = LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
    task.wait(0.2)
    _G.MeridianAntiDieWatch(char)
    _G.MeridianSetAntiReset(true)
end)

if LP.Character then
    task.defer(function()
        _G.MeridianAntiDieWatch(LP.Character)
    end)
end

_G.MeridianHeadlessEnabled = _G.MeridianHeadlessEnabled == true
_G.MeridianKorbloxEnabled = _G.MeridianKorbloxEnabled == true
_G.MeridianHeadlessMeshId = "rbxassetid://1095708"
_G.MeridianKorbloxMeshId = "rbxassetid://101851696"
_G.MeridianKorbloxTextureId = "rbxassetid://101851254"

function _G.MeridianApplyHeadless(char, enabled)
    if not char then return end
    local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
    if not head then return end

    if enabled then
        head.Transparency = 1
        head.CanCollide = false
        local face = head:FindFirstChild("face")
        if face then face:Destroy() end

        local old = head:FindFirstChild("MeridianHeadlessMesh")
        if old then old:Destroy() end

        local mesh = Instance.new("SpecialMesh")
        mesh.Name = "MeridianHeadlessMesh"
        mesh.MeshType = Enum.MeshType.FileMesh
        mesh.MeshId = _G.MeridianHeadlessMeshId
        mesh.Scale = Vector3.new(0.001, 0.001, 0.001)
        mesh.Parent = head
    else
        head.Transparency = 0
        head.CanCollide = true
        local old = head:FindFirstChild("MeridianHeadlessMesh")
        if old then old:Destroy() end
    end
end

function _G.MeridianApplyKorblox(char, enabled)
    if not char then return end
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not hum then return end

    if hum.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R6 then
        local leg = char:FindFirstChild("Right Leg")
        if not leg then return end

        local old = leg:FindFirstChild("MeridianKorbloxMesh")
        if old then old:Destroy() end

        if enabled then
            leg.Color = Color3.fromRGB(64,64,64)
            local mesh = Instance.new("SpecialMesh")
            mesh.Name = "MeridianKorbloxMesh"
            mesh.MeshType = Enum.MeshType.FileMesh
            mesh.MeshId = _G.MeridianKorbloxMeshId
            mesh.TextureId = _G.MeridianKorbloxTextureId
            mesh.Scale = Vector3.new(1,1,1)
            mesh.Parent = leg
        else
            leg.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
        end
    else
        local upper = char:FindFirstChild("RightUpperLeg")
        local lower = char:FindFirstChild("RightLowerLeg")
        local foot = char:FindFirstChild("RightFoot")
        if not upper then return end

        local oldLeg = char:FindFirstChild("MeridianKorbloxLeg")
        if oldLeg then oldLeg:Destroy() end

        if enabled then
            upper.Transparency = 1
            if lower then lower.Transparency = 1 end
            if foot then foot.Transparency = 1 end

            local kleg = Instance.new("Part")
            kleg.Name = "MeridianKorbloxLeg"
            kleg.Size = Vector3.new(1,2,1)
            kleg.Anchored = false
            kleg.CanCollide = false
            kleg.Massless = true
            kleg.Color = Color3.fromRGB(64,64,64)
            kleg.Parent = char

            local mesh = Instance.new("SpecialMesh")
            mesh.Name = "MeridianKorbloxMesh"
            mesh.MeshType = Enum.MeshType.FileMesh
            mesh.MeshId = _G.MeridianKorbloxMeshId
            mesh.TextureId = _G.MeridianKorbloxTextureId
            mesh.Scale = Vector3.new(1,1,1)
            mesh.Parent = kleg

            local weld = Instance.new("Weld")
            weld.Name = "MeridianKorbloxWeld"
            weld.Part0 = upper
            weld.Part1 = kleg
            weld.C0 = CFrame.new(0,-0.8,0)
            weld.Parent = kleg
        else
            upper.Transparency = 0
            if lower then lower.Transparency = 0 end
            if foot then foot.Transparency = 0 end
        end
    end
end

function _G.MeridianApplyCharacterVisuals(char)
    char = char or LP.Character
    if not char then return end
    pcall(function() _G.MeridianApplyHeadless(char, _G.MeridianHeadlessEnabled) end)
    pcall(function() _G.MeridianApplyKorblox(char, _G.MeridianKorbloxEnabled) end)
end

_G.MeridianBodyLockEnabled = _G.MeridianBodyLockEnabled == true
_G.MeridianBodyLockRadius = tonumber(_G.MeridianBodyLockRadius) or 60
_G.MeridianBodyLockConn = _G.MeridianBodyLockConn or nil

function _G.MeridianGetNearestBodyLockTarget()
local character = LP.Character
local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if not root then return nil end

local nearest = nil
local shortest = math.huge

for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
if plr ~= LP and plr.Character then
local tr = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
local hum = plr.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if tr and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
local d = (tr.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
if d <= (_G.MeridianBodyLockRadius or 60) and d < shortest then
shortest = d
nearest = plr
end
end
end
end

return nearest
end

function _G.MeridianStartBodyLock()
if _G.MeridianBodyLockConn then return end
_G.MeridianBodyLockEnabled = true

_G.MeridianBodyLockConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not _G.MeridianBodyLockEnabled then return end

local character = LP.Character
local myRoot = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if not myRoot or not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 then return end

local target = _G.MeridianGetNearestBodyLockTarget()
if target and target.Character and target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
local targetPos = target.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position
local myPos = myRoot.Position
local offset = Vector3.new(targetPos.X, myPos.Y, targetPos.Z) - myPos

if offset.Magnitude > 0.1 then
humanoid.AutoRotate = false
local lookDir = offset.Unit
local currentDir = myRoot.CFrame.LookVector
local cross = currentDir:Cross(lookDir)
local currentVel = myRoot.AssemblyAngularVelocity
myRoot.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new(currentVel.X, cross.Y * 40, currentVel.Z)
end
else
humanoid.AutoRotate = true
end
end)
end

function _G.MeridianStopBodyLock()
_G.MeridianBodyLockEnabled = false

if _G.MeridianBodyLockConn then
pcall(function() _G.MeridianBodyLockConn:Disconnect() end)
_G.MeridianBodyLockConn = nil
end

local hum = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if hum then
hum.AutoRotate = true
end
end

function _G.MeridianSetBodyLock(on)
_G.MeridianBodyLockEnabled = on == true
if _G.MeridianBodyLockEnabled then
_G.MeridianStartBodyLock()
else
_G.MeridianStopBodyLock()
end

if _G.MeridianBodyLockVisual then
pcall(function() _G.MeridianBodyLockVisual(_G.MeridianBodyLockEnabled) end)
end
end

LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
if _G.MeridianBodyLockEnabled then
task.wait(0.3)
_G.MeridianStopBodyLock()
_G.MeridianStartBodyLock()
end
end)

local ok, result = pcall(function() return readfile(path) end)
return ok and result ~= nil
end
_ace_readfile = readfile or (syn and syn.readfile)
_ace_writefile = writefile or (syn and syn.writefile)
canSaveConfig = (type(_ace_readfile) == "function" and type(_ace_writefile) == "function")

end

_G.MeridianMobileButtonShape = _G.MeridianMobileButtonShape or "ROUNDED"

savedMiniPositionTable = nil
savedProgressBarPositionTable = nil
function udim2ToTable(u)
return {xs = u.X.Scale, xo = u.X.Offset, ys = u.Y.Scale, yo = u.Y.Offset}
end
function tableToUDim2(t, fallback)
if type(t) == "table" then
return UDim2.new(tonumber(t.xs) or 0, tonumber(t.xo) or 0, tonumber(t.ys) or 0, tonumber(t.yo) or 0)
end
return fallback
end
function collectMeridianMobileButtonPositions()
local out = {}
for key, entry in pairs(_G.MeridianMobileButtonRefs or {}) do
local holder = entry and entry.holder
if holder then out[key] = udim2ToTable(holder.Position) end
end
if next(out) == nil and type(_G.MeridianMobileButtonPositions) == "table" then
return _G.MeridianMobileButtonPositions
end
_G.MeridianMobileButtonPositions = out
return out
end
function keyToString(key)
if not key then return "None" end
return tostring(key):gsub("Enum.KeyCode.", "")
end
function stringToKeyCode(value)
if type(value) ~= "string" or value == "" or value == "None" then return nil end
return Enum.KeyCode[value]
end
function keybindsToTable()
local out = {}
for keyId in pairs(DEFAULT_SPEED_KEYBINDS) do
out[keyId] = keyToString(speedKeybinds[keyId])
end
for keyId, key in pairs(speedKeybinds) do
out[keyId] = keyToString(key)
end
return out
end
function controllerBindsToTable()
local out = {}
if _G.MeridianControllerBinds then
for keyId, key in pairs(_G.MeridianControllerBinds) do
out[keyId] = keyToString(key)
end
end
return out
end
function collectMeridianKeybindConfig()
return {
keybinds = keybindsToTable(),
tpDownKeybind = keyToString(tpDownKeybind),
controllerBinds = controllerBindsToTable(),
}
end
function applySavedKeybinds(t)
if type(t) ~= "table" then return end
for keyId in pairs(speedKeybinds) do
if t[keyId] ~= nil then
speedKeybinds[keyId] = stringToKeyCode(t[keyId])
end
end
end
function applySavedControllerBinds(t)
if type(t) ~= "table" then return end
_G.MeridianControllerBinds = _G.MeridianControllerBinds or {}
for keyId, value in pairs(t) do
_G.MeridianControllerBinds[keyId] = stringToKeyCode(value)
end
end
function applyDefaultMeridianKeybinds()
for keyId, key in pairs(DEFAULT_SPEED_KEYBINDS) do
speedKeybinds[keyId] = key
end
tpDownKeybind = DEFAULT_TP_DOWN_KEYBIND
end

function saveMeridianConfig()
if not canSaveConfig then return end
pcall(function()
_ace_writefile(CONFIG_FILE, HttpService:JSONEncode(collectMeridianConfig()))
_ace_writefile(KEYBINDS_CONFIG_FILE, HttpService:JSONEncode(collectMeridianKeybindConfig()))
end)
end
function loadMeridianConfig()
_G.MeridianManualSaveConfig = saveMeridianConfig

if _G.MeridianGuiScaleValue then
pcall(function()
if adaptGuiScaleValue ~= nil then adaptGuiScaleValue = tonumber(_G.MeridianGuiScaleValue) or adaptGuiScaleValue end
if aceGuiScaleValue ~= nil then aceGuiScaleValue = tonumber(_G.MeridianGuiScaleValue) or aceGuiScaleValue end
end)
end
if _G.MeridianProgressBarScaleValue then
pcall(function()
if adaptProgressBarScaleValue ~= nil then adaptProgressBarScaleValue = tonumber(_G.MeridianProgressBarScaleValue) or adaptProgressBarScaleValue end
if aceProgressBarScaleValue ~= nil then aceProgressBarScaleValue = tonumber(_G.MeridianProgressBarScaleValue) or aceProgressBarScaleValue end
end)
end
if not canSaveConfig or not _ace_isfile(CONFIG_FILE) then return end
local ok, data = pcall(function()
return HttpService:JSONDecode(_ace_readfile(CONFIG_FILE))
end)
if not ok or type(data) ~= "table" then return end
savedConfig = data
local keybindData = data
pcall(function()
if _ace_isfile(KEYBINDS_CONFIG_FILE) then
local kb = HttpService:JSONDecode(_ace_readfile(KEYBINDS_CONFIG_FILE))
if type(kb) == "table" then keybindData = kb end
end
end)
savedMainPositionTable = data.mainPosition
savedMiniPositionTable = data.miniPosition
savedProgressBarPositionTable = data.progressBarPosition
_G.MeridianGuiLocked = data.guiLocked == true
if data.hideMobileButtons ~= nil then
_G.MeridianHideMobileButtons = data.hideMobileButtons == true

local function syncAnimationPackIndex()
for i, name in ipairs(AnimationPackList) do
if name == selectedAnimationPack then
AnimationPackIndex = i
return
end
end
selectedAnimationPack = "OFF"
AnimationPackIndex = 1
end
local function applySavedAnimationPackToCharacter(char)
syncAnimationPackIndex()
if refreshAnimationPackRow then pcall(refreshAnimationPackRow) end
if not char then char = LP.Character end
if not char then return end
local animate = char:FindFirstChild("Animate") or char:WaitForChild("Animate", 6)
if not animate then return end
task.wait(0.2)
OriginalAnims = {}
unwalkSavedAnimate = nil
if selectedAnimationPack and selectedAnimationPack ~= "OFF" then
    pcall(function() applyAnimationPack(selectedAnimationPack) end)
elseif _G.MeridianSelectedMoreAnimationBundle and applyFullAnimationBundle then

    task.wait(0.15)
    pcall(function()
        applyFullAnimationBundle(_G.MeridianSelectedMoreAnimationBundle)
    end)
else
    pcall(function() resetAnimations() end)
end
end
syncAnimationPackIndex()
task.defer(function()
applySavedAnimationPackToCharacter(LP.Character)
end)
LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
task.wait(0.65)
task.defer(function()
task.wait(0.2)
_G.MeridianApplyCharacterVisuals(char)
end)
if _G.MeridianAntiVoidEnabled then pcall(_G.MeridianAntiVoidSaveSafe) end
applySavedAnimationPackToCharacter(char)

if _G.MeridianSelectedMoreAnimationBundle then
    task.delay(1.25, function()
        if char == LP.Character and char.Parent and applyFullAnimationBundle then
            pcall(function()
                applyFullAnimationBundle(_G.MeridianSelectedMoreAnimationBundle)
            end)
        end
    end)
end
end)
_G.MeridianAutoResetOnMed = _G.MeridianAutoResetOnMed or {}
_G.MeridianAutoResetOnMed.conns = _G.MeridianAutoResetOnMed.conns or {}
_G.MeridianAutoResetOnMed.enabled = autoResetOnMedEnabled == true
_G.MeridianAutoResetOnMed.medTriggered = false
_G.MeridianAutoResetOnMed.lastFire = _G.MeridianAutoResetOnMed.lastFire or 0
_G.MeridianAutoResetOnMed.cooldown = 2.25
_G.MeridianAutoResetOnMed.charAddedConn = _G.MeridianAutoResetOnMed.charAddedConn

function _G.MeridianAutoResetShouldFire(part)
local state = _G.MeridianAutoResetOnMed
if not state or not state.enabled then return false end
if state.medTriggered then return false end
if tick() - (state.lastFire or 0) < (state.cooldown or 2.25) then return false end
if not part or not part.Parent then return false end
if part:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Tool") or part:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Accessory") then
return false
end
return part.Anchored and part.Transparency == 1
end
function _G.MeridianAutoResetFireOnce(part)
if not _G.MeridianAutoResetShouldFire(part) then return end
local state = _G.MeridianAutoResetOnMed
state.medTriggered = true
state.lastFire = tick()
task.delay(2.3, function()
if state.enabled then
end
end)
end
function _G.MeridianAutoResetOnAnchorChanged(part)
return part:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Anchored"):Connect(function()
_G.MeridianAutoResetFireOnce(part)
end)
end
function _G.MeridianStopAutoResetOnMed()
local state = _G.MeridianAutoResetOnMed
if not state then return end
for _, conn in ipairs(state.conns or {}) do
pcall(function()
conn:Disconnect()
end)
end
state.conns = {}
state.medTriggered = false
end
function _G.MeridianStartAutoResetOnMed(char)
local state = _G.MeridianAutoResetOnMed
if not state then return end
_G.MeridianStopAutoResetOnMed()
state.medTriggered = false
char = char or LP.Character
if not char then return end
for _, part in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
if part:IsA("BasePart") then
table.insert(state.conns, _G.MeridianAutoResetOnAnchorChanged(part))
_G.MeridianAutoResetFireOnce(part)
end
end
table.insert(state.conns, char.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(part)
if part:IsA("BasePart") then
table.insert(state.conns, _G.MeridianAutoResetOnAnchorChanged(part))
_G.MeridianAutoResetFireOnce(part)
end
end))
table.insert(state.conns, char.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_, parent)
if not parent then
state.medTriggered = false
end
end))
end
function _G.MeridianEnableAutoResetOnMed()
autoResetOnMedEnabled = true
_G.MeridianAutoResetOnMed.enabled = true
_G.MeridianStartAutoResetOnMed(LP.Character)
end
function _G.MeridianDisableAutoResetOnMed()
autoResetOnMedEnabled = false
_G.MeridianAutoResetOnMed.enabled = false
_G.MeridianStopAutoResetOnMed()
end
function _G.MeridianSetAutoResetOnMed(state, noSave)
autoResetOnMedEnabled = state == true
if autoResetOnMedEnabled then
_G.MeridianEnableAutoResetOnMed()
else
_G.MeridianDisableAutoResetOnMed()
end
if setAutoResetOnMedVisual then
setAutoResetOnMedVisual(autoResetOnMedEnabled)
end
if not noSave and saveMeridianConfig then do end end
end
function enableAutoResetOnMed()
_G.MeridianSetAutoResetOnMed(true)
end
function disableAutoResetOnMed()
_G.MeridianSetAutoResetOnMed(false)
end
function toggleAutoResetOnMed(on)
_G.MeridianSetAutoResetOnMed(on == true)
end
if not _G.MeridianAutoResetOnMed.charAddedConn then
_G.MeridianAutoResetOnMed.charAddedConn = LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
if _G.MeridianAutoResetOnMed and _G.MeridianAutoResetOnMed.enabled then
task.wait(0.25)
_G.MeridianStartAutoResetOnMed(char)
end
end)
end
_G.MeridianCounterState = _G.MeridianCounterState or {}
_G.MeridianCounterState.batConn = nil
_G.MeridianCounterState.batDebounce = false
_G.MeridianCounterState.medConns = _G.MeridianCounterState.medConns or {}
_G.MeridianCounterState.medDebounce = false
_G.MeridianCounterState.medLastUsed = _G.MeridianCounterState.medLastUsed or 0
_G.MeridianMedusaCooldown = 25
function _G.MeridianFindMedusa()
local c = LP.Character
if not c then return nil end
for _, t in ipairs(c:GetChildren()) do
if t:IsA("Tool") then
local n = t.Name:lower()
if n:find("medusa") or n:find("head") or n:find("stone") then return t end
end
end
local bp = LP:FindFirstChild("Backpack") or LP:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
if bp then
for _, t in ipairs(bp:GetChildren()) do
if t:IsA("Tool") then
local n = t.Name:lower()
if n:find("medusa") or n:find("head") or n:find("stone") then return t end
end
end
end
return nil
end
function _G.MeridianUseMedusaCounter()
if not medCounterEnabled then return end
if _G.MeridianCounterState.medDebounce then return end
if tick() - (_G.MeridianCounterState.medLastUsed or 0) < _G.MeridianMedusaCooldown then return end
local c = LP.Character
if not c then return end
_G.MeridianCounterState.medDebounce = true
local med = _G.MeridianFindMedusa()
if not med then
_G.MeridianCounterState.medDebounce = false
return
end
if med.Parent ~= c then
local hum = c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if hum then pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(med) end) end
task.wait(0.05)
end
pcall(function() med:Activate() end)
_G.MeridianCounterState.medLastUsed = tick()
_G.MeridianCounterState.medDebounce = false
end
function _G.MeridianOnMedusaAnchorChanged(part)
return part:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Anchored"):Connect(function()
if medCounterEnabled and part.Anchored and part.Transparency == 1 then
_G.MeridianUseMedusaCounter()
end
end)
end
function _G.MeridianStartMedCounter(char)
_G.MeridianStopMedCounter()
char = char or LP.Character
if not char then return end
for _, part in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
if part:IsA("BasePart") then
table.insert(_G.MeridianCounterState.medConns, _G.MeridianOnMedusaAnchorChanged(part))
end
end
table.insert(_G.MeridianCounterState.medConns, char.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(part)
if part:IsA("BasePart") then
table.insert(_G.MeridianCounterState.medConns, _G.MeridianOnMedusaAnchorChanged(part))
end
end))
end
function _G.MeridianStopMedCounter()
for _, c in pairs(_G.MeridianCounterState.medConns or {}) do
pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end)
end
_G.MeridianCounterState.medConns = {}
_G.MeridianCounterState.medDebounce = false
end
-- ── INSTANT BAT COUNTER + BODY LOCK ─────────────────────────────
-- Trigger: StateChanged to Physics/Ragdoll/FallingDown (instant,
-- no Heartbeat poll). Swings bat 3x with no delay between fires.
-- Body lock: faces closest enemy for 1s via AssemblyAngularVelocity.

_G.MeridianBatCounterSlapList = {"Bat", "Slap", "Iron Slap", "Gold Slap", "Diamond Slap", "Emerald Slap", "Ruby Slap", "Dark Matter Slap", "Flame Slap", "Nuclear Slap", "Galaxy Slap", "Glitched Slap"}

function _G.MeridianFindBatForCounter()
    local c = LP.Character
    if not c then return nil end
    local bp = LP:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack") or LP:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
    for _, name in ipairs(_G.MeridianBatCounterSlapList) do
        local t = c:FindFirstChild(name) or (bp and bp:FindFirstChild(name))
        if t then return t end
    end
    for _, ch in ipairs(c:GetChildren()) do
        if ch:IsA("Tool") and ch.Name:lower():find("bat") then return ch end
    end
    if bp then
        for _, ch in ipairs(bp:GetChildren()) do
            if ch:IsA("Tool") and ch.Name:lower():find("bat") then return ch end
        end
    end
    return nil
end

-- Closest enemy HRP finder (used by body lock)
local function _batCounterGetClosestTarget()
    local root = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not root then return nil end
    local myPos = root.Position
    local closest, minDist = nil, math.huge
    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if plr ~= LP then
            local char = plr.Character
            if char then
                local tRoot = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local hum   = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if tRoot and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                    local dist = (tRoot.Position - myPos).Magnitude
                    if dist < minDist then
                        minDist = dist
                        closest = tRoot
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return closest
end

-- Body lock: face closest enemy for 1 second via angular velocity
local _batBodyLockActive = false
local function _batFaceAttackerFor1s()
    if _batBodyLockActive then return end
    _batBodyLockActive = true
    local c = LP.Character
    local root = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local hum2 = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not root or not hum2 then _batBodyLockActive = false; return end
    local target = _batCounterGetClosestTarget()
    if not target then _batBodyLockActive = false; return end
    hum2.AutoRotate = false
    local deadline = tick() + 1
    local fconn
    fconn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if tick() >= deadline then
            fconn:Disconnect()
            local c2   = LP.Character
            local hum3 = c2 and c2:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local root2 = c2 and c2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if hum3  then hum3.AutoRotate = true end
            if root2 then root2.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero end
            _batBodyLockActive = false
            return
        end
        local c2    = LP.Character
        local root2 = c2 and c2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not root2 then return end
        local toTarget = target.Position - root2.Position
        if toTarget.Magnitude > 0.1 then
            local goalCF = CFrame.lookAt(root2.Position, target.Position)
            local diffCF = root2.CFrame:Inverse() * goalCF
            local _, ry, _ = diffCF:ToEulerAnglesXYZ()
            ry = math.clamp(ry, -2.5, 2.5)
            root2.AssemblyAngularVelocity = root2.CFrame:VectorToWorldSpace(Vector3.new(0, ry * 42, 0))
        end
    end)
end

-- Instant swing: equip then fire 3x with no wait between fires
function _G.MeridianSwingBatForCounter(bat, char)
    if not bat or not char then return end
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if bat.Parent ~= char and hum then
        pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(bat) end)
    end
    local remote = bat:FindFirstChildOfClass("RemoteEvent") or bat:FindFirstChildOfClass("RemoteFunction")
    if remote and remote:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
        pcall(function() remote:FireServer() end)
        pcall(function() remote:FireServer() end)
        pcall(function() remote:FireServer() end)
    else
        pcall(function() bat:Activate() end)
        pcall(function() bat:Activate() end)
        pcall(function() bat:Activate() end)
    end
end

-- Swing: 2x fire with a 0.15s wait between (7up-accurate)
function _G.MeridianSwingBatForCounter(bat, char)
    if not bat or not char then return end
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if bat.Parent ~= char and hum then
        pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(bat) end)
        task.wait(0.05)
    end
    local remote = bat:FindFirstChildOfClass("RemoteEvent") or bat:FindFirstChildOfClass("RemoteFunction")
    if remote and remote:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
        pcall(function() remote:FireServer() end)
        task.wait(0.15)
        pcall(function() remote:FireServer() end)
    else
        pcall(function() bat:Activate() end)
        task.wait(0.15)
        pcall(function() bat:Activate() end)
    end
end

function _G.MeridianCounterIsRagdoll(hum)
    if not hum then return false end
    local st = hum:GetState()
    return st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
        or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
        or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown
        or hum.PlatformStand == true
end

-- Start: Heartbeat polling with a debounce flag (7up-accurate) instead
-- of hooking StateChanged - matches 7up's exact timing/behavior.
function _G.MeridianStartBatCounter()
    if _G.MeridianCounterState.batConn then return end
    _G.MeridianCounterState.batDebounce = false
    _G.MeridianCounterState.batConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not batCounterEnabled or _G.MeridianCounterState.batDebounce then return end
        local char = LP.Character
        if not char then return end
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not hum then return end
        local st = hum:GetState()
        local isRagdolled = st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
            or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
            or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown
        if isRagdolled then
            _G.MeridianCounterState.batDebounce = true
            task.spawn(function()
                local bat = _G.MeridianFindBatForCounter()
                if bat then _G.MeridianSwingBatForCounter(bat, char) end
                task.wait(0.5)
                _G.MeridianCounterState.batDebounce = false
            end)
        end
    end)
end

function _G.MeridianStopBatCounter()
    if _G.MeridianCounterState.batConn then
        _G.MeridianCounterState.batConn:Disconnect()
        _G.MeridianCounterState.batConn = nil
    end
    _G.MeridianCounterState.batDebounce = false
end

startBatCounter = _G.MeridianStartBatCounter
stopBatCounter  = _G.MeridianStopBatCounter
setupMedusaCounter = _G.MeridianStartMedCounter
stopMedusaCounter = _G.MeridianStopMedCounter
_G.MeridianNoPlayerCollisionState = _G.MeridianNoPlayerCollisionState or {connections = {}}
function _G.MeridianSetOtherPlayerCollision(state)
for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
if plr ~= LP and plr.Character then
for _, part in ipairs(plr.Character:GetDescendants()) do
if part:IsA("BasePart") then
pcall(function() part.CanCollide = state end)
end
end
end
end
end
function enableNoPlayerCollision()
if _G.MeridianNoPlayerCollisionState.running then return end
_G.MeridianNoPlayerCollisionEnabled = true
_G.MeridianNoPlayerCollisionState.running = true
for _, conn in ipairs(_G.MeridianNoPlayerCollisionState.connections or {}) do
pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end)
end
_G.MeridianNoPlayerCollisionState.connections = {}
_G.MeridianSetOtherPlayerCollision(false)
table.insert(_G.MeridianNoPlayerCollisionState.connections, LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
task.wait(0.5)
if _G.MeridianNoPlayerCollisionEnabled then _G.MeridianSetOtherPlayerCollision(false) end
end))
table.insert(_G.MeridianNoPlayerCollisionState.connections, Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(plr)
local c = plr.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
task.wait(0.5)
if _G.MeridianNoPlayerCollisionEnabled then _G.MeridianSetOtherPlayerCollision(false) end
end)
table.insert(_G.MeridianNoPlayerCollisionState.connections, c)
end))
local collisionScanElapsed = 0
table.insert(_G.MeridianNoPlayerCollisionState.connections, RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
if not _G.MeridianNoPlayerCollisionEnabled then return end
collisionScanElapsed = collisionScanElapsed + (dt or 0)
if collisionScanElapsed < 0.25 then return end
collisionScanElapsed = 0
for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
if plr ~= LP and plr.Character then
for _, part in ipairs(plr.Character:GetDescendants()) do
if part:IsA("BasePart") and part.CanCollide == true then
pcall(function() part.CanCollide = false end)
end
end
end
end
end))
end
function disableNoPlayerCollision()
if not _G.MeridianNoPlayerCollisionState.running then
_G.MeridianNoPlayerCollisionEnabled = false
return
end
_G.MeridianNoPlayerCollisionEnabled = false
_G.MeridianNoPlayerCollisionState.running = false
for _, conn in ipairs(_G.MeridianNoPlayerCollisionState.connections or {}) do
pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end)
end
_G.MeridianNoPlayerCollisionState.connections = {}
_G.MeridianSetOtherPlayerCollision(true)
end
function _G.MeridianSafeModeGetCountdownLabel()
local ok, label = pcall(function()
return LP.PlayerGui
and LP.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("DuelsMachineTopFrame")
and LP.PlayerGui.DuelsMachineTopFrame:FindFirstChild("DuelsMachineTopFrame")
and LP.PlayerGui.DuelsMachineTopFrame.DuelsMachineTopFrame:FindFirstChild("Timer")
and LP.PlayerGui.DuelsMachineTopFrame.DuelsMachineTopFrame.Timer:FindFirstChild("Label")
end)
return (ok and label) or nil
end
function _G.MeridianSafeModeCountdownNumber(text)
local t = tostring(text or ""):upper():gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
if t == "GO" or t == "START" or t == "READY" then return true end
local n = tonumber(t)
return n ~= nil and n >= 0 and n <= 10
end
function _G.MeridianSafeModeInDuelCountdown()
local label = _G.MeridianSafeModeGetCountdownLabel()
return label and _G.MeridianSafeModeCountdownNumber(label.Text) or false
end
_G.MeridianSafeModeBlockedTools = {
bat=true, slap=true, sword=true, gun=true, pistol=true, rifle=true,
medusa=true, hammer=true, axe=true, knife=true, katana=true, blade=true, fist=true,
}
function _G.MeridianSafeModeIsCarryableTool(tool)
if not tool or not tool:IsA("Tool") then return false end
local name = tool.Name:lower()
for word in pairs(_G.MeridianSafeModeBlockedTools) do
if name:find(word, 1, true) then return false end
end
return true
end
function _G.MeridianSafeModeHoldingBrainrot()
local ok, val = pcall(function() return LP:GetAttribute("Stealing") end)
if ok and val == true then return true end
local ok2, val2 = pcall(function() return LP:GetAttribute("AntiKick") end)
if ok2 and val2 == true then return true end
local char = LP.Character
if not char then return false end
local ok3, val3 = pcall(function() return char:GetAttribute("Stealing") end)
if ok3 and val3 == true then return true end
if _G.AutoCarrySpeed and type(_G.AutoCarrySpeed.IsCarryingBrainrot) == "function" then
local okCarry, carrying = pcall(function() return _G.AutoCarrySpeed.IsCarryingBrainrot(char) end)
if okCarry and carrying then return true end
end
for _, name in ipairs({"Carrying", "IsCarrying", "Grabbed", "Holding", "StealHold", "HasGrab"}) do
local v = char:FindFirstChild(name, true)
if v then
if v:IsA("BoolValue") and v.Value then return true end
if v:IsA("ObjectValue") and v.Value then return true end
if v:IsA("StringValue") and v.Value ~= "" then return true end
end
end
for _, child in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
if child:IsA("Model") and child:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true) then
local n = child.Name:lower()
if n:find("brainrot") or n:find("animal") or n:find("carry") or n:find("grab") or n:find("steal") or n:find("hold") then
return true
end
end
end
return false
end
function _G.MeridianSafeModeIsLocked()
if not safeModeEnabled then return false end
return _G.MeridianSafeModeInDuelCountdown() or _G.MeridianSafeModeHoldingBrainrot()
end
function _G.MeridianSafeModeForceStop(reason)
local stopped = false
if _G.MeridianNormalAimbotOn and _G.MeridianStopNormalAimbot then _G.MeridianStopNormalAimbot(); stopped = true end
if _G.MeridianNormalAimbotOn and _G.MeridianAimbotVersion == "V3" and _G.MeridianStopV3Aimbot then _G.MeridianStopV3Aimbot(); stopped = true end
if _G.MeridianAntiDesyncAimbotOn and _G.MeridianStopAntiDesyncAimbot then _G.MeridianStopAntiDesyncAimbot(); stopped = true end
if autoLeftEnabled then
autoLeftEnabled = false
if _G.MeridianSetAutoLeftVisual then _G.MeridianSetAutoLeftVisual(false) end
if _G.MeridianStopAutoLeft then _G.MeridianStopAutoLeft() end
stopped = true
end
if autoRightEnabled then
autoRightEnabled = false
if _G.MeridianSetAutoRightVisual then _G.MeridianSetAutoRightVisual(false) end
if _G.MeridianStopAutoRight then _G.MeridianStopAutoRight() end
stopped = true
end
if stopped and showActionNotification then pcall(function() showActionNotification(reason or "SAFE MODE LOCK") end) end
end
function _G.MeridianSafeModeTryStart()
if _G.MeridianSafeModeIsLocked and _G.MeridianSafeModeIsLocked() then
_G.MeridianSafeModeForceStop("SAFE MODE LOCK")
return false
end
return true
end
_G.MeridianSafeModeMonitorStarted = _G.MeridianSafeModeMonitorStarted or false
if not _G.MeridianSafeModeMonitorStarted then
_G.MeridianSafeModeMonitorStarted = true
RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if safeModeEnabled and _G.MeridianSafeModeIsLocked and _G.MeridianSafeModeIsLocked() then
_G.MeridianSafeModeForceStop("SAFE MODE LOCK")
end
end)
end
LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
task.wait(0.5)
if medCounterEnabled then _G.MeridianStartMedCounter(char) end
if batCounterEnabled then _G.MeridianStartBatCounter() end
end)
-- Body lock aimbot bridge: when body lock is on, all aimbot loops
-- use the body lock target instead of their own scan.
local function _MeridianGetBodyLockHRP()
    if not _G.MeridianBodyLockEnabled then return nil end
    local plr = _G.MeridianGetNearestBodyLockTarget and _G.MeridianGetNearestBodyLockTarget()
    if plr and plr.Character then
        return plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    end
    return nil
end

_G.MeridianNormalAimbot = _G.MeridianNormalAimbot or {conn = nil, target = nil, swingCooldown = false}
function _G.MeridianFindAimbotBat()
local char = LP.Character
if not char then return nil end
for _, tool in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
if tool:IsA("Tool") and (tool.Name:lower():find("bat") or tool.Name:lower():find("slap")) then
return tool
end
end
local bp = LP:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
if bp then
for _, tool in ipairs(bp:GetChildren()) do
if tool:IsA("Tool") and (tool.Name:lower():find("bat") or tool.Name:lower():find("slap")) then
return tool
end
end
end
return nil
end
function _G.MeridianGetClosestAimbotTarget()
local root = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if not root then return nil end
local closest, minDist = nil, math.huge
for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
if plr ~= LP and plr.Character then
local tRoot = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
local hum = plr.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if tRoot and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
local dist = (tRoot.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
if dist < minDist then
minDist = dist
closest = tRoot
end
end
end
end
return closest
end
function _G.MeridianGetNormalAimbotSpeed()
if currentSpeedMode == "Lagger" or currentSpeedMode == "Lagger Carry" then
return tonumber(LAGGER_AIMBOT_SPEED) or 40
end
return tonumber(AIMBOT_SPEED) or 58
end
function _G.MeridianGetSelectedAimbotSpeedValues()
return tonumber(AIMBOT_SPEED) or 58, tonumber(LAGGER_AIMBOT_SPEED) or 40
end
function _G.MeridianSetSelectedAimbotSpeedValues(normalValue, laggerValue)
if normalValue then AIMBOT_SPEED = normalValue end
if laggerValue then LAGGER_AIMBOT_SPEED = laggerValue end
end
function _G.MeridianRefreshAimbotSpeedBoxes()
local n, l = _G.MeridianGetSelectedAimbotSpeedValues()
if _G.MeridianAimbotSpeedBox then _G.MeridianAimbotSpeedBox.Text = tostring(n) end
if _G.MeridianLaggerAimbotSpeedBox then _G.MeridianLaggerAimbotSpeedBox.Text = tostring(l) end
end

_G.MeridianNormalAimbot = _G.MeridianNormalAimbot or {
    conn = nil,
    target = nil,
    lastScan = 0,
    attachment = nil,
    linearVelocity = nil,
}

local function _MeridianAimbotEnsureLinearVelocity(root)
    local S = _G.MeridianNormalAimbot

    if S.linearVelocity
        and S.linearVelocity.Parent
        and S.attachment
        and S.attachment.Parent == root then
        S.linearVelocity.Enabled = true
        return S.linearVelocity
    end

    if S.linearVelocity then
        pcall(function() S.linearVelocity:Destroy() end)
    end
    if S.attachment then
        pcall(function() S.attachment:Destroy() end)
    end

    local att = Instance.new("Attachment")
    att.Name = "MeridianAimbotAttachment"
    att.Parent = root

    local lv = Instance.new("LinearVelocity")
    lv.Name = "MeridianAimbotLinearVelocity"
    lv.Attachment0 = att
    lv.RelativeTo = Enum.ActuatorRelativeTo.World
    lv.ForceLimitMode = Enum.ForceLimitMode.Magnitude
    lv.MaxForce = math.huge
    lv.VectorVelocity = Vector3.zero
    lv.Enabled = true
    lv.Parent = root

    S.attachment = att
    S.linearVelocity = lv
    return lv
end

local function _MeridianAimbotReleaseLinearVelocity()
    local S = _G.MeridianNormalAimbot
    if S.linearVelocity and S.linearVelocity.Parent then
        S.linearVelocity.VectorVelocity = Vector3.zero
        S.linearVelocity.Enabled = false
    end
end

function _G.MeridianStartNormalAimbot()
    if _G.MeridianSafeModeTryStart and not _G.MeridianSafeModeTryStart() then
        return false
    end


    if _G.MeridianNormalAimbot.conn then
        pcall(function() _G.MeridianNormalAimbot.conn:Disconnect() end)
        _G.MeridianNormalAimbot.conn = nil
    end

    _G.MeridianNormalAimbotAutoTPWasEnabled = false
    if autoTPEnabled then
        _G.MeridianNormalAimbotAutoTPWasEnabled = true
        stopAutoTP()
        if setAutoTPVisual then setAutoTPVisual(false) end
    end

    _G.MeridianNormalAimbotOn = true
    _G.MeridianNormalAimbot.target = nil
    _G.MeridianNormalAimbot.lastScan = 0

    local hum0 = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum0 then hum0.AutoRotate = false end

    _G.MeridianNormalAimbot.conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not _G.MeridianNormalAimbotOn or selectedAimbotMode ~= "Normal" then
            return
        end

        local char = LP.Character
        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not char or not hum or not root or hum.Health <= 0 then
            return
        end

        --Leaked By Xlu0 AndPrime--
        if not char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool") then
            local bat = _G.MeridianFindAimbotBat()
            if bat then
                pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(bat) end)
            end
        end

        --Leaked By Xlu0 AndPrime--
        local now = tick()
        local target = _G.MeridianNormalAimbot.target

        local _blHRP = _MeridianGetBodyLockHRP()
        if _blHRP then
            target = _blHRP
            _G.MeridianNormalAimbot.target = target
        elseif now - (_G.MeridianNormalAimbot.lastScan or 0) > 0.1
            or not target
            or not target.Parent then

            _G.MeridianNormalAimbot.lastScan = now
            local closest, minDist = nil, math.huge

            for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if plr ~= LP and plr.Character then
                    local tRoot = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    local th = plr.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
