                    if tRoot and th and th.Health > 0 then
                        local dist = (tRoot.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
                        if dist < minDist then
                            minDist = dist
                            closest = tRoot
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            target = closest
            _G.MeridianNormalAimbot.target = target
        end

        if not target then
            hum.AutoRotate = true
            root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            _MeridianAimbotReleaseLinearVelocity()
            return
        end

        local targetHum = target.Parent and target.Parent:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not targetHum or targetHum.Health <= 0 then
            _G.MeridianNormalAimbot.target = nil
            _MeridianAimbotReleaseLinearVelocity()
            return
        end

        hum.AutoRotate = false

        --Leaked By Xlu0 AndPrime--
        local targetVel = target.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        local aimTargetPos =
            target.Position
            + (targetVel * math.clamp(targetVel.Magnitude / 130, 0.05, 0.15))
            + Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)

        local look = aimTargetPos - root.Position
        local flatLook = Vector3.new(look.X, 0, look.Z)

        --Leaked By Xlu0 AndPrime--
        if look.Magnitude > 0.01 and flatLook.Magnitude > 0.01 then
            local targetYaw = math.deg(math.atan2(-flatLook.X, -flatLook.Z))
            local yawDelta = (targetYaw - root.Orientation.Y + 180) % 360 - 180

            local targetPitch = math.deg(math.atan2(look.Y, flatLook.Magnitude))
            local pitchDelta = (targetPitch - root.Orientation.X + 180) % 360 - 180

            local yawRate = math.clamp(
                math.rad(yawDelta) * 285,
                -28,
                28
            )

            local pitchRate = math.clamp(
                math.rad(pitchDelta) * 285,
                -28,
                28
            )

            local yawRad = math.rad(root.Orientation.Y)
            local rightAxis = Vector3.new(
                math.cos(yawRad),
                0,
                -math.sin(yawRad)
            )

            root.AssemblyAngularVelocity =
                Vector3.new(0, yawRate, 0)
                + (rightAxis * pitchRate)
        else
            root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end

        --Leaked By Xlu0 AndPrime--
        local dir = look.Magnitude > 0.01 and look.Unit or Vector3.zero
        local standPos =
            aimTargetPos
            - (dir * -2.8)
            + Vector3.new(0, 4.75, 0)

        local moveDir = standPos - root.Position
        local hDir = Vector3.new(moveDir.X, 0, moveDir.Z)

        local selectedSpeed =
            (currentSpeedMode == "Lagger" or currentSpeedMode == "Lagger Carry")
            and (tonumber(LAGGER_AIMBOT_SPEED) or 40)
            or (tonumber(AIMBOT_SPEED) or 58)

        local hVel =
            hDir.Magnitude > 0.1
            and (hDir.Unit * selectedSpeed)
            or Vector3.zero

        local verticalSpeed = 52
        local vVel =
            math.abs(moveDir.Y) > 0.1
            and Vector3.new(
                0,
                math.sign(moveDir.Y) * verticalSpeed,
                0
            )
            or Vector3.new(0, -2, 0)

        --Leaked By Xlu0 AndPrime--
        local lv = _MeridianAimbotEnsureLinearVelocity(root)
        lv.VectorVelocity = hVel + vVel

        if hDir.Magnitude > 0.5 then
            hum:Move(hDir.Unit, false)
        end

        if autoSwingEnabled then
            local bat = char:FindFirstChild("Bat") or _G.MeridianFindAimbotBat()
            if bat and bat:IsA("Tool") then
                pcall(function() bat:Activate() end)
            end
        end
    end)

    if _G.MeridianRefreshAimbotVisual then
        _G.MeridianRefreshAimbotVisual()
    end

    return true
end

function _G.MeridianStopNormalAimbot()
    _G.MeridianNormalAimbotOn = false

    if _G.MeridianNormalAimbot and _G.MeridianNormalAimbot.conn then
        pcall(function() _G.MeridianNormalAimbot.conn:Disconnect() end)
        _G.MeridianNormalAimbot.conn = nil
    end

    if _G.MeridianNormalAimbot then
        _G.MeridianNormalAimbot.target = nil
        _G.MeridianNormalAimbot.lastScan = 0
    end

    _MeridianAimbotReleaseLinearVelocity()

    local char = LP.Character
    local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

    if root then
        root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
    end

    if hum then
        hum.AutoRotate = true
    end

    if _G.MeridianNormalAimbotAutoTPWasEnabled then
        _G.MeridianNormalAimbotAutoTPWasEnabled = false
        autoTPEnabled = true
        if setAutoTPVisual then setAutoTPVisual(true) end
        startAutoTP()
    end

    if _G.MeridianRefreshAimbotVisual then
        _G.MeridianRefreshAimbotVisual()
    end
end


-- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- MERIDIAN V3 AIMBOT — weapon-switch target rotation
-- When the player equips a new tool mid-flight the aimbot immediately
-- re-scans for the closest living target and locks on, so swapping
-- weapons never leaves you chasing a dead target. All existing speed
-- vars (AIMBOT_SPEED / LAGGER_AIMBOT_SPEED) and SafeMode gate are
-- respected 1-to-1 with the V1/V2 systems above.
-- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
_G.MeridianV3Aimbot = _G.MeridianV3Aimbot or {
    conn       = nil,
    toolConn   = nil,
    target     = nil,
    targetPlr  = nil,
    lastScan   = 0,
    attachment = nil,
    linearVelocity = nil,
}

local function _V3EnsureLinearVelocity(root)
    local S = _G.MeridianV3Aimbot
    if S.linearVelocity and S.linearVelocity.Parent
        and S.attachment and S.attachment.Parent == root then
        S.linearVelocity.Enabled = true
        return S.linearVelocity
    end
    if S.linearVelocity then pcall(function() S.linearVelocity:Destroy() end) end
    if S.attachment   then pcall(function() S.attachment:Destroy() end) end

    local att = Instance.new("Attachment")
    att.Name   = "MeridianV3AimbotAtt"
    att.Parent = root

    local lv = Instance.new("LinearVelocity")
    lv.Name          = "MeridianV3AimbotLV"
    lv.Attachment0   = att
    lv.RelativeTo    = Enum.ActuatorRelativeTo.World
    lv.ForceLimitMode = Enum.ForceLimitMode.Magnitude
    lv.MaxForce      = math.huge
    lv.VectorVelocity = Vector3.zero
    lv.Enabled       = true
    lv.Parent        = att

    S.attachment    = att
    S.linearVelocity = lv
    return lv
end

local function _V3ReleaseLinearVelocity()
    local S = _G.MeridianV3Aimbot
    if S.linearVelocity and S.linearVelocity.Parent then
        S.linearVelocity.VectorVelocity = Vector3.zero
        S.linearVelocity.Enabled = false
    end
end

-- Hard-scan: always return the single closest alive player
local function _V3ScanClosest()
    local root = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not root then return nil, nil end
    local best, bestPlr, minD = nil, nil, math.huge
    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if plr ~= LP and plr.Character then
            local tr  = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local hum = plr.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if tr and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                local d = (tr.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
                if d < minD then minD = d; best = tr; bestPlr = plr end
            end
        end
    end
    return best, bestPlr
end

-- Sticky scan: keep current target unless a closer one is significantly
-- nearer (avoids micro-flapping between equally-distant targets).
local function _V3StickyTarget(curRoot)
    local root = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not root then return nil, nil end
    if curRoot and curRoot.Parent then
        local curHum = curRoot.Parent:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if curHum and curHum.Health > 0 then
            local newBest, newPlr = _V3ScanClosest()
            if not newBest then return curRoot, Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(curRoot.Parent) end
            local curD = (curRoot.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
            local newD = (newBest.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
            -- only swap if new target is meaningfully closer (>15% gap)
            if newD >= curD * 0.85 then
                return curRoot, Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(curRoot.Parent)
            end
            return newBest, newPlr
        end
    end
    return _V3ScanClosest()
end

function _G.MeridianStartV3Aimbot()
    if _G.MeridianSafeModeTryStart and not _G.MeridianSafeModeTryStart() then return false end

    -- Stop any running aimbot
    if _G.MeridianStopNormalAimbot  then pcall(_G.MeridianStopNormalAimbot) end
    if _G.MeridianStopAimbotV2      then pcall(_G.MeridianStopAimbotV2) end
    if _G.MeridianStopBypassAimbot  then pcall(_G.MeridianStopBypassAimbot, false) end

    local S = _G.MeridianV3Aimbot
    if S.conn    then pcall(function() S.conn:Disconnect()    end); S.conn    = nil end
    if S.toolConn then pcall(function() S.toolConn:Disconnect() end); S.toolConn = nil end

    -- Pause AutoTP while running
    _G.MeridianV3AimbotAutoTPWasEnabled = autoTPEnabled
    if autoTPEnabled then
        stopAutoTP()
        if setAutoTPVisual then setAutoTPVisual(false) end
    end

    _G.MeridianNormalAimbotOn       = true
    _G.MeridianBypassAimbotOn       = false
    _G.MeridianAntiBypassAimbotOn   = false
    S.target    = nil
    S.targetPlr = nil
    S.lastScan  = 0

    local hum0 = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum0 then hum0.AutoRotate = false end

    -- Weapon-switch hook: force an immediate re-scan when the player
    -- equips anything new so the target is always fresh.
    local char0 = LP.Character
    if char0 then
        S.toolConn = char0.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
            if child:IsA("Tool") then
                S.target    = nil
                S.targetPlr = nil
                S.lastScan  = 0
            end
        end)
    end

    S.conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not _G.MeridianNormalAimbotOn then return end

        local char = LP.Character
        local hum  = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not char or not hum or not root or hum.Health <= 0 then return end

        hum.AutoRotate = false

        -- Auto-equip bat if nothing held
        if not char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool") then
            local bat = _G.MeridianFindAimbotBat and _G.MeridianFindAimbotBat()
            if bat then pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(bat) end) end
        end

        -- Target acquisition (body lock overrides sticky scan)
        local now = tick()
        local _blHRP_v3 = _MeridianGetBodyLockHRP()
        if _blHRP_v3 then
            S.target = _blHRP_v3
            S.targetPlr = nil
        elseif now - S.lastScan > 0.08 or not S.target or not S.target.Parent then
            S.lastScan = now
            S.target, S.targetPlr = _V3StickyTarget(S.target)
        end

        if not S.target then
            hum.AutoRotate = true
            root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            _V3ReleaseLinearVelocity()
            return
        end

        local tHum = S.target.Parent and S.target.Parent:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not tHum or tHum.Health <= 0 then
            S.target = nil; S.targetPlr = nil
            _V3ReleaseLinearVelocity()
            return
        end

        hum.AutoRotate = false

        -- ── V3 = BYPASS CORE (full replacement) ────────────────────
        -- Drives to a point behind AND above the target's LookVector
        -- instead of an orbit stand-position or a flat behind-offset.
        -- No prediction ball/visual — pure velocity-drive positioning.
        local spd = (currentSpeedMode == "Lagger" or currentSpeedMode == "Lagger Carry")
            and (tonumber(_G.MeridianAntiBypassLaggerAimbotSpeed) or 40)
            or  (tonumber(_G.MeridianAntiBypassAimbotSpeed) or 58)

        local BEHIND_OFFSET = 2.5
        local ABOVE_OFFSET = 5
        local targetLook = Vector3.new(S.target.CFrame.LookVector.X, 0, S.target.CFrame.LookVector.Z)
        if targetLook.Magnitude > 0.01 then targetLook = targetLook.Unit end
        local behindPos = Vector3.new(
            S.target.Position.X - targetLook.X * BEHIND_OFFSET,
            S.target.Position.Y + ABOVE_OFFSET,
            S.target.Position.Z - targetLook.Z * BEHIND_OFFSET
        )

        local dir = behindPos - root.Position
        local lv  = _V3EnsureLinearVelocity(root)
        lv.VectorVelocity = dir.Magnitude > 0.01 and (dir.Unit * spd) or Vector3.zero

        local toTarget = Vector3.new(S.target.Position.X - root.Position.X, 0, S.target.Position.Z - root.Position.Z)
        if toTarget.Magnitude > 0.01 then
            local targetYaw = math.deg(math.atan2(-toTarget.X, -toTarget.Z))
            local yawDelta  = (targetYaw - root.Orientation.Y + 180) % 360 - 180
            root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new(0, math.clamp(math.rad(yawDelta) * 285, -28, 28), 0)
        else
            root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end

        local hDist = Vector3.new(root.Position.X - S.target.Position.X, 0, root.Position.Z - S.target.Position.Z).Magnitude
        local vDist = math.abs(root.Position.Y - S.target.Position.Y)
        if hDist <= 6 and vDist <= 10 and autoSwingEnabled then
            local bat = char:FindFirstChild("Bat") or (_G.MeridianFindAimbotBat and _G.MeridianFindAimbotBat())
            if bat and bat:IsA("Tool") then pcall(function() bat:Activate() end) end
        end
    end)

    if _G.MeridianRefreshAimbotVisual then _G.MeridianRefreshAimbotVisual() end
    return true
end

function _G.MeridianStopV3Aimbot()
    local S = _G.MeridianV3Aimbot
    _G.MeridianNormalAimbotOn = false
    if S.conn     then pcall(function() S.conn:Disconnect()     end); S.conn     = nil end
    if S.toolConn then pcall(function() S.toolConn:Disconnect() end); S.toolConn = nil end
    S.target    = nil
    S.targetPlr = nil
    S.lastScan  = 0

    _V3ReleaseLinearVelocity()

    local char = LP.Character
    local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local hum  = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if root then root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero end
    if hum  then hum.AutoRotate = true end

    if _G.MeridianV3AimbotAutoTPWasEnabled then
        _G.MeridianV3AimbotAutoTPWasEnabled = false
        autoTPEnabled = true
        if setAutoTPVisual then setAutoTPVisual(true) end
        startAutoTP()
    end

    if _G.MeridianRefreshAimbotVisual then _G.MeridianRefreshAimbotVisual() end
end

-- Expose under the same API surface as V1/V2 so the version selector
-- and keybind handler both resolve correctly without extra wiring.
_G.MeridianV3AimbotStart = _G.MeridianStartV3Aimbot
_G.MeridianV3AimbotStop  = _G.MeridianStopV3Aimbot
-- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- END MERIDIAN V3 AIMBOT
-- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

-- Meridian Unwalk — Void.VS logic: destroy Animate script so no walk anim plays.
-- Guard added: if the game re-inserts Animate (tool equip / state change) we
-- destroy it again immediately so the freeze holds for the full toggle duration.
_G.MeridianUnwalkEnabled = _G.MeridianUnwalkEnabled or false
_G.MeridianUnwalkSavedAnimate = nil
local _unwalkGuardConn = nil

local function _unwalkDestroyGuard()
    if _unwalkGuardConn then
        pcall(function() _unwalkGuardConn:Disconnect() end)
        _unwalkGuardConn = nil
    end
end

local function _unwalkSetGuard(char)
    _unwalkDestroyGuard()
    if not char then return end
    _unwalkGuardConn = char.ChildAdded:Connect(function(obj)
        if not _G.MeridianUnwalkEnabled then return end
        if obj.Name == "Animate" and obj:IsA("LocalScript") then
            -- Game re-inserted Animate — destroy it again
            task.defer(function()
                if _G.MeridianUnwalkEnabled and obj and obj.Parent then
                    obj:Destroy()
                end
            end)
        end
    end)
end

function _G.MeridianStartUnwalk()
    local c = LP.Character
    if not c then return end
    local hum = c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum then
        for _, t in ipairs(hum:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
            pcall(function() t:Stop(0) end)
        end
    end
    local anim = c:FindFirstChild("Animate")
    if anim then
        _G.MeridianUnwalkSavedAnimate = anim:Clone()
        anim:Destroy()
    end
    _unwalkSetGuard(c)
    _G.MeridianUnwalkEnabled = true
end

function _G.MeridianStopUnwalk()
    _unwalkDestroyGuard()
    local c = LP.Character
    if c and _G.MeridianUnwalkSavedAnimate then
        _G.MeridianUnwalkSavedAnimate:Clone().Parent = c
        _G.MeridianUnwalkSavedAnimate = nil
    end
    _G.MeridianUnwalkEnabled = false
end

function _G.MeridianToggleUnwalk()
    if _G.MeridianUnwalkEnabled then
        _G.MeridianStopUnwalk()
    else
        _G.MeridianStartUnwalk()
    end
end

LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
    _unwalkDestroyGuard()
    _G.MeridianUnwalkSavedAnimate = nil
    if _G.MeridianUnwalkEnabled then
        task.wait(0.5)
        pcall(_G.MeridianStartUnwalk)
    end
end)

-- Meridian Duels -- AIMBOT V2 → replaced with 7UP Duels V1 aimbot engine
-- External API preserved: _G.MeridianStartAimbotV2 / _G.MeridianStopAimbotV2
-- Internal engine: AceNormalAimbot from 7up_duels_by_phantom (RenderStepped chase)
--   velocity lead 0.14s | look offset 0.3 studs | Y pull coeff 19.5
--   Y bias 3.7 studs | Y velocity weight 0.8 | floor min clamp 13
--   vertical clamp [-70, 110] | horizontal lerp 0.8 | angular clamp ±2.5×42

-- Shared helpers (Meridian-namespaced to avoid 7UP collision)
local function _MeridianV2FindBat()
    local char = LP.Character
    if not char then return nil end
    for _, t in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
        if t:IsA("Tool") and (t.Name:lower():find("bat") or t.Name:lower():find("slap")) then
            return t
        end
    end
    local bp = LP:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
    if bp then
        for _, t in ipairs(bp:GetChildren()) do
            if t:IsA("Tool") and (t.Name:lower():find("bat") or t.Name:lower():find("slap")) then
                return t
            end
        end
    end
    return nil
end

local function _MeridianV2GetClosestTarget()
    local root = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not root then return nil end
    local closest, minDist = nil, math.huge
    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if plr ~= LP and plr.Character then
            local tRoot = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local hum   = plr.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if tRoot and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                local dist = (tRoot.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
                if dist < minDist then minDist = dist; closest = tRoot end
            end
        end
    end
    return closest
end

-- State table (same shape as old MeridianAimbotV2 for compat)
_G.MeridianAimbotV2 = _G.MeridianAimbotV2 or {
    conn          = nil,
    target        = nil,
    targetPlr     = nil,
    swingCooldown = false,
}

local function _MeridianV2GetSpeed()
    local laggerSpeed = tonumber(_G.MeridianAntiBypassLaggerAimbotSpeed) or 40
    local normalSpeed = tonumber(_G.MeridianAntiBypassAimbotSpeed)       or 58
    local inLag = _G.MeridianLaggerMode and _G.MeridianLaggerMode ~= 0
    return inLag and laggerSpeed or normalSpeed
end

local function _MeridianV2SwingBat(char)
    local bat = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool") or _MeridianV2FindBat()
    if not bat then return end
    if _G.MeridianAimbotV2.swingCooldown then return end
    _G.MeridianAimbotV2.swingCooldown = true
    pcall(function() bat:Activate() end)
    task.delay(0.08, function()
        if _G.MeridianAimbotV2 then _G.MeridianAimbotV2.swingCooldown = false end
    end)
end

function _G.MeridianStartAimbotV2()
    if _G.MeridianAimbotV2 and _G.MeridianAimbotV2.conn then
        pcall(function() _G.MeridianAimbotV2.conn:Disconnect() end)
        _G.MeridianAimbotV2.conn = nil
    end

    _G.MeridianNormalAimbotOn = true

    if _G.MeridianNormalAimbotAutoTPWasEnabled == nil then
        if autoTPEnabled then
            _G.MeridianNormalAimbotAutoTPWasEnabled = true
            autoTPEnabled = false
            if stopAutoTP then stopAutoTP() end
            if setAutoTPVisual then setAutoTPVisual(false) end
        else
            _G.MeridianNormalAimbotAutoTPWasEnabled = false
        end
    end

    local hum0 = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum0 then hum0.AutoRotate = false end

    _G.MeridianAimbotV2.swingCooldown = false
    _G.MeridianAimbotV2.target        = nil
    _G.MeridianAimbotV2.targetPlr     = nil

    _G.MeridianAimbotV2.conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if not _G.MeridianNormalAimbotOn then return end
        local char = LP.Character
        if not char then return end
        local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not root then return end
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not hum then return end

        local bat = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool") or _MeridianV2FindBat()
        if bat and bat.Parent ~= char then
            pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(bat) end)
        end

        local target = _MeridianV2GetClosestTarget()
        if not target then return end
        _G.MeridianAimbotV2.target = target

        local targetVel  = target.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        local myPos      = root.Position
        local targetPos  = target.Position
        local predictPos = targetPos + targetVel * 0.14 + target.CFrame.LookVector * 0.3
        local direction  = predictPos - myPos
        if direction.Magnitude < 0.01 then return end

        local flatDir = Vector3.new(direction.X, 0, direction.Z)
        if flatDir.Magnitude < 0.01 then return end
        flatDir = flatDir.Unit

        local chaseSpeed    = _MeridianV2GetSpeed()
        local desiredHeight = targetPos.Y + 3.7
        local yVel          = (desiredHeight - myPos.Y) * 19.5 + targetVel.Y * 0.8
        if hum.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air then yVel = math.max(yVel, 13) end
        yVel = math.clamp(yVel, -70, 110)

        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity:Lerp(
            Vector3.new(flatDir.X * chaseSpeed, yVel, flatDir.Z * chaseSpeed), 0.8
        )

        local predictTime  = math.clamp(targetVel.Magnitude / 150, 0.05, 0.2)
        local predictedPos = targetPos + targetVel * predictTime
        local toPredict    = predictedPos - myPos
        if toPredict.Magnitude > 0.1 then
            local goalCF         = CFrame.lookAt(myPos, predictedPos)
            local diffCF         = root.CFrame:Inverse() * goalCF
            local rx, ry, rz     = diffCF:ToEulerAnglesXYZ()
            root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = root.CFrame:VectorToWorldSpace(Vector3.new(
                math.clamp(rx, -2.5, 2.5) * 42,
                math.clamp(ry, -2.5, 2.5) * 42,
                math.clamp(rz, -2.5, 2.5) * 42
            ))
        end

        if _G.MeridianAutoSwingEnabled then
            _MeridianV2SwingBat(char)
        end
    end)

    if _G.MeridianRefreshAimbotVisual then _G.MeridianRefreshAimbotVisual() end
    return true
end

function _G.MeridianStopAimbotV2()
    if _G.MeridianAimbotV2 and _G.MeridianAimbotV2.conn then
        pcall(function() _G.MeridianAimbotV2.conn:Disconnect() end)
        _G.MeridianAimbotV2.conn = nil
    end
    if _G.MeridianAimbotV2 then
        _G.MeridianAimbotV2.target        = nil
        _G.MeridianAimbotV2.targetPlr     = nil
        _G.MeridianAimbotV2.swingCooldown = false
    end
    _G.MeridianNormalAimbotOn = false
    local char = LP.Character
    local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local hum  = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if root then
        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity  = Vector3.new(0, root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y, 0)
        root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
    end
    if hum then hum.AutoRotate = true end
    if _G.MeridianNormalAimbotAutoTPWasEnabled then
        _G.MeridianNormalAimbotAutoTPWasEnabled = false
        autoTPEnabled = true
        if setAutoTPVisual then setAutoTPVisual(true) end
        startAutoTP()
    end
    if _G.MeridianRefreshAimbotVisual then _G.MeridianRefreshAimbotVisual() end
end

-- Meridian Duels -- mutual exclusivity: Auto Left, Auto Right, TP Bat,
-- and Aimbot (V1 or V2) are treated as one group - only one of them can
-- be on at a time. Turning any one of them on turns the other three off
-- first. "except" is the name of the one being turned on, so it's
-- skipped (no point stopping the thing you're about to start).
function _G.MeridianStopAllExclusiveMovement(except)
if except ~= "autoLeft" and autoLeftEnabled then
autoLeftEnabled = false
if _G.MeridianStopAutoLeft then pcall(_G.MeridianStopAutoLeft) end
if _G.MeridianSetAutoLeftVisual then pcall(_G.MeridianSetAutoLeftVisual, false) end
end
if except ~= "autoRight" and autoRightEnabled then
autoRightEnabled = false
if _G.MeridianStopAutoRight then pcall(_G.MeridianStopAutoRight) end
if _G.MeridianSetAutoRightVisual then pcall(_G.MeridianSetAutoRightVisual, false) end
end
if except ~= "tpBat" and _G.MeridianAntiDesyncAimbotOn then
if _G.MeridianStopAntiDesyncAimbot then pcall(_G.MeridianStopAntiDesyncAimbot) end
end
if except ~= "aimbot" and _G.MeridianNormalAimbotOn then
    if _G.MeridianAimbotVersion == "V3" and _G.MeridianStopV3Aimbot then
        pcall(_G.MeridianStopV3Aimbot)
    elseif _G.MeridianAimbotVersion == "V2" and _G.MeridianStopAimbotV2 then
        pcall(_G.MeridianStopAimbotV2)
    elseif _G.MeridianStopNormalAimbot then
        pcall(_G.MeridianStopNormalAimbot)
    end
end
end

function _G.MeridianToggleSelectedAimbot()
selectedAimbotMode = "Normal"
if _G.MeridianNormalAimbotOn then
    -- Stop whichever version is running
    if _G.MeridianAimbotVersion == "V3" and _G.MeridianStopV3Aimbot then
        _G.MeridianStopV3Aimbot()
    elseif _G.MeridianAimbotVersion == "V2" and _G.MeridianStopAimbotV2 then
        _G.MeridianStopAimbotV2()
    elseif _G.MeridianStopNormalAimbot then
        _G.MeridianStopNormalAimbot()
    end
else
    _G.MeridianStopAllExclusiveMovement("aimbot")
    if _G.MeridianAimbotVersion == "V3" and _G.MeridianStartV3Aimbot then
        _G.MeridianStartV3Aimbot()
    elseif _G.MeridianAimbotVersion == "V2" and _G.MeridianStartAimbotV2 then
        _G.MeridianStartAimbotV2()
    elseif _G.MeridianStartNormalAimbot then
        _G.MeridianStartNormalAimbot()
    end
end
if _G.MeridianRefreshAimbotVisual then _G.MeridianRefreshAimbotVisual() end
do end
end
function _G.MeridianRefreshAimbotVisual()
if _G.MeridianAimbotSetVisual then
_G.MeridianAimbotSetVisual(_G.MeridianNormalAimbotOn == true)
end
end
_G.MeridianNormalAimbotStart = _G.MeridianStartNormalAimbot
_G.MeridianNormalAimbotStop = _G.MeridianStopNormalAimbot

local MIRROR_TP_DROP_THRESHOLD = 3
local MIRROR_TP_DOWN_Y = -7.00
local mirrorTPPreviousY = {}
local mirrorTPLastTeleport = 0

local function mirrorTPAimbotActive()
return (_G.MeridianNormalAimbotOn == true)
end

local function mirrorTPTeleportDown()
local character = LP.Character
local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if not root or not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 then return end

local now = tick()
if now - (mirrorTPLastTeleport or 0) < 0.08 then return end
mirrorTPLastTeleport = now

local _, yaw = root.CFrame:ToEulerAnglesYXZ()
local y = (MIRROR_TP_DOWN_Y or -7) + (math.random() * 0.6 - 0.3)
root.CFrame = CFrame.new(root.Position.X, y, root.Position.Z) * CFrame.Angles(0, yaw, 0)
root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new((math.random() - 0.5) * 0.4, 0, (math.random() - 0.5) * 0.4)
end

_G.MeridianMirrorTPConn = _G.MeridianMirrorTPConn or nil
if _G.MeridianMirrorTPConn then pcall(function() _G.MeridianMirrorTPConn:Disconnect() end) end
_G.MeridianMirrorTPConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not mirrorTPDownEnabled or not mirrorTPAimbotActive() then
if next(mirrorTPPreviousY) then
table.clear(mirrorTPPreviousY)
end
return
end

for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
if plr ~= LP and plr.Character then
local root = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if root then
local currentY = root.Position.Y
local previousY = mirrorTPPreviousY[plr.UserId]
if previousY and previousY - currentY >= (MIRROR_TP_DROP_THRESHOLD or 3) then
pcall(mirrorTPTeleportDown)
table.clear(mirrorTPPreviousY)
return
end
mirrorTPPreviousY[plr.UserId] = currentY
end
end
end
end)

function _G.MeridianSetMirrorTPDown(enabled)
mirrorTPDownEnabled = enabled == true
if not mirrorTPDownEnabled then
table.clear(mirrorTPPreviousY)
end
if _G.MeridianMirrorTPDownSetVisual then
_G.MeridianMirrorTPDownSetVisual(mirrorTPDownEnabled)
end
end

_G.MeridianAntiDesync = _G.MeridianAntiDesync or {
    conn = nil,
    hittingCooldown = false,
    h = nil,
    hrp = nil
}

function _G.MeridianAntiDesyncGetBat()
    local char = LP.Character
    if not char then return nil end
    local tool = char:FindFirstChild("Bat")
    if tool then return tool end
    local bp = LP:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
    if bp then
        tool = bp:FindFirstChild("Bat")
        if tool then
            tool.Parent = char
            return tool
        end
    end
    return nil
end

function _G.MeridianAntiDesyncTrySwing()
    if _G.MeridianAntiDesync.hittingCooldown then return end
    _G.MeridianAntiDesync.hittingCooldown = true
    pcall(function()
        local bat = _G.MeridianAntiDesyncGetBat()
        if bat then
            bat:Activate()
            local ev = bat:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("RemoteEvent")
            if ev then ev:FireServer() end
        end
    end)
    task.delay(0.08, function()
        if _G.MeridianAntiDesync then
            _G.MeridianAntiDesync.hittingCooldown = false
        end
    end)
end

function _G.MeridianAntiDesyncGetClosestPlayer()
    local char = LP.Character
    if not char then return nil, math.huge end
    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hrp then return nil, math.huge end
    local closest, bestDist = nil, math.huge
    for _, player in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if player ~= LP and player.Character then
            local root = player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if root then
                local dist = (hrp.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
                if dist < bestDist then
                    bestDist = dist
                    closest = player
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return closest, bestDist
end

function _G.MeridianAntiDesyncSetupChar(char)
    task.wait(0.1)
    if not _G.MeridianAntiDesync then return end
    _G.MeridianAntiDesync.h   = char and char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5) or nil
    _G.MeridianAntiDesync.hrp = char and char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5) or nil
    if _G.MeridianAntiDesyncAimbotOn and not _G.MeridianAntiDesync.conn then
        _G.MeridianStartAntiDesyncAimbot()
    end
end

LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
    pcall(function() _G.MeridianAntiDesyncSetupChar(char) end)
end)
if LP.Character then
    task.spawn(function()
        pcall(function() _G.MeridianAntiDesyncSetupChar(LP.Character) end)
    end)
end

function _G.MeridianStartAntiDesyncAimbot()
    if _G.MeridianSafeModeTryStart and not _G.MeridianSafeModeTryStart() then
        _G.MeridianAntiDesyncAimbotOn = false
        if _G.MeridianAntiDesyncSetVisual then _G.MeridianAntiDesyncSetVisual(false) end
        return false
    end

    if _G.MeridianAntiDesync.conn then
        _G.MeridianAntiDesync.conn:Disconnect()
        _G.MeridianAntiDesync.conn = nil
    end

    _G.MeridianAntiDesyncAimbotOn = true

    local char = LP.Character
    if char then
        _G.MeridianAntiDesync.h   = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        _G.MeridianAntiDesync.hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    end

    -- Meridian Duels -- TP Bat needs to actually sit inside the target
    -- without being pushed out by physics. Disabling collision on the
    -- TARGET (not on ourselves) is what makes that work correctly, and
    -- this reuses the existing No Player Collision system so it plays
    -- nicely if the user also has that toggle on separately.
    _G.MeridianAntiDesyncOwnsNoCollide = not (_G.MeridianNoPlayerCollisionEnabled == true)
    if _G.MeridianAntiDesyncOwnsNoCollide and enableNoPlayerCollision then
        pcall(enableNoPlayerCollision)
    end

    _G.MeridianAntiDesync.conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not _G.MeridianAntiDesyncAimbotOn then
            _G.MeridianStopAntiDesyncAimbot()
            return
        end

        local h   = _G.MeridianAntiDesync.h
        local hrp = _G.MeridianAntiDesync.hrp
        if not h or not hrp then return end

        local _blHRP_ad = _MeridianGetBodyLockHRP()
        local target
        if _blHRP_ad and _blHRP_ad.Parent then
            target = Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(_blHRP_ad.Parent)
        end
        if not target then
            local _dist
            target, _dist = _G.MeridianAntiDesyncGetClosestPlayer()
        end
        _aimbotTargetPlr              = target
        _G.MeridianCurrentAimbotTarget = target
        _G.MeridianAntiDesyncBatTarget = target

        if target and target.Character then
            local tr = target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if tr then
                if sethiddenproperty then
                    sethiddenproperty(hrp, "PhysicsRepRootPart", tr)
                end
                local targetPos = tr.Position + Vector3.new(0, 0.9, 0)
                if (hrp.Position - targetPos).Magnitude > 8 then
                    hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(targetPos)
                end
                local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
                cam.CFrame = CFrame.new(cam.CFrame.Position, tr.Position)
                _G.MeridianAntiDesyncTrySwing()
            end
        end
    end)

    if _G.MeridianAntiDesyncSetVisual then
        _G.MeridianAntiDesyncSetVisual(true)
    end

    return true
end

function _G.MeridianStopAntiDesyncAimbot()
    _G.MeridianAntiDesyncAimbotOn = false
    if _G.MeridianAntiDesync and _G.MeridianAntiDesync.conn then
        _G.MeridianAntiDesync.conn:Disconnect()
        _G.MeridianAntiDesync.conn = nil
    end
    if _G.MeridianAntiDesync then
        _G.MeridianAntiDesync.hittingCooldown = false
    end
    -- Meridian Duels -- only turn collision back on if TP Bat was the
    -- one that enabled it - don't clobber the user's own separate
    -- No Player Collision toggle if they had it on already.
    if _G.MeridianAntiDesyncOwnsNoCollide and disableNoPlayerCollision then
        pcall(disableNoPlayerCollision)
        _G.MeridianAntiDesyncOwnsNoCollide = false
    end
    _G.MeridianAntiDesyncBatTarget = nil
    if _G.MeridianCurrentAimbotTarget == _aimbotTargetPlr then
        _G.MeridianCurrentAimbotTarget = nil
    end
    _aimbotTargetPlr = nil
    if _G.MeridianAntiDesyncSetVisual then
        _G.MeridianAntiDesyncSetVisual(false)
    end
end

-- ── TP BAT V2 (ported from 7up: every-frame CFrame snap with velocity
-- prediction, instead of only re-snapping past an 8-stud threshold) ──
function _G.MeridianStartAntiDesyncAimbotV2()
    if _G.MeridianSafeModeTryStart and not _G.MeridianSafeModeTryStart() then
        _G.MeridianAntiDesyncAimbotOn = false
        if _G.MeridianAntiDesyncSetVisual then _G.MeridianAntiDesyncSetVisual(false) end
        return false
    end

    if _G.MeridianAntiDesync.conn then
        _G.MeridianAntiDesync.conn:Disconnect()
        _G.MeridianAntiDesync.conn = nil
    end

    _G.MeridianAntiDesyncAimbotOn = true

    local char = LP.Character
    if char then
        _G.MeridianAntiDesync.h   = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        _G.MeridianAntiDesync.hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    end

    -- Same collision reuse as V1 - disable collision on the TARGET so we
    -- can sit inside them without physics pushing us back out.
    _G.MeridianAntiDesyncOwnsNoCollide = not (_G.MeridianNoPlayerCollisionEnabled == true)
    if _G.MeridianAntiDesyncOwnsNoCollide and enableNoPlayerCollision then
        pcall(enableNoPlayerCollision)
    end

    _G.MeridianAntiDesync.conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not _G.MeridianAntiDesyncAimbotOn then
            _G.MeridianStopAntiDesyncAimbotV2()
            return
        end

        local h   = _G.MeridianAntiDesync.h
        local hrp = _G.MeridianAntiDesync.hrp
        if not h or not hrp then return end

        local _blHRP_ad = _MeridianGetBodyLockHRP()
        local target
        if _blHRP_ad and _blHRP_ad.Parent then
            target = Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(_blHRP_ad.Parent)
        end
        if not target then
            target = _G.MeridianAntiDesyncGetClosestPlayer()
        end
        _aimbotTargetPlr               = target
        _G.MeridianCurrentAimbotTarget  = target
        _G.MeridianAntiDesyncBatTarget  = target

        if not (target and target.Character) then return end
        local tr = target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not tr then return end

        if sethiddenproperty then
            pcall(function() sethiddenproperty(hrp, "PhysicsRepRootPart", tr) end)
        end

        -- Predict the target's next position from its current velocity
        -- (ported from 7up) so we track slightly ahead of them instead
        -- of always being one frame behind - and re-snap CFrame every
        -- frame instead of only past an 8-stud threshold, which is what
        -- keeps V2 locked on through fast target movement/lag spikes
        -- that V1's threshold-based snap can lose.
        local tvel = tr.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        local predict = Vector3.new(tvel.X, 0, tvel.Z) * 0.05
        local targetPos = tr.Position + Vector3.new(0, 0.9, 0) + predict
        if targetPos.Y < -20 then
            targetPos = Vector3.new(targetPos.X, hrp.Position.Y, targetPos.Z)
        end

        local facing = Vector3.new(tr.CFrame.LookVector.X, 0, tr.CFrame.LookVector.Z)
        if facing.Magnitude < 0.01 then
            local flat = Vector3.new(tr.Position.X - hrp.Position.X, 0, tr.Position.Z - hrp.Position.Z)
            facing = flat.Magnitude > 0.01 and flat.Unit or Vector3.new(0, 0, -1)
        end

        pcall(function()
            hrp.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(targetPos, targetPos + facing.Unit)
            hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(tvel.X, 0, tvel.Z)
            hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end)

        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if cam then
            cam.CFrame = CFrame.new(cam.CFrame.Position, tr.Position)
        end

        _G.MeridianAntiDesyncTrySwing()
    end)

    if _G.MeridianAntiDesyncSetVisual then
        _G.MeridianAntiDesyncSetVisual(true)
    end

    return true
end

function _G.MeridianStopAntiDesyncAimbotV2()
    -- Stop cleanup is identical for both versions (same connection
    -- object, same collision/target state) - reuse V1's stop directly
    -- instead of duplicating it, so every existing caller of
    -- _G.MeridianStopAntiDesyncAimbot (including the auto-TP-restore
    -- wrapper installed later in this script) stays correct regardless
    -- of which version was actually running.
    _G.MeridianStopAntiDesyncAimbot()
end

function _G.MeridianToggleAntiDesyncAimbot()
    _G.MeridianTPBatVersion = _G.MeridianTPBatVersion or "V1"
    if _G.MeridianAntiDesyncAimbotOn then
        _G.MeridianStopAntiDesyncAimbot()
    else
        if _G.MeridianStopAllExclusiveMovement then _G.MeridianStopAllExclusiveMovement("tpBat") end
        if _G.MeridianTPBatVersion == "V2" then
            _G.MeridianStartAntiDesyncAimbotV2()
        else
            _G.MeridianStartAntiDesyncAimbot()
        end
    end
end

_G.__MeridianSetupNormalAutoSteal = function()
local getconnections_v3 = getconnections or get_signal_cons or getconnects or (syn and syn.get_signal_cons)
-- Executor-level prompt fire: fallback when getconnections is unavailable or returns no callbacks
local _fireProximityPrompt = (type(fireproximityprompt) == "function" and fireproximityprompt)
    or (type(fire_proximity_prompt) == "function" and fire_proximity_prompt)
    or nil
_G.MeridianGalaxyV3 = _G.MeridianGalaxyV3 or {
conn=nil, active=false, progress=0, paused=false, pauseTime=nil,
startTime=nil, progressConn=nil, dataCache={}, enabled=false,
StealRadius=63, StealDuration=1.3, TriggerRadius=10, FillCap=0.8
}
local GS = _G.MeridianGalaxyV3

local function getHRP()
local c=LP.Character
return c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
end

local function isMyPlot(plotName)
local plots=workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
if not plots then return false end
local plot=plots:FindFirstChild(plotName)
if not plot then return false end
local sign=plot:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
if sign then
local yb=sign:FindFirstChild("YourBase")
if yb and yb:IsA("BillboardGui") then return yb.Enabled==true end
end
return false
end

local function findPrompt()
local hrp=getHRP()
if not hrp then return nil,nil end
local plots=workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
if not plots then return nil,nil end
local bestPrompt,bestDist,bestSpawn=nil,math.huge,nil

for _,plot in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
if not plot:IsA("Model") then continue end
if isMyPlot(plot.Name) then continue end
local pods=plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
if not pods then continue end

for _,pod in ipairs(pods:GetChildren()) do
pcall(function()
local base=pod:FindFirstChild("Base")
local spawn=base and base:FindFirstChild("Spawn")
if not spawn then return end
local dist=(spawn.Position-hrp.Position).Magnitude
if dist>=bestDist or dist>GS.StealRadius then return end

local function tryPrompt(container)
for _,ch in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
if ch:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and ch.Enabled then
bestPrompt=ch
bestDist=dist
bestSpawn=spawn
return true
end
end
end

local att=spawn:FindFirstChild("PromptAttachment")
if not (att and tryPrompt(att)) then
for _,ch in ipairs(spawn:GetDescendants()) do
if ch:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and ch.Enabled then
bestPrompt=ch
bestDist=dist
bestSpawn=spawn
break
end
end
end
end)
end
end
return bestPrompt,bestSpawn
end

local function distToSpawn(spawnPart)
local hrp=getHRP()
if not hrp then return math.huge end
if not spawnPart or not spawnPart.Parent then
local _,ns=findPrompt()
return ns and (hrp.Position-ns.Position).Magnitude or math.huge
end
return (hrp.Position-spawnPart.Position).Magnitude
end

local function updateBar(p,state)
if _G.StealBar then
pcall(function()
_G.StealBar.SetProgress(p or 0)
if state then _G.StealBar.SetState(state) end
end)
end
end

-- Meridian Duels -- V2: fill 0% -> FillCap% (from the "Stop At %"
-- setting) ONCE, stop and hold there until the player is within
-- TriggerRadius, then finish FillCap% -> 100% and fire. This is
-- distinct from V3/HalfGrab's repeating 0.2s pulse.
local HOLD_TIME = 3

local function execute(prompt,spawnPart)
if GS.active then return end

if not GS.dataCache[prompt] then
local data={hold={},trigger={},ready=true}
if getconnections_v3 then
pcall(function()
for _,c in ipairs(getconnections_v3(prompt.PromptButtonHoldBegan)) do
if c.Function then table.insert(data.hold,c.Function) end
end
for _,c in ipairs(getconnections_v3(prompt.Triggered)) do
if c.Function then table.insert(data.trigger,c.Function) end
end
end)
end
GS.dataCache[prompt]=data
end

local data=GS.dataCache[prompt]
if not data.ready then return end

data.ready=false
GS.active=true
GS.paused=false

task.spawn(function()
local fillCap = math.clamp(tonumber(GS.FillCap) or 0.8, 0.05, 0.95)
local useDirectFire = (#data.hold == 0 and #data.trigger == 0) and _fireProximityPrompt ~= nil

if useDirectFire then
    pcall(function() _fireProximityPrompt(prompt) end)
    local t0 = tick()
    local dur = GS.StealDuration or 1.3
    while GS.enabled and tick()-t0 < dur do
        local p = math.min((tick()-t0)/dur, 1)
        GS.progress = p
        updateBar(p, "STEALING")
        task.wait(0.02)
    end
    if GS.enabled then updateBar(1,"STEALING"); task.wait(0.12) end
    GS.active=false; GS.progress=0; GS.paused=false
    data.ready=true
    updateBar(0, autoStealEnabled and "READY" or "IDLE")
    return
end

for _,fn in ipairs(data.hold) do
    task.spawn(function() pcall(fn) end)
end

-- Phase 1: fill 0 -> FillCap once
local t0=tick()
local dur=GS.StealDuration or 1.3
while GS.enabled and tick()-t0 < dur*fillCap do
    if not prompt.Parent or not prompt.Parent.Parent then
        GS.active=false; GS.progress=0; GS.paused=false
        data.ready=true
        updateBar(0, autoStealEnabled and "READY" or "IDLE")
        return
    end
    if distToSpawn(spawnPart) > GS.StealRadius then
        GS.active=false; GS.progress=0; GS.paused=false
        data.ready=true
        updateBar(0, autoStealEnabled and "READY" or "IDLE")
        return
    end
    local p=math.min((tick()-t0)/dur, fillCap)
    GS.progress=p
    updateBar(p,"STEALING")
    task.wait(0.02)
end

if not GS.enabled then
    GS.active=false; GS.progress=0; GS.paused=false
    data.ready=true
    updateBar(0, autoStealEnabled and "READY" or "IDLE")
    return
end

GS.progress=fillCap
updateBar(fillCap,"STEALING")

-- Phase 2: hold at FillCap until in TriggerRadius, or timeout
GS.paused=true
local holdT0=tick()
local completed=false
while GS.enabled and tick()-holdT0 < HOLD_TIME do
    local dist=distToSpawn(spawnPart)
    if dist<=(GS.TriggerRadius or 15) then
        completed=true
        break
    end
    task.wait(0.02)
end
GS.paused=false

if not GS.enabled or not completed then
    GS.active=false; GS.progress=0
    data.ready=true
    updateBar(0, autoStealEnabled and "READY" or "IDLE")
    return
end

-- Phase 3: finish FillCap -> 100%, then fire
local t1=tick()
local fillDur=dur*(1-fillCap)
while GS.enabled and tick()-t1 < fillDur do
    if not prompt.Parent or not prompt.Parent.Parent then
        GS.active=false; GS.progress=0
        data.ready=true
        updateBar(0, autoStealEnabled and "READY" or "IDLE")
        return
    end
    if distToSpawn(spawnPart) > GS.StealRadius then
        GS.active=false; GS.progress=0
        data.ready=true
        updateBar(0, autoStealEnabled and "READY" or "IDLE")
        return
    end
    local p=fillCap + math.min((tick()-t1)/fillDur, 1) * (1-fillCap)
    GS.progress=p
    updateBar(p,"STEALING")
    task.wait(0.02)
end

if not GS.enabled then
    GS.active=false; GS.progress=0
    data.ready=true
    updateBar(0, autoStealEnabled and "READY" or "IDLE")
    return
end

for _,fn in ipairs(data.trigger) do
    task.spawn(function() pcall(fn) end)
end
pcall(function() if _G.AutoCarrySpeed and _G.AutoCarrySpeed.WatchPickup then _G.AutoCarrySpeed.WatchPickup(1.25) end end)

updateBar(1, "STEALING")
task.wait(0.12)
data.ready=true
GS.active=false
GS.progress=0
updateBar(0, autoStealEnabled and "READY" or "IDLE")
end)
end

_G.MeridianNormalAutoStealSetRadius=function(v)
GS.StealRadius=tonumber(v) or GS.StealRadius or 63
end

_G.MeridianNormalAutoStealStart=function()
if GS.conn then GS.conn:Disconnect();GS.conn=nil end
GS.enabled=true
GS.active=false
GS.progress=0
GS.paused=false
GS.dataCache={}
GS.StealRadius=tonumber(autoStealRadius) or 63
GS.StealDuration=tonumber(_G.MeridianGalaxyV3 and _G.MeridianGalaxyV3.StealDuration) or 1.3
GS.FillCap=tonumber(_G.MeridianGalaxyV3 and _G.MeridianGalaxyV3.FillCap) or 0.8
GS.TriggerRadius=15
GS.conn=RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not GS.enabled or GS.active then return end
if not autoStealEnabled or selectedStealMode~="Auto Steal V3" then return end
local prompt,spawnPart=findPrompt()
if prompt then execute(prompt,spawnPart) end
end)
updateBar(0,"READY")
end

_G.MeridianNormalAutoStealStop=function()
GS.enabled=false
if GS.conn then GS.conn:Disconnect();GS.conn=nil end
if GS.progressConn then GS.progressConn:Disconnect();GS.progressConn=nil end
GS.active=false
GS.progress=0
GS.paused=false
GS.pauseTime=nil
GS.dataCache={}
updateBar(0,autoStealEnabled and "READY" or "IDLE")
end

_G.MeridianNormalAutoStealSync=function()
if autoStealEnabled and selectedStealMode=="Auto Steal V3" then
_G.MeridianNormalAutoStealStart()
else
_G.MeridianNormalAutoStealStop()
end
end
end
_G.__MeridianSetupNormalAutoSteal()

_G.__MeridianSetupV1AutoSteal = function()
_G.MeridianV1Steal = _G.MeridianV1Steal or {
enabled=false, conn=nil, dataCache={},
isStealing=false, stealStartTime=nil, stealEndTime=nil, stealCompleted=false, progressConn=nil,
StealRadius=55, HalfFireRange=15, HalfHoldMin=1.3, HalfHoldMax=2.6, HalfEntryDelay=0.3,
}
local V1 = _G.MeridianV1Steal

_G.MeridianVynxSteal = _G.MeridianVynxSteal or {
    AutoStealEnabled = false,
    StealRadius = 60,
    StealDuration = 1.4,
    StopTime = 0.35,
}
local VynxM = _G.MeridianVynxSteal
local _vynxAnimalCache = {}
local _vynxPromptCache = {}
local _vynxStealCache  = {}
local _vynxStealConn   = nil
local _vynxProgressConn = nil
local _vynxIsStealing  = false
local _vynxStealStart  = 0

local function _vynxIsMyPlot(plotName)
    local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
    if not plots then return false end
    local plot = plots:FindFirstChild(plotName)
    if not plot then return false end
    local sign = plot:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
    if sign then
        local yb = sign:FindFirstChild("YourBase")
        if yb and yb:IsA("BillboardGui") then return yb.Enabled == true end
    end
    return false
end

local function _vynxScanPlot(plot)
    if not plot or not plot:IsA("Model") then return end
    if _vynxIsMyPlot(plot.Name) then return end
    local podiums = plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
    if not podiums then return end
    for _, pod in ipairs(podiums:GetChildren()) do
        if pod:IsA("Model") and pod:FindFirstChild("Base") then
            local uid = plot.Name .. "_" .. pod.Name
            for _, ex in ipairs(_vynxAnimalCache) do if ex.uid == uid then return end end
            table.insert(_vynxAnimalCache, {
                name = pod.Name, plot = plot.Name, slot = pod.Name,
                worldPosition = pod:GetPivot().Position, uid = uid,
            })
        end
    end
end

local function _vynxFindPrompt(ad)
    if not ad then return nil end
    local cp = _vynxPromptCache[ad.uid]
    if cp and cp.Parent then return cp end
    local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
    if not plots then return nil end
    local plot = plots:FindFirstChild(ad.plot)
    if not plot then return nil end
    local pods = plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
    if not pods then return nil end
    local pod = pods:FindFirstChild(ad.slot)
    if not pod then return nil end
    local base = pod:FindFirstChild("Base")
    if not base then return nil end
    local spawn = base:FindFirstChild("Spawn")
    if not spawn then return nil end
    local att = spawn:FindFirstChild("PromptAttachment")
    local prompt = nil
    if att then
        for _, p in ipairs(att:GetChildren()) do
            if p:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then prompt = p; break end
        end
    end
    if not prompt then
        for _, obj in ipairs(spawn:GetDescendants()) do
            if obj:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then prompt = obj; break end
        end
    end
    if prompt then _vynxPromptCache[ad.uid] = prompt end
    return prompt
end

local function _vynxNearestAnimal()
    local hrp = LP.Character and (LP.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or LP.Character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso"))
    if not hrp then return nil, math.huge end
    local best, bestDist = nil, math.huge
    for _, ad in ipairs(_vynxAnimalCache) do
        if not _vynxIsMyPlot(ad.plot) and ad.worldPosition then
            local d = (hrp.Position - ad.worldPosition).Magnitude
            if d < bestDist then bestDist = d; best = ad end
        end
    end
    return best, bestDist
end

local function _vynxBuildCallbacks(prompt)
    if _vynxStealCache[prompt] then return end
    local data = {ready = true, holdCallbacks = {}, triggerCallbacks = {}}
    local getconn = getconnections or get_signal_cons or getconnects or (syn and syn.get_signal_cons)
    if getconn then
        local ok1, c1 = pcall(getconn, prompt.PromptButtonHoldBegan)
        if ok1 and type(c1) == "table" then
            for _, c in ipairs(c1) do
                if type(c.Function) == "function" then table.insert(data.holdCallbacks, c.Function) end
            end
        end
        local ok2, c2 = pcall(getconn, prompt.Triggered)
        if ok2 and type(c2) == "table" then
            for _, c in ipairs(c2) do
                if type(c.Function) == "function" then table.insert(data.triggerCallbacks, c.Function) end
            end
        end
    end
    if #data.holdCallbacks > 0 or #data.triggerCallbacks > 0 then
        _vynxStealCache[prompt] = data
    end
end

local function _vynxExecSteal(prompt)
    local data = _vynxStealCache[prompt]
    if not data or not data.ready then return false end
    data.ready = false
    _vynxIsStealing = true
    _vynxStealStart = tick()
    task.spawn(function()
        for _, fn in ipairs(data.holdCallbacks) do task.spawn(function() pcall(fn) end) end
        local duration = VynxM.StealDuration or 1.4
        local elapsed = 0
        -- Fill progress bar during steal
        if _G.StealBar then
            pcall(function() _G.StealBar.SetProgress(0) end)
            pcall(function() _G.StealBar.SetState("STEALING") end)
        end
        while elapsed < duration do
            local dt = task.wait()
            elapsed = elapsed + dt
            local p = math.clamp(elapsed / duration, 0, 1)
            if _G.StealBar then
                pcall(function() _G.StealBar.SetProgress(p) end)
            end
        end
        for _, fn in ipairs(data.triggerCallbacks) do task.spawn(function() pcall(fn) end) end
        task.wait(0.01)
        -- Reset bar
        if _G.StealBar then
            pcall(function() _G.StealBar.SetProgress(0) end)
            pcall(function() _G.StealBar.SetState(autoStealEnabled and "READY" or "IDLE") end)
        end
        _vynxIsStealing = false
        data.ready = true
    end)
    return true
end

function _G.MeridianStartVynxSteal()
    if _vynxStealConn then return end

    local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
    if plots then
        for _, p in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do _vynxScanPlot(p) end
        plots.ChildAdded:Connect(function(p) task.wait(0.5); _vynxScanPlot(p) end)
    end
    _vynxStealConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if selectedStealMode ~= "Vynx" or _vynxIsStealing then return end
        local target, dist = _vynxNearestAnimal()
        if not target then return end
        if dist > (VynxM.StealRadius or 60) then return end
        local prompt = _vynxPromptCache[target.uid]
        if not prompt or not prompt.Parent then
            prompt = _vynxFindPrompt(target)
        end
        if prompt then
            _vynxBuildCallbacks(prompt)
            _vynxExecSteal(prompt)
        end
    end)
end

function _G.MeridianStopVynxSteal()
    if _vynxStealConn then _vynxStealConn:Disconnect(); _vynxStealConn = nil end
    _vynxIsStealing = false
end

task.spawn(function()
    local plots = workspace:WaitForChild("Plots", 10)
    if plots then
        for _, plot in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
            _vynxScanPlot(plot)
        end
    end
end)

local function getHRP()
local c=LP.Character
return c and (c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or c:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or c:FindFirstChild("Torso"))
end

local function isMyPlot(plotName)
local plots=workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
if not plots then return false end
local plot=plots:FindFirstChild(plotName)
if not plot then return false end
local sign=plot:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
if sign then
local yb=sign:FindFirstChild("YourBase")
if yb and yb:IsA("BillboardGui") then return yb.Enabled==true end
end
return false
end

local function findNearestPrompt()
local root=getHRP()
if not root then return nil end
local plots=workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
if not plots then return nil end
local nearest,dist=nil,math.huge
for _,plot in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
if plot:IsA("Model") and not isMyPlot(plot.Name) then
local pods=plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
if pods then
for _,pod in ipairs(pods:GetChildren()) do
local base=pod:FindFirstChild("Base")
local sp=base and base:FindFirstChild("Spawn")
if sp then
local d=(sp.Position-root.Position).Magnitude
if d<=V1.StealRadius and d<dist then
local found=nil
local att=sp:FindFirstChild("PromptAttachment")
if att then
for _,pr in ipairs(att:GetChildren()) do
if pr:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and pr.ActionText and pr.ActionText:find("Steal") then found=pr end
end
end
if not found then
for _,pr in ipairs(sp:GetDescendants()) do
if pr:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and pr.ActionText and pr.ActionText:find("Steal") then found=pr end
end
end
if found then nearest,dist=found,d end
end
end
end
end
end
end
return nearest
end

local function promptDist(prompt)
local root=getHRP()
if not root then return math.huge end
local part=prompt.Parent
if part and part:IsA("Attachment") then part=part.Parent end
if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then return (part.Position-root.Position).Magnitude end
local ok,cf=pcall(function() return prompt.Parent and prompt.Parent.WorldPosition end)
if ok and cf then return (cf-root.Position).Magnitude end
return math.huge
end

local function updateBar(p,state)
if _G.StealBar then
pcall(function()
_G.StealBar.SetProgress(p or 0)
if state then _G.StealBar.SetState(state) end
end)
end
end

local _fireProximityPromptV1 = (type(fireproximityprompt) == "function" and fireproximityprompt)
    or (type(fire_proximity_prompt) == "function" and fire_proximity_prompt)
    or nil
local function executeSteal(prompt)
if V1.isStealing then return end
local getconnections_v1 = getconnections or get_signal_cons or getconnects or (syn and syn.get_signal_cons)
if not V1.dataCache[prompt] then
V1.dataCache[prompt]={hold={},trigger={},ready=true}
if getconnections_v1 then
pcall(function()
for _,c in ipairs(getconnections_v1(prompt.PromptButtonHoldBegan)) do
if c.Function then table.insert(V1.dataCache[prompt].hold,c.Function) end
end
for _,c in ipairs(getconnections_v1(prompt.Triggered)) do
if c.Function then table.insert(V1.dataCache[prompt].trigger,c.Function) end
end
end)
end
end
local data=V1.dataCache[prompt]
if not data.ready then return end
data.ready=false
-- Direct fire fallback: if no callbacks collected and fireproximityprompt is available, use it
if #data.hold == 0 and #data.trigger == 0 and _fireProximityPromptV1 then
    V1.isStealing=true
    V1.stealStartTime=tick()
    if V1.progressConn then V1.progressConn:Disconnect() end
    V1.progressConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not V1.isStealing then
            if V1.progressConn then V1.progressConn:Disconnect(); V1.progressConn=nil end
            return
        end
        local prog = math.clamp((tick()-V1.stealStartTime)/V1.HalfHoldMin,0,1)
        updateBar(prog,"STEALING")
    end)
    task.spawn(function()
        pcall(function() _fireProximityPromptV1(prompt) end)
        local holdStart=tick()
        while tick()-holdStart < V1.HalfHoldMin do task.wait() end
        if V1.progressConn then V1.progressConn:Disconnect(); V1.progressConn=nil end
        updateBar(1,"STEALING")
        task.wait(0.12)
        data.ready=true
        V1.isStealing=false
        V1.stealCompleted=true
        V1.stealEndTime=tick()
        updateBar(0,autoStealEnabled and "READY" or "IDLE")
    end)
    return
end
V1.isStealing=true
V1.stealCompleted=false
V1.stealStartTime=tick()
V1.stealEndTime=nil
-- Vynx-style progressConn: smooth real-time bar fill
if V1.progressConn then V1.progressConn:Disconnect() end
V1.progressConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
    if not V1.isStealing then
        V1.progressConn:Disconnect()
        V1.progressConn = nil
        return
    end
    local prog = math.clamp((tick() - V1.stealStartTime) / V1.HalfHoldMin, 0, 1)
    updateBar(prog, "STEALING")
end)

task.spawn(function()
for _,fn in ipairs(data.hold) do task.spawn(function() pcall(fn) end) end
local holdStart=tick()
while tick()-holdStart<V1.HalfHoldMin do
if not V1.enabled or selectedStealMode~="V1" then break end
task.wait()
end
if not V1.enabled or selectedStealMode~="V1" then
data.ready=true
V1.isStealing=false
if V1.progressConn then V1.progressConn:Disconnect(); V1.progressConn=nil end
updateBar(0,autoStealEnabled and "READY" or "IDLE")
return
end
local inRange=promptDist(prompt)<=V1.HalfFireRange
local fired=false
while true do
local el=tick()-V1.stealStartTime
if el>V1.HalfHoldMax or not prompt.Parent then break end
if not V1.enabled or selectedStealMode~="V1" then break end
if promptDist(prompt)<=V1.HalfFireRange then
if not inRange then task.wait(V1.HalfEntryDelay) end
if V1.progressConn then V1.progressConn:Disconnect(); V1.progressConn=nil end
updateBar(1,"STEALING")
for _,fn in ipairs(data.trigger) do task.spawn(function() pcall(fn) end) end
fired=true
break
end
task.wait()
end
V1.stealCompleted=true
V1.stealEndTime=tick()
if V1.progressConn then V1.progressConn:Disconnect(); V1.progressConn=nil end
task.wait(fired and 0.12 or 0.05)
data.ready=true
V1.isStealing=false
V1.stealCompleted=false
updateBar(0,autoStealEnabled and "READY" or "IDLE")
end)
end

_G.MeridianV1AutoStealSetRadius=function(v)
V1.StealRadius=tonumber(v) or V1.StealRadius or 55
end

_G.MeridianV1AutoStealStart=function()
if V1.conn then V1.conn:Disconnect();V1.conn=nil end
V1.enabled=true
V1.isStealing=false
V1.stealCompleted=false
V1.dataCache={}
V1.StealRadius=tonumber(autoStealRadius) or 55
V1.conn=RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not V1.enabled or V1.isStealing then return end
if not autoStealEnabled or selectedStealMode~="V1" then return end
local p=findNearestPrompt()
if p then executeSteal(p) end
end)
updateBar(0,"READY")
end

_G.MeridianV1AutoStealStop=function()
V1.enabled=false
if V1.conn then V1.conn:Disconnect();V1.conn=nil end
if V1.progressConn then V1.progressConn:Disconnect();V1.progressConn=nil end
V1.isStealing=false
V1.stealCompleted=false
V1.dataCache={}
updateBar(0,autoStealEnabled and "READY" or "IDLE")
end

_G.MeridianV1AutoStealSync=function()
if autoStealEnabled and selectedStealMode=="V1" then
_G.MeridianV1AutoStealStart()
else
_G.MeridianV1AutoStealStop()
end
end
end
_G.__MeridianSetupV1AutoSteal()
_G.__MeridianSetupSemiAutoSteal = function()
local A = _G.MeridianSemiSteal
if not A then
A = {
enabled = false,
conn = nil,
dataCache = {},
isStealing = false,
stealStartTime = nil,
radius = 62,
holdMin = 1.3,
holdMax = 2.6,
fireRange = 15,
entryDelay = 0.3,
}
_G.MeridianSemiSteal = A
end
local function rootPart()
local char = LP.Character
return char and (char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or char:FindFirstChild("Torso"))
end
local function barSet(pct, status)
pct = math.clamp(pct or 0, 0, 1)
if _G.StealBar then
    _G.StealBar.SetProgress(pct)
    _G.StealBar.SetState(status or "STEALING")
end
end
local function barReset()
if _G.StealBar then
    _G.StealBar.SetProgress(0)
    _G.StealBar.SetState(autoStealEnabled and "READY" or "IDLE")
end
end
local function isMyPlotByName(plotName)
local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
local plot = plots and plots:FindFirstChild(plotName)
if not plot then return false end
local sign = plot:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
local yourBase = sign and sign:FindFirstChild("YourBase")
return yourBase and yourBase:IsA("BillboardGui") and yourBase.Enabled == true or false
end
local function findNearestPrompt()
local root = rootPart()
local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
if not root or not plots then return nil end

local detectRadius = tonumber(A.radius) or 62
local nearest, distance = nil, math.huge
for _, plot in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
if plot:IsA("Model") and not isMyPlotByName(plot.Name) then
local podiums = plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
if podiums then
for _, podium in ipairs(podiums:GetChildren()) do
local base = podium:FindFirstChild("Base")
local spawn = base and base:FindFirstChild("Spawn")
if spawn then
local d = (spawn.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
if d <= detectRadius and d < distance then
local found
local attachment = spawn:FindFirstChild("PromptAttachment")
local candidates = attachment and attachment:GetChildren() or spawn:GetDescendants()
for _, prompt in ipairs(candidates) do
if prompt:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and prompt.ActionText and prompt.ActionText:find("Steal") then
found = prompt
break
end
end
if found then nearest, distance = found, d end
end
end
end
end
end
end
return nearest
end
local function promptDistance(prompt)
local root = rootPart()
if not root then return math.huge end
local part = prompt.Parent
if part and part:IsA("Attachment") then part = part.Parent end
if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then return (part.Position - root.Position).Magnitude end
local ok, position = pcall(function() return prompt.Parent and prompt.Parent.WorldPosition end)
return ok and position and (position - root.Position).Magnitude or math.huge
end
local function executeSteal(prompt)
if A.isStealing then return end
if not A.dataCache[prompt] then
A.dataCache[prompt] = {hold = {}, trigger = {}, ready = true}
if getconnections then
pcall(function()
for _, c in ipairs(getconnections(prompt.PromptButtonHoldBegan)) do
if c.Function then table.insert(A.dataCache[prompt].hold, c.Function) end
end
for _, c in ipairs(getconnections(prompt.Triggered)) do
if c.Function then table.insert(A.dataCache[prompt].trigger, c.Function) end
end
end)
end
end
local data = A.dataCache[prompt]
if not data.ready then return end
data.ready, A.isStealing, A.stealStartTime = false, true, tick()

task.spawn(function()
for _, fn in ipairs(data.hold) do task.spawn(function() pcall(fn) end) end

local holdStart = A.stealStartTime
while A.enabled and selectedStealMode == "Semi" and tick() - holdStart < A.holdMin do
local raw = (tick() - holdStart) / A.holdMin
local wobble = math.sin((tick() - holdStart) * 14) * 0.035 * (1 - raw)
barSet(math.clamp(raw + wobble, 0, 0.97), "STEALING")
task.wait()
end
if not A.enabled or selectedStealMode ~= "Semi" then
data.ready = true
A.isStealing = false
barReset()
return
end
local inRange = promptDistance(prompt) <= A.fireRange
local fired = false
while A.enabled and selectedStealMode == "Semi" do
local elapsed = tick() - A.stealStartTime
if elapsed > A.holdMax or not prompt.Parent then break end
if promptDistance(prompt) <= A.fireRange then
if not inRange then task.wait(A.entryDelay) end
if A.enabled and selectedStealMode == "Semi" then
for _, fn in ipairs(data.trigger) do task.spawn(function() pcall(fn) end) end
pcall(function() if _G.AutoCarrySpeed and _G.AutoCarrySpeed.WatchPickup then _G.AutoCarrySpeed.WatchPickup(1.25) end end)
fired = true
end
break
end
barSet(0.97, "STEALING")
task.wait()
end
if fired then
barSet(1, "SUCCESS")
else
barSet(0, "FAILED")
end
task.wait(fired and 0.12 or 0.05)
data.ready, A.isStealing = true, false
barReset()
end)
end
_G.MeridianSemiAutoStealSetRadius = function(v)
local n = tonumber(v)
if n then A.radius = n end
end
_G.MeridianSemiAutoStealStop = function()
A.enabled = false
if A.conn then A.conn:Disconnect(); A.conn = nil end
A.isStealing = false
barReset()
end
_G.MeridianSemiAutoStealStart = function()
A.radius = tonumber(autoStealRadius) or A.radius or 62
A.enabled = true
if A.conn then A.conn:Disconnect(); A.conn = nil end
A.conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not A.enabled then return end
if selectedStealMode ~= "Semi" then _G.MeridianSemiAutoStealStop(); return end

if selectedStealMode == "Semi" then
A.radius = tonumber(_G.MeridianStealRadii and _G.MeridianStealRadii.Semi) or A.radius or 62
end
if A.isStealing then return end
local prompt = findNearestPrompt()
if prompt then executeSteal(prompt) end
end)
end
_G.MeridianSemiAutoStealSync = function()
if selectedStealMode == "Semi" and autoStealEnabled then
_G.MeridianSemiAutoStealStart()
else
_G.MeridianSemiAutoStealStop()
end
end
end
_G.__MeridianSetupSemiAutoSteal()
_G.MeridianAutoStealSync = function()
if not autoStealEnabled then
if _G.MeridianNormalAutoStealStop then _G.MeridianNormalAutoStealStop() end
if _G.MeridianSemiAutoStealStop then _G.MeridianSemiAutoStealStop() end
if _G.MeridianV1AutoStealStop then _G.MeridianV1AutoStealStop() end
if _G.MeridianStopVynxSteal then _G.MeridianStopVynxSteal() end
if _G.MeridianStopHalfGrab then _G.MeridianStopHalfGrab() end
return
end
if selectedStealMode == "Auto Steal V3" then
if _G.MeridianSemiAutoStealStop then _G.MeridianSemiAutoStealStop() end
if _G.MeridianV1AutoStealStop then _G.MeridianV1AutoStealStop() end
if _G.MeridianStopVynxSteal then _G.MeridianStopVynxSteal() end
if _G.MeridianStopHalfGrab then _G.MeridianStopHalfGrab() end
if _G.MeridianNormalAutoStealSync then _G.MeridianNormalAutoStealSync() end
elseif selectedStealMode == "Semi" then
if _G.MeridianNormalAutoStealStop then _G.MeridianNormalAutoStealStop() end
if _G.MeridianV1AutoStealStop then _G.MeridianV1AutoStealStop() end
if _G.MeridianStopVynxSteal then _G.MeridianStopVynxSteal() end
if _G.MeridianStopHalfGrab then _G.MeridianStopHalfGrab() end
if _G.MeridianSemiAutoStealSync then _G.MeridianSemiAutoStealSync() end
elseif selectedStealMode == "V1" then
if _G.MeridianNormalAutoStealStop then _G.MeridianNormalAutoStealStop() end
if _G.MeridianSemiAutoStealStop then _G.MeridianSemiAutoStealStop() end
if _G.MeridianStopVynxSteal then _G.MeridianStopVynxSteal() end
if _G.MeridianStopHalfGrab then _G.MeridianStopHalfGrab() end
if _G.MeridianV1AutoStealSync then _G.MeridianV1AutoStealSync() end
elseif selectedStealMode == "Vynx" then
if _G.MeridianNormalAutoStealStop then _G.MeridianNormalAutoStealStop() end
if _G.MeridianSemiAutoStealStop then _G.MeridianSemiAutoStealStop() end
if _G.MeridianV1AutoStealStop then _G.MeridianV1AutoStealStop() end
if _G.MeridianStopHalfGrab then _G.MeridianStopHalfGrab() end
if _G.MeridianStartVynxSteal then _G.MeridianStartVynxSteal() end
elseif selectedStealMode == "HalfGrab" then
if _G.MeridianNormalAutoStealStop then _G.MeridianNormalAutoStealStop() end
if _G.MeridianSemiAutoStealStop then _G.MeridianSemiAutoStealStop() end
if _G.MeridianV1AutoStealStop then _G.MeridianV1AutoStealStop() end
if _G.MeridianStopVynxSteal then _G.MeridianStopVynxSteal() end
if _G.MeridianStartHalfGrab then _G.MeridianStartHalfGrab() end
end
end
task.spawn(function()
while task.wait(30) do
do end
end
end)
local lastMoveDir = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)

-- Speed mode: manual only (Auto Carry Speed feature removed).
local refreshSpeedModeRows = nil

local function getCurrentSpeedValue()
    if currentSpeedMode == "Lagger Carry" then return LAGGER_CARRY_SPEED
    elseif currentSpeedMode == "Lagger"   then return LAGGER_SPEED
    elseif currentSpeedMode == "Carry"    then return CS
    else                                       return NS end
end

local function setSpeedMode(mode)
    if mode ~= "Normal" and mode ~= "Carry" and mode ~= "Lagger" and mode ~= "Lagger Carry" then
        mode = "Normal"
    end
    currentSpeedMode = mode
    if refreshSpeedModeRows then refreshSpeedModeRows() end
end
local function toggleCarryMode()
    if currentSpeedMode == "Lagger" or currentSpeedMode == "Lagger Carry" then
        setSpeedMode("Carry")
    elseif currentSpeedMode == "Carry" then setSpeedMode("Normal")
    else setSpeedMode("Carry") end
end
local function toggleLaggerMode()
    if currentSpeedMode == "Lagger" then setSpeedMode("Normal")
    elseif currentSpeedMode == "Lagger Carry" then setSpeedMode("Lagger")
    else setSpeedMode("Lagger") end
end
local function toggleLaggerCarryMode()
    if currentSpeedMode == "Lagger Carry" then setSpeedMode("Normal")
    else setSpeedMode("Lagger Carry") end
end

_G.MeridianAutoPathState = _G.MeridianAutoPathState or {leftConn=nil,rightConn=nil,leftPhase=1,rightPhase=1}
_G.MeridianAutoPathPoints = _G.MeridianAutoPathPoints or {
L1=Vector3.new(-476.48,-6.28,92.73), L2=Vector3.new(-483.12,-4.95,94.80), LFace=Vector3.new(-482.25,-4.96,92.09),
R1=Vector3.new(-476.16,-6.52,25.62), R2=Vector3.new(-483.06,-5.03,25.48), RFace=Vector3.new(-482.06,-6.93,35.47),
}
function _G.MeridianAutoPathSpeed()
if currentSpeedMode == "Lagger" or currentSpeedMode == "Lagger Carry" then
return LAGGER_SPEED
end
return NS
end
function _G.MeridianStopAutoLeft()
local S=_G.MeridianAutoPathState
if S.leftConn then S.leftConn:Disconnect(); S.leftConn=nil end
S.leftPhase=1
local char=LP.Character
local hum=char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
local hrp=char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if hum then hum:Move(Vector3.zero,false) end
if hrp then hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity=Vector3.new(0,hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y,0) end
end
function _G.MeridianStopAutoRight()
local S=_G.MeridianAutoPathState
if S.rightConn then S.rightConn:Disconnect(); S.rightConn=nil end
S.rightPhase=1
local char=LP.Character
local hum=char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
local hrp=char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if hum then hum:Move(Vector3.zero,false) end
if hrp then hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity=Vector3.new(0,hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y,0) end
end
function _G.MeridianSetAutoLeft(on, skipSave)
if on and _G.MeridianSafeModeTryStart and not _G.MeridianSafeModeTryStart() then
autoLeftEnabled = false
if _G.MeridianSetAutoLeftVisual then _G.MeridianSetAutoLeftVisual(false) end
if not skipSave then do end end
return false
end
autoLeftEnabled = on and true or false
if _G.MeridianSetAutoLeftVisual then _G.MeridianSetAutoLeftVisual(autoLeftEnabled) end
if autoLeftEnabled then
autoRightEnabled=false
if _G.MeridianSetAutoRightVisual then _G.MeridianSetAutoRightVisual(false) end
if _G.MeridianStopAutoRight then _G.MeridianStopAutoRight() end
if _G.MeridianStartAutoLeft then _G.MeridianStartAutoLeft() end
else
if _G.MeridianStopAutoLeft then _G.MeridianStopAutoLeft() end
end
if not skipSave then do end end
end
function _G.MeridianSetAutoRight(on, skipSave)
if on and _G.MeridianSafeModeTryStart and not _G.MeridianSafeModeTryStart() then
autoRightEnabled = false
if _G.MeridianSetAutoRightVisual then _G.MeridianSetAutoRightVisual(false) end
if not skipSave then do end end
return false
end
autoRightEnabled = on and true or false
if _G.MeridianSetAutoRightVisual then _G.MeridianSetAutoRightVisual(autoRightEnabled) end
if autoRightEnabled then
autoLeftEnabled=false
if _G.MeridianSetAutoLeftVisual then _G.MeridianSetAutoLeftVisual(false) end
if _G.MeridianStopAutoLeft then _G.MeridianStopAutoLeft() end
if _G.MeridianStartAutoRight then _G.MeridianStartAutoRight() end
else
if _G.MeridianStopAutoRight then _G.MeridianStopAutoRight() end
end
if not skipSave then do end end
end

-- Returns the correct auto-left/right travel speed based on current speed mode.
-- Lagger ON (Lagger or Lagger Carry) -> LAGGER_SPEED (always)
-- Normal/Carry -> NS (Normal Speed)
local function getAutoLRSpeed()
    if currentSpeedMode == "Lagger" or currentSpeedMode == "Lagger Carry" then
        return tonumber(LAGGER_SPEED) or 29
    end
    return tonumber(NS) or 60
end

-- Returns the carry-phase speed for the Full path, also capped by lagger when active.
local function getAutoLRCarrySpeed()
    if currentSpeedMode == "Lagger" or currentSpeedMode == "Lagger Carry" then
        return tonumber(LAGGER_SPEED) or 29
    end
    return tonumber(_G.MeridianAutoMoveCarrySpeed) or tonumber(NS) or 60
end

_G.MeridianZombieMove = _G.MeridianZombieMove or {
    leftConn = nil,
    rightConn = nil,
    leftPhase = 1,
    rightPhase = 1,
    attachment = nil,
    linearVelocity = nil,
}

_G.MeridianAutoMoveSpeed = tonumber(_G.MeridianAutoMoveSpeed) or 60
_G.MeridianAutoMoveCarrySpeed = tonumber(_G.MeridianAutoMoveCarrySpeed) or 30
_G.MeridianDuelMode = (_G.MeridianDuelMode == "full") and "full" or "half"

_G.MeridianZombieMovePoints = _G.MeridianZombieMovePoints or {
    L1 = Vector3.new(-476.48, -6.28, 92.73),
    L2 = Vector3.new(-483.12, -4.95, 94.80),
    LFace = Vector3.new(-482.25, -4.96, 92.09),

    R1 = Vector3.new(-476.16, -6.52, 25.62),
    R2 = Vector3.new(-483.06, -5.03, 25.48),
    RFace = Vector3.new(-482.06, -6.93, 35.47),

    FullLeft = {
        Vector3.new(-474.0, -7.3, 90.2),

        Vector3.new(-473.6, -7.3, 93.5),
        Vector3.new(-473.0, -7.3, 27.6),

    },

    }
