function _G.MeridianZombieEnsureLinearVelocity(hrp)
    local S = _G.MeridianZombieMove

    if S.linearVelocity and S.linearVelocity.Parent and S.attachment and S.attachment.Parent == hrp then
        return S.linearVelocity
    end

    if S.linearVelocity then
        pcall(function() S.linearVelocity:Destroy() end)
    end
    if S.attachment then
        pcall(function() S.attachment:Destroy() end)
    end

    local att = Instance.new("Attachment")
    att.Name = "MeridianZombieMoveAttachment"
    att.Parent = hrp

    local lv = Instance.new("LinearVelocity")
    lv.Name = "MeridianZombieMoveVelocity"
    lv.Attachment0 = att
    lv.RelativeTo = Enum.ActuatorRelativeTo.World
    lv.ForceLimitMode = Enum.ForceLimitMode.PerAxis
    lv.MaxAxesForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, 0, math.huge)
    lv.VectorVelocity = Vector3.zero
    lv.Parent = att

    S.attachment = att
    S.linearVelocity = lv
    return lv
end

function _G.MeridianZombieStopVelocity()
    local S = _G.MeridianZombieMove
    if S.linearVelocity and S.linearVelocity.Parent then
        S.linearVelocity.VectorVelocity = Vector3.zero
        S.linearVelocity.MaxAxesForce = Vector3.zero
        S.linearVelocity.Enabled = false
    end
end

function _G.MeridianStopAutoLeft()
    local S = _G.MeridianZombieMove
    if S.leftConn then
        S.leftConn:Disconnect()
        S.leftConn = nil
    end
    S.leftPhase = 1

    local char = LP.Character
    local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum then hum:Move(Vector3.zero, false) end
    _G.MeridianZombieStopVelocity()

    if _G.MeridianSetAutoLeftVisual then
        pcall(function() _G.MeridianSetAutoLeftVisual(false) end)
    end
end

function _G.MeridianStopAutoRight()
    local S = _G.MeridianZombieMove
    if S.rightConn then
        S.rightConn:Disconnect()
        S.rightConn = nil
    end
    S.rightPhase = 1

    local char = LP.Character
    local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum then hum:Move(Vector3.zero, false) end
    _G.MeridianZombieStopVelocity()

    if _G.MeridianSetAutoRightVisual then
        pcall(function() _G.MeridianSetAutoRightVisual(false) end)
    end
end

function _G.MeridianZombieMoveIsFull()
    return (_G.MeridianDuelMode == "full")
        or (_G.MeridianDuelMode == "Full")
        or (typeof(State) == "table" and State.duelMode == "full")
end

function _G.MeridianStartAutoLeft()
    if autoRightEnabled then
        autoRightEnabled = false
        _G.MeridianStopAutoRight()
    end

    local S = _G.MeridianZombieMove
    if S.leftConn then S.leftConn:Disconnect() end
    S.leftPhase = 1

    S.leftConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not autoLeftEnabled then return end
        if dropBrainrotActive then return end

        local char = LP.Character
        local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not hrp or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then return end

        local st = hum:GetState()
        if hum.PlatformStand
            or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
            or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
            or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown then
            hum:Move(Vector3.zero, false)
            _G.MeridianZombieStopVelocity()
            return
        end

        local lv = _G.MeridianZombieEnsureLinearVelocity(hrp)
        lv.Enabled = true
        lv.MaxAxesForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, 0, math.huge)
        local P = _G.MeridianZombieMovePoints
        local spd = getAutoLRSpeed()

        if _G.MeridianZombieMoveIsFull() then
            local points = P.FullLeft
            if S.leftPhase > #points then
                _G.MeridianZombieStopVelocity()
                local prev = points[#points - 1] or points[#points]
                hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(hrp.Position, Vector3.new(prev.X, hrp.Position.Y, prev.Z))
                hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y, 0)
                hum:Move(Vector3.zero, false)
                pcall(function() hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running) end)
                autoLeftEnabled = false
                if S.leftConn then S.leftConn:Disconnect(); S.leftConn = nil end
                S.leftPhase = 1
                if _G.MeridianSetAutoLeftVisual then _G.MeridianSetAutoLeftVisual(false) end
                return
            end

            local tgt = points[S.leftPhase]
            local flat = Vector3.new(tgt.X, hrp.Position.Y, tgt.Z)
            if (flat - hrp.Position).Magnitude < 1 then
                S.leftPhase += 1
                return
            end

            local dir = (flat - hrp.Position).Unit
            local fspd = (S.leftPhase <= 2)
                and getAutoLRSpeed()
                or getAutoLRCarrySpeed()

            if not hum.AutoRotate then hum.AutoRotate = true end
            lv.VectorVelocity = Vector3.new(dir.X * fspd, 0, dir.Z * fspd)
            pcall(function() hrp.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(hrp.Position, hrp.Position + Vector3.new(dir.X, 0, dir.Z)) end)
        else
            if S.leftPhase == 1 then
                local tgt = Vector3.new(P.L1.X, hrp.Position.Y, P.L1.Z)
                if (tgt - hrp.Position).Magnitude < 1 then
                    S.leftPhase = 2
                    return
                end

                local d = P.L1 - hrp.Position
                local mv = Vector3.new(d.X, 0, d.Z).Unit
                if not hum.AutoRotate then hum.AutoRotate = true end
                lv.VectorVelocity = Vector3.new(mv.X * spd, 0, mv.Z * spd)
                pcall(function() hrp.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(hrp.Position, hrp.Position + mv) end)
            else
                local tgt = Vector3.new(P.L2.X, hrp.Position.Y, P.L2.Z)
                if (tgt - hrp.Position).Magnitude < 1 then
                    hum:Move(Vector3.zero, false)
                    _G.MeridianZombieStopVelocity()
                    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y, 0)
                    hum:Move(Vector3.zero, false)
                    pcall(function() hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running) end)
                    autoLeftEnabled = false
                    if S.leftConn then S.leftConn:Disconnect(); S.leftConn = nil end
                    S.leftPhase = 1
                    if _G.MeridianSetAutoLeftVisual then _G.MeridianSetAutoLeftVisual(false) end
                    if P.LFace and (P.LFace - hrp.Position).Magnitude > 0.01 then
                        hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(hrp.Position, Vector3.new(P.LFace.X, hrp.Position.Y, P.LFace.Z))
                    end
                    return
                end

                local d = P.L2 - hrp.Position
                local mv = Vector3.new(d.X, 0, d.Z).Unit
                if not hum.AutoRotate then hum.AutoRotate = true end
                lv.VectorVelocity = Vector3.new(mv.X * spd, 0, mv.Z * spd)
                pcall(function() hrp.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(hrp.Position, hrp.Position + mv) end)
            end
        end
    end)
end

function _G.MeridianStartAutoRight()
    if autoLeftEnabled then
        autoLeftEnabled = false
        _G.MeridianStopAutoLeft()
    end

    local S = _G.MeridianZombieMove
    if S.rightConn then S.rightConn:Disconnect() end
    S.rightPhase = 1

    S.rightConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not autoRightEnabled then return end
        if dropBrainrotActive then return end

        local char = LP.Character
        local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not hrp or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then return end

        local st = hum:GetState()
        if hum.PlatformStand
            or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
            or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
            or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown then
            hum:Move(Vector3.zero, false)
            _G.MeridianZombieStopVelocity()
            return
        end

        local lv = _G.MeridianZombieEnsureLinearVelocity(hrp)
        lv.Enabled = true
        lv.MaxAxesForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, 0, math.huge)
        local P = _G.MeridianZombieMovePoints
        local spd = getAutoLRSpeed()

        if _G.MeridianZombieMoveIsFull() then
            local points = P.FullRight
            if S.rightPhase > #points then
                _G.MeridianZombieStopVelocity()
                local prev = points[#points - 1] or points[#points]
                hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(hrp.Position, Vector3.new(prev.X, hrp.Position.Y, prev.Z))
                hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y, 0)
                hum:Move(Vector3.zero, false)
                pcall(function() hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running) end)
                autoRightEnabled = false
                if S.rightConn then S.rightConn:Disconnect(); S.rightConn = nil end
                S.rightPhase = 1
                if _G.MeridianSetAutoRightVisual then _G.MeridianSetAutoRightVisual(false) end
                return
            end

            local tgt = points[S.rightPhase]
            local flat = Vector3.new(tgt.X, hrp.Position.Y, tgt.Z)
            if (flat - hrp.Position).Magnitude < 1 then
                S.rightPhase += 1
                return
            end

            local dir = (flat - hrp.Position).Unit
            local fspd = (S.rightPhase <= 2)
                and getAutoLRSpeed()
                or getAutoLRCarrySpeed()

            if not hum.AutoRotate then hum.AutoRotate = true end
            lv.VectorVelocity = Vector3.new(dir.X * fspd, 0, dir.Z * fspd)
            pcall(function() hrp.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(hrp.Position, hrp.Position + Vector3.new(dir.X, 0, dir.Z)) end)
        else
            if S.rightPhase == 1 then
                local tgt = Vector3.new(P.R1.X, hrp.Position.Y, P.R1.Z)
                if (tgt - hrp.Position).Magnitude < 1 then
                    S.rightPhase = 2
                    return
                end

                local d = P.R1 - hrp.Position
                local mv = Vector3.new(d.X, 0, d.Z).Unit
                if not hum.AutoRotate then hum.AutoRotate = true end
                lv.VectorVelocity = Vector3.new(mv.X * spd, 0, mv.Z * spd)
                pcall(function() hrp.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(hrp.Position, hrp.Position + mv) end)
            else
                local tgt = Vector3.new(P.R2.X, hrp.Position.Y, P.R2.Z)
                if (tgt - hrp.Position).Magnitude < 1 then
                    hum:Move(Vector3.zero, false)
                    _G.MeridianZombieStopVelocity()
                    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y, 0)
                    hum:Move(Vector3.zero, false)
                    pcall(function() hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running) end)
                    autoRightEnabled = false
                    if S.rightConn then S.rightConn:Disconnect(); S.rightConn = nil end
                    S.rightPhase = 1
                    if _G.MeridianSetAutoRightVisual then _G.MeridianSetAutoRightVisual(false) end
                    if P.RFace and (P.RFace - hrp.Position).Magnitude > 0.01 then
                        hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(hrp.Position, Vector3.new(P.RFace.X, hrp.Position.Y, P.RFace.Z))
                    end
                    return
                end

                local d = P.R2 - hrp.Position
                local mv = Vector3.new(d.X, 0, d.Z).Unit
                if not hum.AutoRotate then hum.AutoRotate = true end
                lv.VectorVelocity = Vector3.new(mv.X * spd, 0, mv.Z * spd)
                pcall(function() hrp.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(hrp.Position, hrp.Position + mv) end)
            end
        end
    end)
end

function _G.MeridianSetAutoLeft(on, skipSave)
    autoLeftEnabled = on == true
    if autoLeftEnabled then
        if _G.MeridianStopAllExclusiveMovement then _G.MeridianStopAllExclusiveMovement("autoLeft") end
        _G.MeridianStartAutoLeft()
    else
        _G.MeridianStopAutoLeft()
    end
    if _G.MeridianSetAutoLeftVisual then _G.MeridianSetAutoLeftVisual(autoLeftEnabled) end
end

function _G.MeridianSetAutoRight(on, skipSave)
    autoRightEnabled = on == true
    if autoRightEnabled then
        if _G.MeridianStopAllExclusiveMovement then _G.MeridianStopAllExclusiveMovement("autoRight") end
        _G.MeridianStartAutoRight()
    else
        _G.MeridianStopAutoRight()
    end
    if _G.MeridianSetAutoRightVisual then _G.MeridianSetAutoRightVisual(autoRightEnabled) end
end

LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
task.wait(0.5)
if autoLeftEnabled and _G.MeridianStartAutoLeft then _G.MeridianStartAutoLeft() end
if autoRightEnabled and _G.MeridianStartAutoRight then _G.MeridianStartAutoRight() end
end)

local function darkenColor(c, factor)
    factor = factor or 0.35
    return Color3.new(c.R * factor, c.G * factor, c.B * factor)
end
local function applyOverheadThemeTint()

_G.MeridianThemeColors = _G.MeridianThemeColors or {
PURPLE = Color3.fromRGB(207, 159, 255),
BLUE   = Color3.fromRGB(58, 128, 245),
RED    = Color3.fromRGB(232, 52, 68),
PINK   = Color3.fromRGB(255, 105, 180),
YELLOW = Color3.fromRGB(255, 214, 0),
GREY   = Color3.fromRGB(90, 90, 90),
WHITE  = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
FOREST = Color3.fromRGB(46, 139, 87),
}
_G.MeridianThemeName = _G.MeridianThemeName or tostring((savedConfig and savedConfig.currentThemeName) or "WHITE")
local tint = (_G.MeridianThemeColors and _G.MeridianThemeColors[_G.MeridianThemeName]) or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
pcall(function() overheadSpeedLabel.TextColor3 = tint end)
pcall(function() overheadSpeedStroke.Color = darkenColor(tint, 0.35) end)
end
applyOverheadThemeTint()
_G.MeridianOverheadRetintFns = _G.MeridianOverheadRetintFns or {}
table.insert(_G.MeridianOverheadRetintFns, applyOverheadThemeTint)
task.spawn(function()
local t = 0
local baseLabelPos = overheadSpeedLabel.Position
local baseShadowPos = overheadSpeedShadow and overheadSpeedShadow.Position
while overheadSpeedLabel and overheadSpeedLabel.Parent do
t = t + task.wait(0.03)
if overheadSpeedGradient then overheadSpeedGradient.Offset = Vector2.new(math.sin(t * 1.4) * 0.6, 0) end
local pulse = 0.08 + math.sin(t * 3) * 0.08
overheadSpeedStroke.Transparency = pulse
local bump = 1 + math.sin(t * 2.2) * 0.015
overheadSpeedLabel.TextSize = 17 * bump
local bob = math.sin(t * 2.6) * 1.6
overheadSpeedLabel.Position = UDim2.new(baseLabelPos.X.Scale, baseLabelPos.X.Offset, baseLabelPos.Y.Scale, baseLabelPos.Y.Offset + bob)
if overheadSpeedShadow and baseShadowPos then
overheadSpeedShadow.Position = UDim2.new(baseShadowPos.X.Scale, baseShadowPos.X.Offset, baseShadowPos.Y.Scale, baseShadowPos.Y.Offset + bob)
end
if overheadSpeedShadow then overheadSpeedShadow.Text = overheadSpeedLabel.Text end
end
end)
end
local ragdollCountdownConn = nil
local ragdollCountdownCharConn = nil
local ragdollCountdownEndTime = 0
local RAGDOLL_COUNTDOWN_SECONDS = 2.6
function stopRagdollCountdown()
if ragdollCountdownConn then ragdollCountdownConn:Disconnect(); ragdollCountdownConn = nil end
if ragdollCountdownCharConn then ragdollCountdownCharConn:Disconnect(); ragdollCountdownCharConn = nil end
if ragdollCountdownLabel then
ragdollCountdownLabel.Visible = false
ragdollCountdownLabel.Text = ""
end
end
function hookRagdollCountdown(char)
stopRagdollCountdown()
if not ragdollCountdownEnabled then return end
char = char or LP.Character
if not char then return end
local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") or char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 4)
if not hum then return end
local function beginCountdown()
ragdollCountdownEndTime = tick() + RAGDOLL_COUNTDOWN_SECONDS
if ragdollCountdownLabel then
ragdollCountdownLabel.Visible = true
ragdollCountdownLabel.Text = string.format("GET UP: %.1fs", RAGDOLL_COUNTDOWN_SECONDS)
end
end
local function isRagdollStateForCountdown()
local st = hum:GetState()
return hum.PlatformStand
or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown
end
ragdollCountdownCharConn = hum.StateChanged:Connect(function(_, newState)
if newState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
or newState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
or newState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown then
beginCountdown()
end
end)
ragdollCountdownConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
if not ragdollCountdownEnabled then stopRagdollCountdown(); return end
if not ragdollCountdownLabel or not ragdollCountdownLabel.Parent then return end
if isRagdollStateForCountdown() and ragdollCountdownEndTime < tick() then
beginCountdown()
end
local left = math.max(0, ragdollCountdownEndTime - tick())
if left > 0 and isRagdollStateForCountdown() then
ragdollCountdownLabel.Visible = true
ragdollCountdownLabel.Text = string.format("GET UP: %.1fs", left)
ragdollCountdownLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
else
ragdollCountdownLabel.Visible = false
ragdollCountdownLabel.Text = ""
end
end)
end
if LP.Character then
task.spawn(function()
setupOverheadInfo(LP.Character)
task.wait(0.05)
if ragdollCountdownEnabled then hookRagdollCountdown(LP.Character) end
end)
end
LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
task.wait(0.5)
setupOverheadInfo(char)
if ragdollCountdownEnabled then hookRagdollCountdown(char) end
end)

-- ── AKAI LV SPEED ENGINE (SpeedBoost_Test replacement) ─────────
-- Replaces the old Prodigy AssemblyLinearVelocity + WalkSpeed method.
-- Uses a single LinearVelocity constraint (PlaneVelocity, World-space)
-- reused across the entire character lifetime — no destroy/recreate per
-- mode switch. No-drop floor: holds lastMoveDir when keys still held but
-- MoveDirection is zero (server momentarily returns 0 between inputs).
-- Velocity spoof keeps server reading Vector3.zero on HRP so lag is clean.
-- All 4 modes (Normal, Carry, Lagger, Lagger Carry) drive through
-- getCurrentSpeedValue() — same as before, no mode logic changed.

local MOVE_KEYS = {
    [Enum.KeyCode.W]     = true, [Enum.KeyCode.A]    = true,
    [Enum.KeyCode.S]     = true, [Enum.KeyCode.D]    = true,
    [Enum.KeyCode.Up]    = true, [Enum.KeyCode.Left]  = true,
    [Enum.KeyCode.Down]  = true, [Enum.KeyCode.Right] = true,
}

-- ── SENPAI SPEED ENGINE ────────────────────────────────────────
-- WalkSpeed + throttled soft-impulse assist (~20Hz).
-- Never fights physics, never spams velocity every frame.

local speedEnabled      = false
local currentSpeedValue = 0
local speedConnection   = nil
local speedLinearVelocity = nil
local speedAttachment   = nil
local _lowFrictionCheckTimer = 0
_G._akaiBypassSpoof = false

local function _speedSafeToModify(part)
    if not part then return false end
    local char = part.Parent
    if not char then return false end
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then return false end
    local st = hum:GetState()
    if st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead
    or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
    or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
    or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown
    or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp then
        return false
    end
    if hum.PlatformStand then return false end
    return true
end

local function setVelocityWithImpulse(part, desiredVel)
    if not part or not desiredVel then return end
    if not _speedSafeToModify(part) then return end
    local cur = part.AssemblyLinearVelocity
    local desired = Vector3.new(desiredVel.X, cur.Y, desiredVel.Z)
    local delta = desired - cur
    if math.abs(delta.X) < 1.2 and math.abs(delta.Z) < 1.2 then return end
    pcall(function() part.AssemblyLinearVelocity = desired end)
    local after = part.AssemblyLinearVelocity
    if (Vector3.new(after.X, 0, after.Z) - Vector3.new(desired.X, 0, desired.Z)).Magnitude > 4 then
        local mass = part:GetMass()
        if mass < 0.001 then mass = 1 end
        local impulse = mass * (desired - after) * 0.55
        pcall(function() part:ApplyImpulse(impulse) end)
    end
end

local function zeroVelocity(part)
    if not part then return end
    if not _speedSafeToModify(part) then return end
    pcall(function()
        local cur = part.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        part.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, cur.Y, 0)
        part.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
    end)
end

local function cleanupMeridianSpeedPhysics() end
local function cleanupSpeedPhysics() end
local function claimSpeedOwnership(root)
    pcall(function() root:SetNetworkOwner(LP) end)
end
local function spdRestorePhysics() end
local function applyLowFriction() end

function applyMeridianSpeedWithLinearVelocity(spd) end

function getMeridianHorizontalSpeedLV(root) return nil end

LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
    lastMoveDir = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
end)

-- Anti-jerk on grab: when a brainrot Tool is equipped mid-chase, any
-- leftover LinearVelocity/impulse from the speed engine can fight the
-- game's own grip weld the instant it attaches, reading as a visible
-- snap-back. Zeroing the HRP's horizontal velocity the moment a Tool
-- is equipped (ported from 7up's own pattern of zeroing velocity right
-- when a chase/lock begins) removes that residual momentum so the
-- pickup itself stays smooth instead of rubber-banding.
do
    local function watchForGrabSnap(char)
        char.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
            if not child:IsA("Tool") then return end
            task.defer(function()
                local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if not hrp then return end
                local v = getRealVelocity(hrp)
                if v then
                    writeRealVelocity(hrp, Vector3.new(0, v.Y, 0))
                end
            end)
        end)
    end
    if LP.Character then watchForGrabSnap(LP.Character) end
    LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(watchForGrabSnap)
end

-- ── MAIN SPEED LOOP ─────────────────────────────────────────────
-- WalkSpeed written only when it actually changes (not 60x/sec).
-- Impulse assist throttled to 10Hz max and only when clearly under
-- target speed — prevents server desync / random respawn.
local _senpaiLastApply   = 0
local _senpaiLastWS      = -1   -- last WalkSpeed we wrote — gate replicated writes
local _senpaiImpulseWait = 0.10 -- impulse throttle: 10Hz max (was 20Hz — too aggressive)

_G.MeridianSpeedLabelConn = _G.MeridianSpeedLabelConn or nil
if _G.MeridianSpeedLabelConn then pcall(function() _G.MeridianSpeedLabelConn:Disconnect() end) end
_G.MeridianSpeedLabelConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
    local char = LP.Character
    if not char then return end
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hum or not hrp then return end

    -- Overhead speed label
    if overheadSpeedLabel then
        local targetSpeed = getCurrentSpeedValue()
        local autoPathing = autoLeftEnabled or autoRightEnabled
        local isMoving = hum.MoveDirection.Magnitude > 0.1 or autoPathing
        local RAMP_TIME = 0.20
        local dt = 1/60
        if not _G._meridianDisplaySpeed then _G._meridianDisplaySpeed = 0 end
        if autoPathing then
            _G._meridianDisplaySpeed = tonumber(getAutoLRSpeed()) or tonumber(NS) or 60
        elseif isMoving then
            local step = (targetSpeed / RAMP_TIME) * dt
            _G._meridianDisplaySpeed = math.min(_G._meridianDisplaySpeed + step, targetSpeed)
        else
            local step = (targetSpeed / RAMP_TIME) * dt
            _G._meridianDisplaySpeed = math.max(_G._meridianDisplaySpeed - step, 0)
        end
        local rounded = math.floor(_G._meridianDisplaySpeed * 10 + 0.5) / 10
        local modeName = autoPathing and "Normal" or (currentSpeedMode or "Normal")
        overheadSpeedLabel.Text = string.format("%.1f┃%s", rounded, modeName)
        local shadow = overheadGui and overheadGui:FindFirstChild("SpeedShadow")
        if shadow then shadow.Text = overheadSpeedLabel.Text end
    end

    local spd = getCurrentSpeedValue()
    local st  = hum:GetState()

    local unsafe = hum.PlatformStand
        or hum.Health <= 0
        or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
        or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
        or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown
        or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead
        or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp

    if unsafe then
        lastMoveDir = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        -- Restore WalkSpeed to 16 only once on unsafe entry
        if _senpaiLastWS ~= 16 then
            _senpaiLastWS = 16
            pcall(function() hum.WalkSpeed = 16 end)
        end
        return
    end

    if not speedBoosterEnabled or spd <= 16 then
        if _senpaiLastWS ~= 16 then
            _senpaiLastWS = 16
            pcall(function() hum.WalkSpeed = 16 end)
        end
        return
    end

    local aimbotActive = _G.MeridianNormalAimbotOn == true
        or _G.MeridianAntiBypassAimbotOn == true
        or _G.MeridianAntiDesyncAimbotOn == true
    if autoLeftEnabled or autoRightEnabled or aimbotActive then
        return
    end

    -- WalkSpeed: write ONLY when value actually changed — not every frame
    -- (WalkSpeed is a replicated property; writing it 60x/sec desyncs server)
    if _senpaiLastWS ~= spd then
        _senpaiLastWS = spd
        pcall(function() hum.WalkSpeed = spd end)
    end

    local md = hum.MoveDirection
    if md.Magnitude > 0.05 then
        lastMoveDir = md
        local now = tick()
        -- Throttled impulse assist at 10Hz — only when server drag is eating speed
        if now - _senpaiLastApply >= _senpaiImpulseWait then
            _senpaiLastApply = now
            local flat = Vector3.new(md.X, 0, md.Z)
            if flat.Magnitude > 0.05 then
                flat = flat.Unit
                local curH = Vector3.new(
                    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.X, 0,
                    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Z
                ).Magnitude
                -- Only assist when clearly starved — tight threshold (0.70) to
                -- avoid fighting the server physics solver
                if curH < spd * 0.70 then
                    setVelocityWithImpulse(hrp, Vector3.new(
                        flat.X * spd,
                        hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y,
                        flat.Z * spd
                    ))
                end
            end
        end
    else
        lastMoveDir = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
    end
end)

-- Reset WalkSpeed write gate on respawn so first frame after spawn writes correctly
LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
    _senpaiLastWS = -1
    _senpaiLastApply = 0
    lastMoveDir = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
end)

-- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
--  ANTI E01 TIMER  —  Black pill, top of screen, white bold
--  Fires when carry is detected (WalkSpeed ≤ 25 and ≠ 16).
--  Shows: ANTI E01: X.XX  counting down from 3 seconds.
-- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
local _e01Gui        = nil
local _e01Conn       = nil
local _e01WasCarry   = false

local function _e01DestroyUI()
    if _e01Gui and _e01Gui.Parent then
        pcall(function() _e01Gui:Destroy() end)
    end
    _e01Gui = nil
    if _e01Conn then
        pcall(function() _e01Conn:Disconnect() end)
        _e01Conn = nil
    end
end

local function _e01Start()
    _e01DestroyUI()

    local pg = LP:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
    if not pg then return end

    local sg = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    sg.Name           = "MeridianAntiE01Gui"
    sg.ResetOnSpawn   = false
    sg.DisplayOrder   = 999
    sg.Parent         = pg
    _e01Gui           = sg

    -- Black pill container — top-center
    local pill = Instance.new("Frame", sg)
    pill.Name                  = "E01Pill"
    pill.AnchorPoint           = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
    pill.Size                  = UDim2.new(0, 220, 0, 32)
    pill.Position              = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 14)
    pill.BackgroundColor3      = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    pill.BackgroundTransparency = 0
    pill.BorderSizePixel       = 0
    Instance.new("UICorner", pill).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

    -- Subtle white stroke on the pill
    local pillStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", pill)
    pillStroke.Color       = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    pillStroke.Thickness   = 1.2
    pillStroke.Transparency = 0.72

    -- Label: "ANTI E01: X.XX"
    local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", pill)
    lbl.Name                    = "E01Label"
    lbl.Size                    = UDim2.new(1, -16, 1, 0)
    lbl.Position                = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 0)
    lbl.BackgroundTransparency  = 1
    lbl.Font                    = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    lbl.TextSize                = 13
    lbl.TextColor3              = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    lbl.TextStrokeColor3        = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    lbl.TextStrokeTransparency  = 0.4
    lbl.TextXAlignment          = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
    lbl.TextYAlignment          = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
    lbl.Text                    = "ANTI E01: 3.00"

    -- Slide in from top
    pill.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, -40)
    TweenService:Create(pill, TweenInfo.new(0.22, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
        Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 14)
    }):Play()

    local DURATION  = 3.0
    local startTime = tick()
    local done      = false

    _e01Conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if done or not pill.Parent then
            if _e01Conn then _e01Conn:Disconnect(); _e01Conn = nil end
            return
        end

        local elapsed   = tick() - startTime
        local remaining = math.max(0, DURATION - elapsed)

        if remaining > 0 then
            lbl.Text        = string.format("ANTI E01:  %.2f", remaining)
            lbl.TextColor3  = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        else
            done            = true
            lbl.Text        = "ANTI E01:  SAFE ✓"
            lbl.TextColor3  = Color3.fromRGB(120, 255, 160)
            if _e01Conn then _e01Conn:Disconnect(); _e01Conn = nil end

            -- Slide out after 2s
            task.delay(2, function()
                if pill and pill.Parent then
                    TweenService:Create(pill, TweenInfo.new(0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In), {
                        Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, -40)
                    }):Play()
                    task.wait(0.2)
                    _e01DestroyUI()
                end
            end)
        end
    end)
end

-- Carry detection: poll WalkSpeed same way as the source notifier
-- but hooked into the existing character so no extra polling thread.
local function _e01CheckCarry()
    local char = LP.Character
    if not char then return false end
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not hum then return false end
    local ws = hum.WalkSpeed
    return ws > 0 and ws <= 25 and ws ~= 16
end

-- Fire on steal-start detected by the existing GalaxyV3 steal pipeline
-- _G.MeridianOnStealStart is called from the steal loop when it begins
local _origOnStealStart = _G.MeridianOnStealStart
_G.MeridianOnStealStart = function(...)
    if _origOnStealStart then pcall(_origOnStealStart, ...) end
    -- E01 timer starts when carry begins (WalkSpeed drops post-steal)
    task.delay(0.1, function()
        if _e01CheckCarry() then _e01Start() end
    end)
end

-- Fallback: heartbeat polling (catches manual steals outside the pipeline)
task.spawn(function()
    while task.wait(0.1) do
        local carrying = _e01CheckCarry()
        if not _e01WasCarry and carrying then
            _e01Start()
        end
        _e01WasCarry = carrying
    end
end)

local COLORS = {
bg = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0),
row = Color3.fromRGB(6, 6, 9),
row2 = Color3.fromRGB(8, 8, 12),
stroke = Color3.fromRGB(90, 90, 105),
strokeSoft = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 72),
white = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
textDim = Color3.fromRGB(180, 180, 190),
toggleBg = Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 26),
knob = Color3.fromRGB(238, 238, 245),
}
function corner(parent, radius)
local c = Instance.new("UICorner")
c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, radius or 8)
c.Parent = parent
return c
end
function stroke(parent, color, thickness, transparency)
local s = Instance.new("UIStroke")
s.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
s.Color = color or COLORS.stroke
s.Thickness = thickness or 1
s.Transparency = transparency or 0.35
s.Parent = parent
local g = Instance.new("UIGradient")
g.Color = ColorSequence.new({
ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)),
ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.fromRGB(155, 160, 185)),
ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)),
})
g.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.55),
NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0.1),
NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0.55),
})
g.Parent = s

pcall(function()
if _G.MeridianAddRotatingGlow then _G.MeridianAddRotatingGlow(s, 2.4) end
end)
return s
end
function tween(obj, props, time)
TweenService:Create(obj, TweenInfo.new(time or 0.14, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), props):Play()
end

edge.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
edge.Thickness = 1.35
edge.Transparency = 0.24
edge.LineJoinMode = Enum.LineJoinMode.Round

grad.Name = "MovingLight"
grad.Rotation = 0
grad.Color = ColorSequence.new({
ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.00, Color3.fromRGB(95,95,95)),
ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.38, Color3.fromRGB(125,125,125)),
ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.46, Color3.fromRGB(235,235,235)),

ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.54, Color3.fromRGB(235,235,235)),
ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.62, Color3.fromRGB(125,125,125)),
ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1.00, Color3.fromRGB(95,95,95))
})
grad.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.00, 0.90),
NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.37, 0.82),
NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.45, 0.28),
NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.50, 0.00),
NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.55, 0.28),

})
grad.Parent = edge

TweenService:Create(grad, info, {Rotation = 360}):Play()
end)
end

_G.MeridianGlowRegistry = _G.MeridianGlowRegistry or setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
_G.MeridianStrokeRegistry = _G.MeridianStrokeRegistry or setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})

local function meridianGlowColorSequence()
local tint = (_G.MeridianThemeColors and _G.MeridianThemeColors[_G.MeridianThemeName]) or Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
local function dim(c, f)
return Color3.new(c.R * f, c.G * f, c.B * f)
end
return ColorSequence.new({
ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.00, dim(tint, 0.15)),
ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.25, dim(tint, 0.45)),
ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.40, dim(tint, 0.85)),
ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.50, tint),
ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.60, dim(tint, 0.85)),
ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.75, dim(tint, 0.45)),
ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1.00, dim(tint, 0.15))
})
end

function _G.MeridianRefreshGlowTheme()
local seq = meridianGlowColorSequence()
for grad in pairs(_G.MeridianGlowRegistry) do
if grad and grad.Parent then
pcall(function() grad.Color = seq end)
end
end

local tint = (_G.MeridianThemeColors and _G.MeridianThemeColors[_G.MeridianThemeName]) or Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
for st in pairs(_G.MeridianStrokeRegistry) do
if st and st.Parent then
pcall(function() st.Color = tint end)
end
end

pcall(function()
local roots = {}
if _G.MeridianMainGui then table.insert(roots, _G.MeridianMainGui) end
if _G.MeridianInfoBarGui then table.insert(roots, _G.MeridianInfoBarGui) end
if _G.MeridianMobileGui then table.insert(roots, _G.MeridianMobileGui) end
for _, root in ipairs(roots) do
if root and root.Parent then
for _, obj in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
if obj:IsA("UIGradient") and obj.Name ~= "MeridianFillGrad" then
pcall(function() obj.Color = seq end)
elseif obj:IsA("UIStroke") and obj.Name ~= "MeridianPressFlash" and obj.Name ~= "MeridianTextOutline" then
pcall(function() obj.Color = tint end)
end
end
end
end
end)

if _G.MeridianOverheadRetintFns then
for _, fn in ipairs(_G.MeridianOverheadRetintFns) do
pcall(fn)
end
end

if _G.MeridianAimbotVersionGlowRefreshFns then
for _, fn in ipairs(_G.MeridianAimbotVersionGlowRefreshFns) do
pcall(fn)
end
end

if _G.MeridianDropModeGlowRefreshFns then
for _, fn in ipairs(_G.MeridianDropModeGlowRefreshFns) do
pcall(fn)
end
end

-- Refresh fill bar color whenever theme changes
if _G.MeridianRefreshFillBarTheme then
    pcall(_G.MeridianRefreshFillBarTheme)
end
end

function _G.MeridianAddRotatingGlow(existingStroke, speed)
if not existingStroke or not existingStroke:IsA("UIStroke") then return end
if existingStroke:FindFirstChild("MeridianRotatingGlow") then return end

local grad = existingStroke:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIGradient")
if grad then
grad.Name = "MeridianRotatingGlow"
else
grad = Instance.new("UIGradient")
grad.Name = "MeridianRotatingGlow"
grad.Parent = existingStroke
end
grad.Rotation = 0
grad.Color = meridianGlowColorSequence()

grad.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.00, 0.25),
NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.25, 0.15),
NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.40, 0.0),
NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.50, 0.0),
NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.60, 0.0),
NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.75, 0.15),
NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1.00, 0.25)
})
_G.MeridianGlowRegistry[grad] = true
_G.MeridianStrokeRegistry[existingStroke] = true

pcall(function()
existingStroke.Thickness = math.max(existingStroke.Thickness or 1, 3.0)
end)

local info = TweenInfo.new(speed or 1.6, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut, -1, false, 0)
pcall(function()
TweenService:Create(grad, info, {Rotation = 360}):Play()
end)
return grad
end

function _G.MeridianApplyMovingEdgeLights(root)
root = root or PlayerGui
if not root then return end

for _, obj in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
if (obj:IsA("TextButton") or obj:IsA("ImageButton")) and obj.Name == "ArrowButton" then
pcall(function() _G.MeridianAddMovingEdgeLight(obj) end)
end
end

root.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
if (obj:IsA("TextButton") or obj:IsA("ImageButton")) and obj.Name == "ArrowButton" then
task.defer(function()
task.wait()
pcall(function() _G.MeridianAddMovingEdgeLight(obj) end)
end)
end
end)
end

function makeDraggable(frame)
local dragging = false
local dragStart
local startPos
local dragInput
frame.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
if _G.MeridianGuiLocked == true then return end
if _G.__MeridianInputClaimedByButton then return end
if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
dragging = true
dragStart = input.Position
startPos = frame.Position
input.Changed:Connect(function()
if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
dragging = false
end
end)
end
end)
frame.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
dragInput = input
end
end)
UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
if _G.MeridianGuiLocked == true then return end
if input == dragInput and dragging then
local delta = input.Position - dragStart
frame.Position = UDim2.new(
startPos.X.Scale,
startPos.X.Offset + delta.X,
startPos.Y.Scale,
startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y
)
end
end)
end

-- Meridian Duels -- prevents a tap on a control button (Close, etc.)
-- that sits on top of a draggable frame (Main) from also being read
-- as the start of a drag. Both frame.InputBegan (drag start) and the
-- button's own click fire on the same touch since Roblox doesn't
-- suppress one for the other automatically - the resulting tiny
-- position nudge from the drag handler was enough to make Roblox's
-- own click-vs-drag gesture detection misread the first tap as a
-- drag, so MouseButton1Click never fired and a second tap was needed.
-- Call this on any button that sits inside a draggable frame.
--
-- Fix: the claim flag used to be set from the button's own
-- InputBegan connection, but makeDraggable(Main) was always wired up
-- BEFORE this ran (Main is built first, buttons like Close come
-- after), so Main's InputBegan connection fired first and read the
-- flag before Close's connection had a chance to set it - the flag
-- was effectively always one tap too late. MouseButton1Down fires
-- synchronously as soon as Roblox recognizes the press on this
-- specific GuiObject, ahead of the frame-level InputBegan gesture
-- processing, so setting the flag there actually wins the race.
function claimInputForButton(button)
button.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function()
_G.__MeridianInputClaimedByButton = true
end)
button.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
_G.__MeridianInputClaimedByButton = true
end
end)
button.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
_G.__MeridianInputClaimedByButton = false
end
end)
end

Gui.Name = "MeridianDuelsHub"
Gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
Gui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
Gui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
Gui.DisplayOrder = 999
safeParentGui(Gui)
_G.MeridianMainGui = Gui
local FULL_MAIN_SIZE = UDim2.new(0, 340, 0, 510)
local Main = Instance.new("Frame")
Main.Name = "Main"
Main.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
Main.Size = FULL_MAIN_SIZE
Main.Position = tableToUDim2(savedMainPositionTable, UDim2.new(0, 20, 0.5, 0))
savedMainPositionTable = udim2ToTable(Main.Position)
Main.BackgroundColor3 = COLORS.bg
Main.BorderSizePixel = 0
Main.Active = true
Main.ClipsDescendants = false
Main.Parent = Gui
corner(Main, 14)

makeDraggable(Main)
Main:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Position"):Connect(function()
savedMainPositionTable = udim2ToTable(Main.Position)
end)

-- Slide animation helpers
-- Open: slide in from left → final saved position
-- Close: slide out to left → hide
local _slideConn = nil
local function _cancelSlide()
    if _slideConn then
        pcall(function() _slideConn:Disconnect() end)
        _slideConn = nil
    end
end
local SLIDE_SHRINK = 0.88 -- starting scale for the grow-in, 1.0 = full size
function _G.MeridianSlideOpen(onDone)
    _cancelSlide()
    -- Compute final X from saved position; slide starts 400px off to the left
    local finalPos = tableToUDim2(savedMainPositionTable, UDim2.new(0, 20, 0.5, 0))
    local offscreenPos = UDim2.new(finalPos.X.Scale, finalPos.X.Offset - 400, finalPos.Y.Scale, finalPos.Y.Offset)
    local startSize = UDim2.new(
        FULL_MAIN_SIZE.X.Scale, FULL_MAIN_SIZE.X.Offset * SLIDE_SHRINK,
        FULL_MAIN_SIZE.Y.Scale, FULL_MAIN_SIZE.Y.Offset * SLIDE_SHRINK
    )
    Main.Size = startSize
    Main.Position = offscreenPos
    Main.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    Main.ClipsDescendants = true -- cuts composite overdraw from the 24 live glow tweens while sliding
    Main.Visible = true
    local t = TweenService:Create(Main,
        TweenInfo.new(0.28, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
        { Position = finalPos, Size = FULL_MAIN_SIZE, BackgroundTransparency = 0 }
    )
    t:Play()
    _slideConn = t.Completed:Connect(function()
        _cancelSlide()
        Main.Position = finalPos
        Main.Size = FULL_MAIN_SIZE
        Main.BackgroundTransparency = 0
        Main.ClipsDescendants = false -- restore so nothing gets cropped once settled
        savedMainPositionTable = udim2ToTable(Main.Position)
        _G.MeridianGuiVisible = true
        if onDone then pcall(onDone) end
    end)
end
function _G.MeridianSlideClose(onDone)
    _cancelSlide()
    local currentPos = Main.Position
    local currentSize = Main.Size
    local offscreenPos = UDim2.new(currentPos.X.Scale, currentPos.X.Offset - 400, currentPos.Y.Scale, currentPos.Y.Offset)
    local shrinkSize = UDim2.new(
        currentSize.X.Scale, currentSize.X.Offset * SLIDE_SHRINK,
        currentSize.Y.Scale, currentSize.Y.Offset * SLIDE_SHRINK
    )
    Main.ClipsDescendants = true -- cuts composite overdraw from the 24 live glow tweens while sliding
    local t = TweenService:Create(Main,
        TweenInfo.new(0.20, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.In),
        { Position = offscreenPos, Size = shrinkSize, BackgroundTransparency = 1 }
    )
    t:Play()
    _slideConn = t.Completed:Connect(function()
        _cancelSlide()
        Main.Visible = false
        Main.Position = currentPos  -- restore position so next open starts correctly
        Main.Size = currentSize
        Main.BackgroundTransparency = 0
        Main.ClipsDescendants = false -- restore so nothing gets cropped once hidden/reopened
        savedMainPositionTable = udim2ToTable(currentPos)
        _G.MeridianGuiVisible = false
        if canSaveConfig and saveMeridianConfig then pcall(saveMeridianConfig) end
        if onDone then pcall(onDone) end
    end)
end

local MeridianMiniLogo = Instance.new("ImageLabel")

local MiniMeridianStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
MiniMeridianStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)

do
local miniDragging = false
local miniDragStart = nil
local miniStartPos = nil
local miniMoved = false
local miniHeldInput = nil
local DRAG_DEADZONE = 6
MiniButton.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
if _G.MeridianGuiLocked == true then return end
if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
miniDragging = true
miniMoved = false
miniHeldInput = input
miniDragStart = input.Position
miniStartPos = MiniFrame.Position
end
end)
UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
if not miniDragging then return end
if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
if not miniDragStart or not miniStartPos then return end
local delta = input.Position - miniDragStart
if math.abs(delta.X) > DRAG_DEADZONE or math.abs(delta.Y) > DRAG_DEADZONE then
miniMoved = true
end
MiniFrame.Position = UDim2.new(
miniStartPos.X.Scale,
miniStartPos.X.Offset + delta.X,
miniStartPos.Y.Scale,
miniStartPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y
)
end)
UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
if input ~= miniHeldInput then return end
local wasDrag = miniMoved
miniDragging = false
miniHeldInput = nil
miniDragStart = nil
miniStartPos = nil
if wasDrag then
return
end
_G.__MeridianDuelsMinimized = false
MiniFrame.Visible = false
_G.MeridianSlideOpen(function()
    savedMainPositionTable = udim2ToTable(Main.Position)
    if _G.MeridianRefreshStealModeSettings then pcall(_G.MeridianRefreshStealModeSettings) end
end)
end)
-- Always-working open trigger: while the GUI is locked, InputBegan
-- above never arms miniHeldInput, so the drag-aware InputEnded path
-- never fires. This click handler opens the menu regardless of lock
-- state, so the opener can still be pressed (just not dragged) while
-- locked.
MiniButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
if _G.MeridianGuiLocked ~= true then return end
_G.__MeridianDuelsMinimized = false
MiniFrame.Visible = false
_G.MeridianSlideOpen(function()
    savedMainPositionTable = udim2ToTable(Main.Position)
    if _G.MeridianRefreshStealModeSettings then pcall(_G.MeridianRefreshStealModeSettings) end
end)
end)
end

function applyBackground(index)
currentBackground = index or 0
if currentBackground == 0 then
Main.BackgroundColor3 = COLORS.bg
BgImage.Visible = false
do end
return "None"
end
local id = BackgroundIDs[currentBackground]
if id then
BgImage.Image = "rbxassetid://" .. id
local selectedTint = _G.MeridianThemeColors[_G.MeridianThemeName] or _G.MeridianThemeColors.WHITE
BgImage.ImageColor3 = selectedTint
if MeridianLogoAsset then MeridianLogoAsset.ImageColor3 = selectedTint end
BgImage.Visible = true
do end
return "Image " .. tostring(currentBackground)
end
currentBackground = 0
BgImage.Visible = false
do end
return "None"
end
applyBackground(currentBackground)

local Content = Instance.new("Frame")
Content.Name = "Content"
Content.BackgroundTransparency = 1

Content.ZIndex = 3
Content.Parent = Main
local Tabs = Instance.new("Frame")
Tabs.Name = "Tabs"
Tabs.BackgroundTransparency = 1
Tabs.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 1, -42)
Tabs.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 34)
Tabs.ZIndex = 3
Tabs.Parent = Main

do
local IDLE_DELAY = 5
local FADE_TIME = 0.45
local idleToken = 0
local idleCollapsed = false
local savedTransparency = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
local activeTweens = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})

local function rememberObject(obj)
    if savedTransparency[obj] then return end
    local data = {}
    if obj:IsA("GuiObject") then
        data.BackgroundTransparency = obj.BackgroundTransparency
    end
    if obj:IsA("TextLabel") or obj:IsA("TextButton") or obj:IsA("TextBox") then
        data.TextTransparency = obj.TextTransparency
        data.TextStrokeTransparency = obj.TextStrokeTransparency
    end
    if obj:IsA("ImageLabel") or obj:IsA("ImageButton") then
        data.ImageTransparency = obj.ImageTransparency
    end
    if obj:IsA("UIStroke") then
        data.Transparency = obj.Transparency
    end
    savedTransparency[obj] = data
end

local function tweenObject(obj, collapsed)
    rememberObject(obj)
    local base = savedTransparency[obj]
    if not base then return end

    if activeTweens[obj] then
        pcall(function() activeTweens[obj]:Cancel() end)
        activeTweens[obj] = nil
    end

    local goal = {}
    if base.BackgroundTransparency ~= nil then
        goal.BackgroundTransparency = collapsed and math.max(base.BackgroundTransparency, 0.78) or base.BackgroundTransparency
    end
    if base.TextTransparency ~= nil then
        goal.TextTransparency = collapsed and math.max(base.TextTransparency, 0.58) or base.TextTransparency
    end
    if base.TextStrokeTransparency ~= nil then
        goal.TextStrokeTransparency = collapsed and math.max(base.TextStrokeTransparency, 0.82) or base.TextStrokeTransparency
    end
    if base.ImageTransparency ~= nil then
        goal.ImageTransparency = collapsed and math.max(base.ImageTransparency, 0.68) or base.ImageTransparency
    end
    if base.Transparency ~= nil then
        goal.Transparency = collapsed and math.max(base.Transparency, 0.72) or base.Transparency
    end

    if next(goal) then
        local tw = TweenService:Create(
            obj,
            TweenInfo.new(FADE_TIME, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
            goal
        )
        activeTweens[obj] = tw
        tw:Play()
    end
end

local restoreToken = 0

local function forceOriginalTransparency()
    for obj, base in pairs(savedTransparency) do
        if obj and obj.Parent and base then
            if activeTweens[obj] then
                pcall(function() activeTweens[obj]:Cancel() end)
                activeTweens[obj] = nil
            end
            pcall(function()
                if base.BackgroundTransparency ~= nil then obj.BackgroundTransparency = base.BackgroundTransparency end
                if base.TextTransparency ~= nil then obj.TextTransparency = base.TextTransparency end
                if base.TextStrokeTransparency ~= nil then obj.TextStrokeTransparency = base.TextStrokeTransparency end
                if base.ImageTransparency ~= nil then obj.ImageTransparency = base.ImageTransparency end
                if base.Transparency ~= nil then obj.Transparency = base.Transparency end
            end)
        end
    end
end

local function setMenuIdleCollapsed(state)
    local newState = state == true
    restoreToken += 1
    local myRestoreToken = restoreToken

    if newState and not idleCollapsed then
        savedTransparency = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
        for _, root in ipairs({Content, Tabs, Close, HeaderDivider}) do
            rememberObject(root)
            for _, obj in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
                rememberObject(obj)
            end
        end
    end

    idleCollapsed = newState

    Content.Visible = true
    Tabs.Visible = true
    Close.Visible = true
    HeaderDivider.Visible = false

    for _, root in ipairs({Content, Tabs, Close, HeaderDivider}) do
        tweenObject(root, idleCollapsed)
        for _, obj in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
            tweenObject(obj, idleCollapsed)
        end
    end

    if not newState then

        task.delay(FADE_TIME + 0.08, function()
            if myRestoreToken ~= restoreToken or idleCollapsed then return end

            local untilTime = tick() + 1.5
            repeat
                if myRestoreToken ~= restoreToken or idleCollapsed then return end
                forceOriginalTransparency()
                RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
            until tick() >= untilTime
            forceOriginalTransparency()

        end)
    end
end

local function cancelIdleAndRestore()
    idleToken += 1
    if idleCollapsed then
        setMenuIdleCollapsed(false)
    elseif next(savedTransparency) then

        forceOriginalTransparency()
    end
end

local function scheduleIdleCollapse()

    if UserInputService.TouchEnabled and not UserInputService.MouseEnabled then return end
    idleToken += 1
    local myToken = idleToken
    task.delay(IDLE_DELAY, function()
        if myToken ~= idleToken then return end
        if not Main or not Main.Parent or not Main.Visible then return end
        setMenuIdleCollapsed(true)
    end)
end

Main.MouseEnter:Connect(cancelIdleAndRestore)
Main.MouseLeave:Connect(scheduleIdleCollapse)

local _wasMouseInsideMain = false
_G.MeridianMenuIdleConn = _G.MeridianMenuIdleConn or nil
if _G.MeridianMenuIdleConn then pcall(function() _G.MeridianMenuIdleConn:Disconnect() end) end
_G.MeridianMenuIdleConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
    if not Main or not Main.Parent or not Main.Visible then return end
    if UserInputService.TouchEnabled and not UserInputService.MouseEnabled then return end
    local mousePos = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
    local pos = Main.AbsolutePosition
    local size = Main.AbsoluteSize
    local inside = mousePos.X >= pos.X and mousePos.X <= pos.X + size.X
        and mousePos.Y >= pos.Y and mousePos.Y <= pos.Y + size.Y
    if inside then
        if idleCollapsed then
            cancelIdleAndRestore()
        elseif not _wasMouseInsideMain then

            for _, root in ipairs({Content, Tabs, Close, HeaderDivider}) do
                if root:IsA("TextLabel") or root:IsA("TextButton") or root:IsA("TextBox") then
                    root.TextTransparency = 0
                end
                for _, obj in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
                    if obj:IsA("TextLabel") or obj:IsA("TextButton") or obj:IsA("TextBox") then
                        if obj.Visible then
                            obj.TextTransparency = 0
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    _wasMouseInsideMain = inside
end)

Main.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)

    if idleCollapsed then
        task.defer(function()
            if obj and obj.Parent then
                savedTransparency[obj] = nil
                tweenObject(obj, true)
            end
        end)
    end
end)

task.defer(scheduleIdleCollapse)
end

local TabLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
TabLayout.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
TabLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 6)
TabLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
TabLayout.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center
TabLayout.Parent = Tabs
local pages = {}
local tabButtons = {}
local tabNames = {"MOVEMENT", "COMBAT", "KEYBINDS", "VISUALS", "MENU", "SETTINGS"}
local activeTab = "MOVEMENT"
function addPage(name)
local page = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
page.Name = name
page.BackgroundTransparency = 1
page.BorderSizePixel = 0
page.ScrollBarThickness = 0
page.ScrollBarImageTransparency = 1
page.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
page.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
page.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
page.ZIndex = 3
page.Visible = false
page.Parent = Content
local layout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
layout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 7)
layout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
layout.Parent = page
pages[name] = page
return page
end
function setTab(name)
activeTab = name
for pageName, page in pairs(pages) do
page.Visible = pageName == name
end
for tabName, btn in pairs(tabButtons) do
local on = tabName == name
btn.TextColor3 = on and Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255) or Color3.fromRGB(190,187,202)
btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
btn.BackgroundTransparency = on and 0.78 or 1
local st = btn:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
if st then
st.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
st.Thickness = math.max(st.Thickness or 1, 3.0)
st.Transparency = on and 0.15 or 0.52
end
end
end
for _, name in ipairs(tabNames) do
addPage(name)
local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
btn.Name = name
btn.Size = UDim2.new(0.166667, -5, 1, 0)
btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
btn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
btn.BorderSizePixel = 0
btn.Text = ({KEYBINDS="KBM", MENU="CTRL", VISUALS="VISUAL"})[name] or name
btn.LayoutOrder = ({MOVEMENT=1, COMBAT=2, KEYBINDS=3, MENU=4, VISUALS=5, SETTINGS=6})[name] or 99
btn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(190,187,202)
btn.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0)
btn.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.22
btn.TextSize = 11
btn.TextScaled = true
btn.TextWrapped = true
btn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
btn.AutoButtonColor = false
btn.ZIndex = 4
btn.Parent = Tabs
local tabTextConstraint = Instance.new("UITextSizeConstraint")
tabTextConstraint.MinTextSize = 6
tabTextConstraint.MaxTextSize = 12
tabTextConstraint.Parent = btn
corner(btn, 9)
local tabBtnStroke = stroke(btn, Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255), 1.2, 0.52)
pcall(function() _G.MeridianAddRotatingGlow(tabBtnStroke, 2.4) end)
tabButtons[name] = btn
local targetTabName = name
btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
setTab(targetTabName)
end)
end

local button = Instance.new("TextButton")

button.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)

button.AutoButtonColor = false
button.ZIndex = 7
button.Parent = row
local track = Instance.new("Frame")
track.Name = "Track"
track.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
track.BackgroundTransparency = 0.2
track.Size = UDim2.new(0, 34, 0, 18)
track.Position = UDim2.new(1, -44, 0.5, -9)

corner(track, 9)
local toggleTrackStroke = stroke(track, COLORS.strokeSoft, 1, 0.45)
pcall(function() _G.MeridianAddRotatingGlow(toggleTrackStroke, 2.0) end)
local knob = Instance.new("Frame")
knob.Name = "Knob"
knob.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(18,18,22)
knob.Size = UDim2.new(0, 13, 0, 13)
knob.Position = default and UDim2.new(1, -16, 0.5, -6) or UDim2.new(0, 3, 0.5, -6)
knob.BorderSizePixel = 0
knob.ZIndex = 6
knob.Parent = track
corner(knob, 999)
local shine = Instance.new("Frame")
shine.Name = "Shine"
shine.BackgroundColor3 = COLORS.white
shine.BackgroundTransparency = 0.72
shine.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 4)
shine.Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, 2)
shine.BorderSizePixel = 0
shine.ZIndex = 7
shine.Parent = knob
corner(shine, 4)
local state = default and true or false
local trackStroke = track:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
local rowStroke = row:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
local function setVisual(on)
state = on and true or false
tween(knob, {
Position = state and UDim2.new(1,-16,0.5,-6) or UDim2.new(0,3,0.5,-6),
BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(18,18,22)
})
tween(track, {

BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255),
BackgroundTransparency = 0.2
})
if trackStroke then
tween(trackStroke, {
Color = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255),
Transparency = 0.45,
Thickness = math.max(trackStroke.Thickness or 1, 3.0)
})
end
if rowStroke then
rowStroke.Thickness = math.max(rowStroke.Thickness or 1, 3.0)
end
row.BackgroundTransparency = 0.3
end
setVisual(state)
button.Activated:Connect(function()
end)
return row, setVisual
end

pcall(function() if setAutoStealVisual then setAutoStealVisual(autoStealEnabled == true) end end)
pcall(function() if setInfJumpVisual then setInfJumpVisual(infJumpEnabled == true) end end)
pcall(function() if setAntiRagdollVisual then setAntiRagdollVisual(antiRagdollEnabled == true) end end)
pcall(function() if setAutoCarrySpeedVisual then setAutoCarrySpeedVisual(autoCarrySpeedEnabled == true) end end)
pcall(function() if setAutoTPVisual then setAutoTPVisual(autoTPEnabled == true) end end)
pcall(function() if setAutoResetOnMedVisual then setAutoResetOnMedVisual(autoResetOnMedEnabled == true) end end)
end
