function refreshAnimationPackRow()
local displayName = tostring(selectedAnimationPack or "OFF")

if animationPackValueLabel and animationPackValueLabel.Parent then
animationPackValueLabel.Text = displayName
end

pcall(function()
    if pages and pages.VISUALS then
        for _, obj in ipairs(pages.VISUALS:GetDescendants()) do
            if obj:IsA("TextLabel") and obj.Name == "AnimationPackValue" then
                obj.Text = displayName
            end
        end
    end
end)
end
function animationPackRow(parent, order)
-- Row is kept as a minimal invisible anchor so existing callers don't break.
-- The real UI is the side panel built below (MeridianAnimSidePanel).
local row = baseRow(parent, "Animation Pack", order)
row.Size = UDim2.new(1,-4,0,0)
row.Visible = false
row.BackgroundTransparency = 1
local animRowStroke = row:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
if animRowStroke then animRowStroke.Transparency = 1 end

local function setPackIndex(idx)
    idx = ((idx - 1) % #AnimationPackList) + 1
    AnimationPackIndex = idx
    selectedAnimationPack = AnimationPackList[idx]
    if selectedAnimationPack == "OFF" then
        if _G.MeridianAnimStop then pcall(_G.MeridianAnimStop) end
    else
        if _G.MeridianAnimStart then pcall(_G.MeridianAnimStart, selectedAnimationPack) end
    end
    if saveMeridianConfig then pcall(saveMeridianConfig) end
    -- Refresh side panel list highlight if open
    if _G.MeridianAnimPanelRefresh then pcall(_G.MeridianAnimPanelRefresh) end
end

-- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- ANIMATION SIDE PANEL
-- Parented to Main frame, right-side overlay, full height.
-- Layout: sticky search bar at top, scrollable list below.
-- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
local SIDE_W       = 155
local SEARCH_H     = 30
local ITEM_H       = 28
local PAD          = 6
local animSideOpen = false
local animSidePanel= nil
local animSideSearchText = ""

local function buildSidePanel()
    if animSidePanel and animSidePanel.Parent then animSidePanel:Destroy() end

    local panel = Instance.new("Frame")
    panel.Name = "MeridianAnimSidePanel"
    panel.Size = UDim2.new(0, SIDE_W, 1, 0)
    panel.Position = UDim2.new(1, 2, 0, 0)   -- just outside right edge of Main
    panel.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(8, 8, 12)
    panel.BackgroundTransparency = 0.05
    panel.BorderSizePixel = 0
    panel.ZIndex = 30
    panel.ClipsDescendants = true
    panel.Parent = Main
    corner(panel, 10)
    stroke(panel, COLORS.strokeSoft, 1.2, 0.15)
    animSidePanel = panel

    -- Slide in from right
    panel.Position = UDim2.new(1, SIDE_W + 8, 0, 0)
    TweenService:Create(panel, TweenInfo.new(0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
        Position = UDim2.new(1, 2, 0, 0)
    }):Play()

    -- ── SEARCH BAR (sticky — direct child of panel, not inside scroll) ──
    local searchBar = Instance.new("TextBox")
    searchBar.Name = "AnimSearch"
    searchBar.PlaceholderText = "Search..."
    searchBar.Text = animSideSearchText
    searchBar.ClearTextOnFocus = false
    searchBar.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    searchBar.TextSize = 11
    searchBar.TextColor3 = COLORS.white
    searchBar.PlaceholderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(110, 110, 120)
    searchBar.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 24)
    searchBar.BackgroundTransparency = 0.05
    searchBar.BorderSizePixel = 0
    searchBar.Size = UDim2.new(1, -PAD*2, 0, SEARCH_H)
    searchBar.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(PAD, PAD)
    searchBar.ZIndex = 32
    searchBar.Parent = panel
    corner(searchBar, 7)
    stroke(searchBar, COLORS.strokeSoft, 1, 0.4)

    -- ── SCROLL LIST (below search bar, never overlaps it) ──
    local listTop = PAD + SEARCH_H + PAD
    local scroll = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
    scroll.Name = "AnimScroll"
    scroll.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    scroll.BorderSizePixel = 0
    scroll.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(PAD, listTop)
    scroll.Size = UDim2.new(1, -PAD*2, 1, -(listTop + PAD))
    scroll.ScrollBarThickness = 3
    scroll.ScrollBarImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(160, 160, 170)
    scroll.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    scroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0,0,0,0)
    scroll.ZIndex = 31
    scroll.Parent = panel

    local listLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
    listLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 3)
    listLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    listLayout.Parent = scroll

    local function renderList(query)
        for _, ch in ipairs(scroll:GetChildren()) do
            if ch:IsA("TextButton") then ch:Destroy() end
        end
        query = (query or ""):lower()
        local idx = 0
        for _, packName in ipairs(AnimationPackList) do
            if query == "" or packName:lower():find(query, 1, true) then
                idx = idx + 1
                local isSel = packName == selectedAnimationPack
                local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
                btn.Text = packName
                btn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
                btn.TextSize = 11
                btn.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                btn.BackgroundColor3 = isSel
                    and Color3.fromRGB(50, 50, 65)
                    or  Color3.fromRGB(16, 16, 22)
                btn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.08
                btn.BorderSizePixel = 0
                btn.TextColor3 = isSel
                    and COLORS.white
                    or  Color3.fromRGB(185, 185, 200)
                btn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, ITEM_H)
                btn.LayoutOrder = idx
                btn.ZIndex = 32
                btn.AutoButtonColor = false
                btn.Parent = scroll
                corner(btn, 6)

                local pad = Instance.new("UIPadding")
                pad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 8)
                pad.Parent = btn

                btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                    for i, n in ipairs(AnimationPackList) do
                        if n == packName then
                            setPackIndex(i); break
                        end
                    end
                    renderList(searchBar.Text)
                end)
                btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
                    if packName ~= selectedAnimationPack then
                        btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(28, 28, 38)
                    end
                end)
                btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
                    btn.BackgroundColor3 = packName == selectedAnimationPack
                        and Color3.fromRGB(50, 50, 65)
                        or  Color3.fromRGB(16, 16, 22)
                end)
            end
        end
    end

    -- Expose refresh so setPackIndex can update highlight
    _G.MeridianAnimPanelRefresh = function()
        renderList(searchBar.Text)
    end

    renderList(animSideSearchText)

    searchBar:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
        animSideSearchText = searchBar.Text
        task.defer(function() renderList(searchBar.Text) end)
    end)

    -- Close on outside click
    local clickConn
    clickConn = UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
        and input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
        if not animSidePanel or not animSidePanel.Parent then
            if clickConn then clickConn:Disconnect() end; return
        end
        -- Check the actual clicked GUI object instead of raw mouse-
        -- coordinate math against the panel's AbsolutePosition/Size.
        -- The coordinate check could momentarily read stale panel
        -- bounds right after renderList() destroys/recreates the list
        -- buttons on selection (reflow from AutomaticCanvasSize), which
        -- made picking an option look like clicking "outside" and
        -- closed the panel immediately after a selection.
        local mouseLoc = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
        local guiObjects = UserInputService:GetGuiObjectsAtPosition(mouseLoc.X, mouseLoc.Y)
        local inside = false
        for _, obj in ipairs(guiObjects) do
            if obj == animSidePanel or obj:IsDescendantOf(animSidePanel) then
                inside = true
                break
            end
        end
        if not inside then
            TweenService:Create(animSidePanel, TweenInfo.new(0.14, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), {
                Position = UDim2.new(1, SIDE_W + 8, 0, 0)
            }):Play()
            task.delay(0.15, function()
                if animSidePanel then animSidePanel:Destroy(); animSidePanel = nil end
                animSideOpen = false
                _G.MeridianAnimPanelRefresh = nil
            end)
            if clickConn then clickConn:Disconnect(); clickConn = nil end
        end
    end)
end

-- Expose toggle so anything can open/close the panel
function _G.MeridianToggleAnimPanel()
    if animSideOpen and animSidePanel and animSidePanel.Parent then
        TweenService:Create(animSidePanel, TweenInfo.new(0.14, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), {
            Position = UDim2.new(1, SIDE_W + 8, 0, 0)
        }):Play()
        task.delay(0.15, function()
            if animSidePanel then animSidePanel:Destroy(); animSidePanel = nil end
            animSideOpen = false
            _G.MeridianAnimPanelRefresh = nil
        end)
    else
        animSideOpen = true
        buildSidePanel()
    end
end

return row
end
function keyName(key)
if not key then return "None" end
local name = tostring(key):gsub("Enum.KeyCode.", "")
name = name:gsub("Button", "BTN ")
name = name:gsub("DPad", "DPad ")
return name
end
function refreshSpeedKeybindButton(keyId)
local btn = speedKeybindButtons[keyId]
if btn then
if listeningForSpeedKey == keyId then
btn.Text = "Press..."
else
btn.Text = keyName(speedKeybinds[keyId])
end
end
end
function refreshAllSpeedKeybinds()
for keyId in pairs(speedKeybindButtons) do
refreshSpeedKeybindButton(keyId)
end
end
function refreshTPDownKeybind()
if tpDownKeybindButton then
tpDownKeybindButton.Text = listeningForTPDownKey and "Press..." or keyName(tpDownKeybind)
end
end
function tpDownKeybindRow(parent, order)
local row = baseRow(parent, "TP Down", order)
local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
btn.Name = "TPDownKeybindButton"
btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(8, 8, 12)
btn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.18
btn.Text = keyName(tpDownKeybind)
btn.TextColor3 = COLORS.white
btn.TextSize = 11
btn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
btn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 56, 0, 24)
btn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -64, 0.5, -12)
btn.BorderSizePixel = 0
btn.ZIndex = 6
btn.AutoButtonColor = false
btn.Parent = row
corner(btn, 14)
stroke(btn, COLORS.strokeSoft, 1, 0.45)
local clearBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
clearBtn.Name = "ClearKeybindButton"
clearBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(8, 8, 12)
clearBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.18
clearBtn.Text = "×"
clearBtn.TextColor3 = COLORS.white
clearBtn.TextSize = 14
clearBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
clearBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 22, 0, 24)
clearBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -90, 0.5, -12)
clearBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
clearBtn.ZIndex = 6
clearBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
clearBtn.Parent = row
corner(clearBtn, 7)
stroke(clearBtn, COLORS.strokeSoft, 1, 0.45)
tpDownKeybindButton = btn
btn.Activated:Connect(function()
listeningForSpeedKey = nil
listeningForTPDownKey = true
keybindListenStartedAt = tick()
refreshAllSpeedKeybinds()
refreshTPDownKeybind()
end)
clearBtn.Activated:Connect(function()
listeningForSpeedKey = nil
listeningForTPDownKey = false
tpDownKeybind = nil
if tpDownKeybindButton then tpDownKeybindButton.Text = "None" end
refreshAllSpeedKeybinds()
refreshTPDownKeybind()
do end
end)
return row, btn
end
function speedKeybindRow(parent, labelText, keyId, order)
local row = baseRow(parent, labelText, order)
local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
btn.Name = "KeybindButton"
btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(8, 8, 12)
btn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.18
btn.Text = keyName(speedKeybinds[keyId])
btn.TextColor3 = COLORS.white
btn.TextSize = 11
btn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
btn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 56, 0, 24)
btn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -64, 0.5, -12)
btn.BorderSizePixel = 0
btn.ZIndex = 6
btn.AutoButtonColor = false
btn.Parent = row
corner(btn, 14)
stroke(btn, COLORS.strokeSoft, 1, 0.45)
local clearBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
clearBtn.Name = "ClearKeybindButton"
clearBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(8, 8, 12)
clearBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.18
clearBtn.Text = "×"
clearBtn.TextColor3 = COLORS.white
clearBtn.TextSize = 14
clearBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
clearBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 22, 0, 24)
clearBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -90, 0.5, -12)
clearBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
clearBtn.ZIndex = 6
clearBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
clearBtn.Parent = row
corner(clearBtn, 7)
stroke(clearBtn, COLORS.strokeSoft, 1, 0.45)
speedKeybindButtons[keyId] = btn
btn.Activated:Connect(function()
listeningForSpeedKey = keyId
listeningForTPDownKey = false
keybindListenStartedAt = tick()
refreshAllSpeedKeybinds()
refreshTPDownKeybind()
end)
clearBtn.Activated:Connect(function()
listeningForSpeedKey = nil
listeningForTPDownKey = false
speedKeybinds[keyId] = nil
if speedKeybindButtons[keyId] then speedKeybindButtons[keyId].Text = "None" end
refreshAllSpeedKeybinds()
refreshTPDownKeybind()
do end
end)
return row, btn
end
local normalModeValueLabel = nil
local laggerModeValueLabel = nil
local aimbotButtonLabel = nil
local aimbotSpeedLabel = nil
local laggerAimbotSpeedLabel = nil
local combatAimbotKeybindLabel = nil
function getAimbotModeDisplay()
return "Normal"
end
function refreshAimbotButtonLabel()
if aimbotButtonLabel then
aimbotButtonLabel.Text = "Bat Aimbot"
end
end
function refreshAimbotModeLabels()
if aimbotSpeedLabel then
aimbotSpeedLabel.Text = "Bat Aimbot Speed"
end
if laggerAimbotSpeedLabel then
laggerAimbotSpeedLabel.Text = "Lagger Speed"
end
if combatAimbotKeybindLabel then
combatAimbotKeybindLabel.Text = "Bat Aimbot"
end
refreshAimbotButtonLabel()
end
refreshSpeedModeRows = function()
if normalModeValueLabel then
normalModeValueLabel.Text = (currentSpeedMode == "Carry") and "Carry" or "Normal"
end
if laggerModeValueLabel then
laggerModeValueLabel.Text = (currentSpeedMode == "Lagger Carry") and "Lagger Carry" or "Lagger"
end
end
function modeDisplayRow(parent, order, side)
local row = baseRow(parent, "Mode", order)
row.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 34)
row.BackgroundTransparency = 0.3
local label = row:FindFirstChild("Label")
if label then
label.Text = "Mode"
label.TextSize = 11
label.Size = UDim2.new(1, -132, 1, 0)
label.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 0)
label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 255)
end
local value = Instance.new("TextLabel")
value.Name = "ModeValue"
value.BackgroundTransparency = 1
value.Text = side == "Normal" and "Normal" or "Lagger"
value.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
value.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
value.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.22
value.TextSize = 12
value.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
value.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
value.Position = UDim2.new(1, -150, 0, 0)
value.Size = UDim2.new(0, 138, 1, 0)
value.ZIndex = 6
value.Parent = row
local click = Instance.new("TextButton")
click.Name = "ModeClick"
click.BackgroundTransparency = 1
click.Text = ""
click.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
click.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
click.AutoButtonColor = false
click.ZIndex = 8
click.Parent = row
if side == "Normal" then
normalModeValueLabel = value
click.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
setSpeedMode(currentSpeedMode == "Normal" and "Carry" or "Normal")
end)
else
laggerModeValueLabel = value
click.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
setSpeedMode(currentSpeedMode == "Lagger" and "Normal" or "Lagger")
end)
end
refreshSpeedModeRows()
return row, value
end
-- Meridian Duels -- Aimbot Version selector: AIMBOT | AIMBOT V2
-- Same segmented-tab visual pattern as Auto Steal Mode, but the active
-- tab glows using the current theme color instead of a fixed white/gray
-- highlight, so switching themes also re-colors this selector.
_G.MeridianAimbotVersion = _G.MeridianAimbotVersion or "V1"

function aimbotVersionSelectorRow(parent, order)
local holder = Instance.new("Frame")
holder.Name = "Aimbot Version"
holder.BackgroundColor3 = COLORS.row
holder.BackgroundTransparency = 0.28
holder.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 42)
holder.BorderSizePixel = 0
holder.LayoutOrder = order
holder.ZIndex = 4
holder.ClipsDescendants = true
holder.Parent = parent
corner(holder, 9)
stroke(holder, COLORS.strokeSoft, 1.15, 0.38)

local TAB_GAP = 6
local TAB_COUNT = 3

local function makeVersionTab(name, index, label)
    local box = Instance.new("Frame")
    box.Name = name .. "Box"
    box.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(24, 24, 28)
    box.BackgroundTransparency = 0.08
    box.Size = UDim2.new(1/TAB_COUNT, -TAB_GAP, 1, -14)
    box.Position = UDim2.new(index/TAB_COUNT, TAB_GAP/2, 0, 7)
    box.BorderSizePixel = 0
    box.ZIndex = 6
    box.Parent = holder
    corner(box, 8)

    local boxGradient = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    boxGradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(24, 24, 28)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.fromRGB(46, 46, 52)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 22)),
    })
    boxGradient.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.08),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0.02),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0.08),
    })
    boxGradient.Parent = box

    local boxStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    boxStroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
    boxStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(90, 90, 98)
    boxStroke.Thickness = 1
    boxStroke.Transparency = 0.35
    boxStroke.Parent = box
    pcall(function() _G.MeridianAddRotatingGlow(boxStroke, 2.4) end)

    -- Meridian Duels -- extra glow layer that lights up in the current
    -- theme color only while this tab is the active one.
    local themeGlow = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    themeGlow.Name = "ThemeGlow"
    themeGlow.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
    themeGlow.Thickness = 2
    themeGlow.Transparency = 1
    themeGlow.Color = (_G.MeridianThemeColors and _G.MeridianThemeColors[_G.MeridianThemeName]) or Color3.fromRGB(90, 160, 255)
    themeGlow.Parent = box

    local txt = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    txt.Name = name .. "Text"
    txt.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    txt.Text = label
    txt.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 170, 178)
    txt.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0)
    txt.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.22
    txt.TextSize = 11
    txt.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    txt.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
    txt.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    txt.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    txt.ZIndex = 8
    txt.Parent = box

    local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
    btn.Name = name .. "Click"
    btn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    btn.Text = ""
    btn.AutoButtonColor = false
    btn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    btn.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    btn.ZIndex = 10
    btn.Parent = box
    return txt, btn, box, boxStroke, themeGlow
end

local v1Text, v1Click, v1Box, v1Stroke, v1Glow = makeVersionTab("AimbotV1", 0, "AIMBOT")
local v2Text, v2Click, v2Box, v2Stroke, v2Glow = makeVersionTab("AimbotV2", 1, "AIMBOT V2")
local v3Text, v3Click, v3Box, v3Stroke, v3Glow = makeVersionTab("AimbotV3", 2, "AIMBOT V3")

local function refreshAimbotVersionTheme()
    local tint = (_G.MeridianThemeColors and _G.MeridianThemeColors[_G.MeridianThemeName]) or Color3.fromRGB(90, 160, 255)
    v1Glow.Color = tint
    v2Glow.Color = tint
    v3Glow.Color = tint
end
_G.MeridianAimbotVersionGlowRefreshFns = _G.MeridianAimbotVersionGlowRefreshFns or {}
table.insert(_G.MeridianAimbotVersionGlowRefreshFns, refreshAimbotVersionTheme)

local function setAimbotVersion(version)
if version ~= "V2" and version ~= "V3" then
    version = "V1"
end
_G.MeridianAimbotVersion = version
selectedAimbotMode = "Normal"

-- Switch the live aimbot if one is currently running
if _G.MeridianNormalAimbotOn then
    if version == "V3" then
        if _G.MeridianStopNormalAimbot then pcall(_G.MeridianStopNormalAimbot) end
        if _G.MeridianStopAimbotV2     then pcall(_G.MeridianStopAimbotV2) end
        if _G.MeridianStartV3Aimbot    then pcall(_G.MeridianStartV3Aimbot) end
    elseif version == "V2" then
        if _G.MeridianStopNormalAimbot then pcall(_G.MeridianStopNormalAimbot) end
        if _G.MeridianStopV3Aimbot     then pcall(_G.MeridianStopV3Aimbot) end
        if _G.MeridianStartAimbotV2    then pcall(_G.MeridianStartAimbotV2) end
    else
        if _G.MeridianStopAimbotV2     then pcall(_G.MeridianStopAimbotV2) end
        if _G.MeridianStopV3Aimbot     then pcall(_G.MeridianStopV3Aimbot) end
        if _G.MeridianStartNormalAimbot then pcall(_G.MeridianStartNormalAimbot) end
    end
end

for _, pair in ipairs({
    {v1Text, v1Box, v1Stroke, v1Glow, version == "V1"},
    {v2Text, v2Box, v2Stroke, v2Glow, version == "V2"},
    {v3Text, v3Box, v3Stroke, v3Glow, version == "V3"},
}) do
    local text, box, strokeObj, glowObj, isActive = pair[1], pair[2], pair[3], pair[4], pair[5]
    tween(box, {
        BackgroundColor3 = isActive and Color3.fromRGB(58, 58, 64) or Color3.fromRGB(24, 24, 28),
        BackgroundTransparency = isActive and 0.02 or 0.08,
    }, 0.14)
    tween(strokeObj, {
        Color = isActive and Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 245) or Color3.fromRGB(90, 90, 98),
        Transparency = isActive and 0.08 or 0.35,
    }, 0.14)
    tween(glowObj, {
        Transparency = isActive and 0.15 or 1,
    }, 0.18)
    tween(text, {
        TextColor3             = isActive and COLORS.white or Color3.fromRGB(170, 170, 178),
        TextTransparency       = isActive and 0    or 0.18,
        TextStrokeTransparency = isActive and 0.2  or 0.38,
    }, 0.14)
end

if saveMeridianConfig then pcall(saveMeridianConfig) end
end

v1Click.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setAimbotVersion("V1") end)
v2Click.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setAimbotVersion("V2") end)
v3Click.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setAimbotVersion("V3") end)

refreshAimbotVersionTheme()
setAimbotVersion(_G.MeridianAimbotVersion)

return holder
end

        tween(box, {
            BackgroundColor3 = isActive and Color3.fromRGB(58, 58, 64) or Color3.fromRGB(24, 24, 28),

        tween(strokeObj, {
            Color = isActive and Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 245) or Color3.fromRGB(90, 90, 98),
            Transparency = isActive and 0.08 or 0.35,
        }, 0.14)
        tween(glowObj, {
            Transparency = isActive and 0.15 or 1,
        }, 0.18)
        tween(text, {
            TextColor3             = isActive and COLORS.white or Color3.fromRGB(170, 170, 178),
            TextTransparency       = isActive and 0    or 0.18,
            TextStrokeTransparency = isActive and 0.2  or 0.38,
        }, 0.14)
    end

    if saveMeridianConfig then pcall(saveMeridianConfig) end
end

function autoStealSelectorRow(parent, order)
local holder = Instance.new("Frame")
holder.Name = "Auto Steal Mode"
holder.BackgroundColor3 = COLORS.row
holder.BackgroundTransparency = 0.28
holder.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 42)
holder.BorderSizePixel = 0
holder.LayoutOrder = order
holder.ZIndex = 4
holder.ClipsDescendants = true
holder.Parent = parent
corner(holder, 9)
stroke(holder, COLORS.strokeSoft, 1.15, 0.38)

local TAB_GAP = 6
local TAB_COUNT = 4

-- ╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗
-- ║  V3 HALF-GRAB AUTO STEAL - ported from op_half_auto_grab      ║
-- ║  Same hold/fire-range behavior, wired into Meridian's own     ║
-- ║  StealBar so it fills like every other mode instead of        ║
-- ║  spawning a separate GUI.                                     ║
-- ╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝
_G.MeridianHalfGrab = _G.MeridianHalfGrab or {
    AutoStealEnabled = false,
    StealRadius = 62,
    HalfFireRange = 10,
    HalfHoldMin = 1.3,
    HalfHoldMax = 2.6,
    HalfEntryDelay = 0.3,
    Data = {},
}
local HG = _G.MeridianHalfGrab
local hgIsStealing, hgStealStartTime, hgAutoConn = false, nil, nil

local function hgIsMyPlotByName(plotName)
    local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
    local plot = plots and plots:FindFirstChild(plotName)
    if not plot then return false end
    local sign = plot:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
    local yourBase = sign and sign:FindFirstChild("YourBase")
    return yourBase and yourBase:IsA("BillboardGui") and yourBase.Enabled == true or false
end

local function hgFindNearestPrompt()
    local char = LP.Character
    local root = char and (char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or char:FindFirstChild("Torso"))
    local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
    if not root or not plots then return nil end

    local nearest, distance = nil, math.huge
    for _, plot in ipairs(plots:GetChildren()) do
        if plot:IsA("Model") and not hgIsMyPlotByName(plot.Name) then
            local podiums = plot:FindFirstChild("AnimalPodiums")
            if podiums then
                for _, podium in ipairs(podiums:GetChildren()) do
                    local base = podium:FindFirstChild("Base")
                    local spawn = base and base:FindFirstChild("Spawn")
                    if spawn then
                        local d = (spawn.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
                        if d <= HG.StealRadius and d < distance then
                            local found
                            local attachment = spawn:FindFirstChild("PromptAttachment")
                            local candidates = attachment and attachment:GetChildren() or spawn:GetDescendants()
                            for _, prompt in ipairs(candidates) do
                                if prompt:IsA("ProximityPrompt") and prompt.ActionText and prompt.ActionText:find("Steal") then
                                    found = prompt
                                    break
                                end
                            end
                            if found then nearest, distance = found, d end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return nearest
end

local function hgPromptDistance(prompt)
    local char = LP.Character
    local root = char and (char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or char:FindFirstChild("Torso"))
    if not root then return math.huge end
    local part = prompt.Parent
    if part and part:IsA("Attachment") then part = part.Parent end
    if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then return (part.Position - root.Position).Magnitude end
    local ok, position = pcall(function() return prompt.Parent and prompt.Parent.WorldPosition end)
    return ok and position and (position - root.Position).Magnitude or math.huge
end

local function hgExecuteSteal(prompt)
    if hgIsStealing then return end
    if not HG.Data[prompt] then
        HG.Data[prompt] = {hold = {}, trigger = {}, ready = true}
        if getconnections then
            for _, c in ipairs(getconnections(prompt.PromptButtonHoldBegan)) do
                if c.Function then table.insert(HG.Data[prompt].hold, c.Function) end
            end
            for _, c in ipairs(getconnections(prompt.Triggered)) do
                if c.Function then table.insert(HG.Data[prompt].trigger, c.Function) end
            end
        end
    end

    local data = HG.Data[prompt]
    if not data.ready then return end
    data.ready, hgIsStealing, hgStealStartTime = false, true, tick()

    task.spawn(function()
        for _, fn in ipairs(data.hold) do task.spawn(fn) end

        if _G.StealBar then
            pcall(function() _G.StealBar.SetState("STEALING") end)
        end

        -- Non-stop 0.2s fill -> 0 pulse, running independently so it
        -- never blocks/delays the fire-range check below.
        local holdMin, holdMax = HG.HalfHoldMin, HG.HalfHoldMax
        local fired, stealDone = false, false
        local FILL_TIME = 0.2

        local pulseThread = task.spawn(function()
            local FILL_TIME_LOCAL = FILL_TIME
            while not stealDone do
                local fillStart = tick()
                while not stealDone and tick() - fillStart < FILL_TIME_LOCAL do
                    local p = math.clamp((tick() - fillStart) / FILL_TIME_LOCAL, 0, 1)
                    if _G.StealBar then
                        pcall(function() _G.StealBar.SetProgress(p) end)
                    end
                    task.wait()
                end
                if stealDone then break end
                if _G.StealBar then
                    pcall(function() _G.StealBar.SetProgress(1) end)
                end
                task.wait()
                if _G.StealBar then
                    pcall(function() _G.StealBar.SetProgress(0) end)
                end
            end
        end)

        while true do
            local elapsed = tick() - hgStealStartTime
            if elapsed > holdMax or not prompt.Parent then break end

            if elapsed >= holdMin and hgPromptDistance(prompt) <= HG.HalfFireRange then
                if not fired then
                    task.wait(HG.HalfEntryDelay)
                    for _, fn in ipairs(data.trigger) do task.spawn(fn) end
                    fired = true
                end
                break
            end
            task.wait()
        end

        stealDone = true
        pcall(function() task.cancel(pulseThread) end)

        if _G.StealBar then
            pcall(function() _G.StealBar.SetProgress(0) end)
            pcall(function() _G.StealBar.SetState(HG.AutoStealEnabled and "READY" or "IDLE") end)
        end

        task.wait(0.05)
        data.ready, hgIsStealing = true, false
    end)
end

function _G.MeridianStartHalfGrab()
    HG.AutoStealEnabled = true
    if _G.StealBar then pcall(function() _G.StealBar.SetState("READY") end) end
    if hgAutoConn then return end
    hgAutoConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if HG.AutoStealEnabled and not hgIsStealing then
            local prompt = hgFindNearestPrompt()
            if prompt then hgExecuteSteal(prompt) end
        end
    end)
end

function _G.MeridianStopHalfGrab()
    HG.AutoStealEnabled = false
    if hgAutoConn then hgAutoConn:Disconnect(); hgAutoConn = nil end
    hgIsStealing = false
    if _G.StealBar then
        pcall(function() _G.StealBar.SetProgress(0) end)
        pcall(function() _G.StealBar.SetState("IDLE") end)
    end
end

local function makeTab(name, index, label)

    local box = Instance.new("Frame")
    box.Name = name .. "Box"
    box.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(24, 24, 28)
    box.BackgroundTransparency = 0.08
    box.Size = UDim2.new(1/TAB_COUNT, -TAB_GAP, 1, -14)
    box.Position = UDim2.new(index/TAB_COUNT, TAB_GAP/2, 0, 7)
    box.BorderSizePixel = 0
    box.ZIndex = 6
    box.Parent = holder
    corner(box, 8)

    local boxGradient = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    boxGradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(24, 24, 28)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.fromRGB(46, 46, 52)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 22)),
    })
    boxGradient.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.08),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0.02),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0.08),
    })
    boxGradient.Parent = box

    local boxStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    boxStroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
    boxStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(90, 90, 98)
    boxStroke.Thickness = 1
    boxStroke.Transparency = 0.35
    boxStroke.Parent = box
    pcall(function() _G.MeridianAddRotatingGlow(boxStroke, 2.4) end)

    local txt = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    txt.Name = name .. "Text"
    txt.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    txt.Text = label
    txt.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 170, 178)
    txt.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0)
    txt.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.22
    txt.TextSize = 11
    txt.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    txt.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
    txt.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    txt.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    txt.ZIndex = 8
    txt.Parent = box

    local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
    btn.Name = name .. "Click"
    btn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    btn.Text = ""
    btn.AutoButtonColor = false
    btn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    btn.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    btn.ZIndex = 10
    btn.Parent = box
    return txt, btn, box, boxStroke
end

local vynxText,  vynxClick,  vynxBox,  vynxStroke  = makeTab("Vynx",  0, "NORM")
local v1Text,    v1Click,    v1Box,    v1Stroke    = makeTab("V1",    1, "V1")
local normalText,normalClick,normalBox,normalStroke= makeTab("Normal",2, "V2")
local v3Text,    v3Click,    v3Box,    v3Stroke    = makeTab("HalfGrab", 3, "V3")

local function setMode(mode)
if mode ~= "V1" and mode ~= "Auto Steal V3" and mode ~= "Vynx" and mode ~= "HalfGrab" then
mode = "Vynx"
end
_G.MeridianStealRadii = _G.MeridianStealRadii or {["Auto Steal V3"]=63, V1=55, Vynx=60}
_G.MeridianStealRadii[selectedStealMode] = tonumber(autoStealRadius) or _G.MeridianStealRadii[selectedStealMode]
selectedStealMode = mode
autoStealRadius = _G.MeridianStealRadii[selectedStealMode] or 60
if autoStealRadiusBox then autoStealRadiusBox.Text = tostring(autoStealRadius) end
if _G.MeridianNormalAutoStealSetRadius then _G.MeridianNormalAutoStealSetRadius(_G.MeridianStealRadii["Auto Steal V3"] or 63) end
if _G.MeridianSemiAutoStealSetRadius then _G.MeridianSemiAutoStealSetRadius(_G.MeridianStealRadii.Semi or 62) end
if _G.MeridianV1AutoStealSetRadius then _G.MeridianV1AutoStealSetRadius(_G.MeridianStealRadii.V1 or 55) end
if _G.MeridianVynxSteal then _G.MeridianVynxSteal.StealRadius = _G.MeridianStealRadii.Vynx or 60 end
if _G.MeridianAutoStealSync then _G.MeridianAutoStealSync() end

if selectedStealMode == "Vynx" then
    if autoStealEnabled then
        if _G.MeridianStartVynxSteal then _G.MeridianStartVynxSteal() end
    else
        if _G.MeridianStopVynxSteal then _G.MeridianStopVynxSteal() end
    end
end

if mode ~= "Vynx" and _G.MeridianStopVynxSteal then _G.MeridianStopVynxSteal() end
if mode ~= "HalfGrab" and _G.MeridianStopHalfGrab then _G.MeridianStopHalfGrab() end
if mode == "HalfGrab" and autoStealEnabled and _G.MeridianStartHalfGrab then _G.MeridianStartHalfGrab() end

for _, pair in ipairs({
    {vynxText,   vynxBox,   vynxStroke,   mode == "Vynx"},
    {v1Text,     v1Box,     v1Stroke,     mode == "V1"},
    {normalText, normalBox, normalStroke, mode == "Auto Steal V3"},
    {v3Text,     v3Box,     v3Stroke,     mode == "HalfGrab"},
}) do
    local text, box, strokeObj, isActive = pair[1], pair[2], pair[3], pair[4]
    tween(box, {
        BackgroundColor3 = isActive and Color3.fromRGB(58, 58, 64) or Color3.fromRGB(24, 24, 28),
        BackgroundTransparency = isActive and 0.02 or 0.08,
    }, 0.14)
    tween(strokeObj, {
        Color = isActive and Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 245) or Color3.fromRGB(90, 90, 98),
        Transparency = isActive and 0.08 or 0.35,
    }, 0.14)
    tween(text, {
        TextColor3             = isActive and COLORS.white or Color3.fromRGB(170, 170, 178),
        TextTransparency       = isActive and 0    or 0.18,
        TextStrokeTransparency = isActive and 0.2  or 0.38,
    }, 0.14)
end
end

local function makeSettingRow(labelText, defaultVal, onChange)
    local row = baseRow(parent, labelText, 99)
    row.Visible = false
    row.LayoutOrder = 99

    local lbl = row:FindFirstChild("Label")
    if lbl then
        lbl.Text = labelText
    end

    local box = Instance.new("TextBox")
    box.Name = "SettingBox"
    box.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(8, 8, 12)
    box.BackgroundTransparency = 0.18
    box.BorderSizePixel = 0
    box.Position = UDim2.new(1, -68, 0.5, -12)
    box.Size = UDim2.new(0, 58, 0, 24)
    box.Text = tostring(defaultVal)
    box.TextColor3 = COLORS.white
    box.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    box.TextSize = 12
    box.ClearTextOnFocus = false
    box.ZIndex = 6
    box.Parent = row
    corner(box, 7)
    local boxStroke = stroke(box, COLORS.strokeSoft, 1, 0.45)
    pcall(function() _G.MeridianAddRotatingGlow(boxStroke, 2.0) end)

    box.FocusLost:Connect(function()
        local v = tonumber(box.Text)
        if v and v > 0 then
            box.Text = tostring(v)
            if onChange then onChange(v) end
        else
            box.Text = tostring(defaultVal)
        end
    end)
    return row, lbl, box
end

local row1, lbl1, box1 = makeSettingRow("Hold Duration", (_G.MeridianVynxSteal and _G.MeridianVynxSteal.StealDuration) or 1.4, nil)
row1.LayoutOrder = 4.2
_G.MeridianStealRow1 = row1

local row2, lbl2, box2 = makeSettingRow("Radius", _G.MeridianV1Steal and _G.MeridianV1Steal.StealRadius or 55, nil)
row2.LayoutOrder = 4.3
_G.MeridianStealRow2 = row2

local row3, lbl3, box3 = makeSettingRow("Stop At %", math.floor(((_G.MeridianGalaxyV3 and _G.MeridianGalaxyV3.FillCap) or 0.8) * 100 + 0.5), nil)
row3.LayoutOrder = 4.4
_G.MeridianStealRow3 = row3

local function updateModeSettings(mode)
    if mode == "V1" then

        row1.Visible = false
        row2.Visible = false
        row3.Visible = false
    elseif mode == "Auto Steal V3" then

        row1.Visible = false
        row2.Visible = false

        row3.Visible = true
        lbl3.Text = "Stop At %"
        box3.Text = tostring(math.floor(((_G.MeridianGalaxyV3 and _G.MeridianGalaxyV3.FillCap) or 0.8) * 100 + 0.5))
        box3.FocusLost:Connect(function()
            local v = tonumber(box3.Text)
            if v and v >= 5 and v <= 95 then
                if _G.MeridianGalaxyV3 then _G.MeridianGalaxyV3.FillCap = v / 100 end
            else
                box3.Text = tostring(math.floor(((_G.MeridianGalaxyV3 and _G.MeridianGalaxyV3.FillCap) or 0.8) * 100 + 0.5))
            end
        end)
    elseif mode == "Vynx" then

        row1.Visible = true
        lbl1.Text = "Hold Duration"
        box1.Text = tostring(_G.MeridianVynxSteal and _G.MeridianVynxSteal.StealDuration or 1.4)
        box1.FocusLost:Connect(function()
            local v = tonumber(box1.Text)
            if v and v > 0 then
                if _G.MeridianVynxSteal then _G.MeridianVynxSteal.StealDuration = v end
            else
                box1.Text = tostring(_G.MeridianVynxSteal and _G.MeridianVynxSteal.StealDuration or 1.4)
            end
        end)

        row2.Visible = true
        lbl2.Text = "Radius"
        box2.Text = tostring(_G.MeridianVynxSteal and _G.MeridianVynxSteal.StealRadius or 60)
        box2.FocusLost:Connect(function()
            local v = tonumber(box2.Text)
            if v and v > 0 then
                if _G.MeridianVynxSteal then _G.MeridianVynxSteal.StealRadius = v end
                if _G.MeridianStealRadii then _G.MeridianStealRadii.Vynx = v end
                autoStealRadius = v
            else
                box2.Text = tostring(_G.MeridianVynxSteal and _G.MeridianVynxSteal.StealRadius or 60)
            end
        end)

        row3.Visible = false
    elseif mode == "HalfGrab" then
        row1.Visible = false
        row2.Visible = false
        row3.Visible = false
    else

        row1.Visible = false
        row2.Visible = false
        row3.Visible = false
    end
end

v1Click.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
setMode("V1"); updateModeSettings("V1")
end)
normalClick.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
setMode("Auto Steal V3"); updateModeSettings("Auto Steal V3")
end)
vynxClick.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
setMode("Vynx"); updateModeSettings("Vynx")
if _G.MeridianStartVynxSteal and autoStealEnabled then _G.MeridianStartVynxSteal() end
end)
v3Click.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
setMode("HalfGrab"); updateModeSettings("HalfGrab")
if _G.MeridianStartHalfGrab and autoStealEnabled then _G.MeridianStartHalfGrab() end
end)
setMode(selectedStealMode)
updateModeSettings(selectedStealMode)
_G.MeridianRefreshStealModeSettings = function()
updateModeSettings(selectedStealMode)
end
return holder, setMode
end

local function setMode(mode)
_G.MeridianAntiRagdollMode = (mode == "V2") and "V2" or "V1"

local onV2 = _G.MeridianAntiRagdollMode == "V2"
tween(slide, {
Position = onV2 and UDim2.new(0.5, -1, 0, 4) or UDim2.new(0, 4, 0, 4)
}, 0.18)

tween(v1Text, {
TextTransparency = onV2 and 0.18 or 0,
TextStrokeTransparency = onV2 and 0.38 or 0.2
}, 0.14)

tween(v2Text, {
TextTransparency = onV2 and 0 or 0.18,
TextStrokeTransparency = onV2 and 0.2 or 0.38
}, 0.14)

if antiRagdollEnabled then
setAntiRagdoll(true)
end

do end
end

v1Click.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
setMode("V1")
end)

v2Click.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
setMode("V2")
end)

setMode(_G.MeridianAntiRagdollMode)
return holder, setMode
end

Movement = pages.MOVEMENT

-- Auto Carry Speed feature removed (auto-detect was unreliable and
-- caused an FPS drop) — Speed Configuration below still lets the user
-- switch modes manually via Carry/Lagger/Lagger Carry buttons.
-- ── SPEED CONFIGURATION (Normal + Lagger, dual-box, no separate sections) ──
local function dualBoxRow(parent, labelText, val1, val2, order, onChange1, onChange2)
    local row = baseRow(parent, labelText, order)
    local box2 = Instance.new("TextBox")
    box2.Name = "ValueBox2"
    box2.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(8,8,12)
    box2.BackgroundTransparency = 0.18
    box2.Text = tostring(val2 or "")
    box2.TextColor3 = COLORS.white
    box2.TextSize = 12
    box2.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    box2.ClearTextOnFocus = false
    box2.Size = UDim2.new(0,46,0,24)
    box2.Position = UDim2.new(1,-52,0.5,-12)
    box2.BorderSizePixel = 0
    box2.ZIndex = 6
    box2.Parent = row
    corner(box2,7)
    local s2 = stroke(box2,COLORS.strokeSoft,1,0.45)
    pcall(function() _G.MeridianAddRotatingGlow(s2,2.0) end)
    local box1 = Instance.new("TextBox")
    box1.Name = "ValueBox1"
    box1.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(8,8,12)
    box1.BackgroundTransparency = 0.18
    box1.Text = tostring(val1 or "")
    box1.TextColor3 = COLORS.white
    box1.TextSize = 12
    box1.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    box1.ClearTextOnFocus = false
    box1.Size = UDim2.new(0,46,0,24)
    box1.Position = UDim2.new(1,-102,0.5,-12)
    box1.BorderSizePixel = 0
    box1.ZIndex = 6
    box1.Parent = row
    corner(box1,7)
    local s1 = stroke(box1,COLORS.strokeSoft,1,0.45)
    pcall(function() _G.MeridianAddRotatingGlow(s1,2.0) end)
    box1.FocusLost:Connect(function()
        local v = tonumber(box1.Text)
        if v and v > 0 and v <= 250 then onChange1(v) else box1.Text = tostring(val1) end
    end)
    box2.FocusLost:Connect(function()
        local v = tonumber(box2.Text)
        if v and v > 0 and v <= 250 then onChange2(v) else box2.Text = tostring(val2) end
    end)
    return row, box1, box2
end

-- Meridian Duels -- Drop Mode selector: JUMP | STAND
-- JUMP maps to the current Drop behavior (runDrop). STAND is a
-- placeholder for now with no logic - selecting it only changes the
-- selector visuals below.
_G.MeridianDropMode = _G.MeridianDropMode or "Jump"

function dropModeSelectorRow(parent, order)
local holder = Instance.new("Frame")
holder.Name = "Drop Mode"
holder.BackgroundColor3 = COLORS.row
holder.BackgroundTransparency = 0.28
holder.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 42)
holder.BorderSizePixel = 0
holder.LayoutOrder = order
holder.ZIndex = 4
holder.ClipsDescendants = true
holder.Parent = parent
corner(holder, 9)
stroke(holder, COLORS.strokeSoft, 1.15, 0.38)

local TAB_GAP = 6
local TAB_COUNT = 2

local function makeDropTab(name, index, label)
    local box = Instance.new("Frame")
    box.Name = name .. "Box"
    box.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(24, 24, 28)
    box.BackgroundTransparency = 0.08
    box.Size = UDim2.new(1/TAB_COUNT, -TAB_GAP, 1, -14)
    box.Position = UDim2.new(index/TAB_COUNT, TAB_GAP/2, 0, 7)
    box.BorderSizePixel = 0
    box.ZIndex = 6
    box.Parent = holder
    corner(box, 8)

    local boxGradient = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    boxGradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(24, 24, 28)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.fromRGB(46, 46, 52)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 22)),
    })
    boxGradient.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.08),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0.02),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0.08),
    })
    boxGradient.Parent = box

    local boxStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    boxStroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
    boxStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(90, 90, 98)
    boxStroke.Thickness = 1
    boxStroke.Transparency = 0.35
    boxStroke.Parent = box
    pcall(function() _G.MeridianAddRotatingGlow(boxStroke, 2.4) end)

    local themeGlow = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    themeGlow.Name = "ThemeGlow"
    themeGlow.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
    themeGlow.Thickness = 2
    themeGlow.Transparency = 1
    themeGlow.Color = (_G.MeridianThemeColors and _G.MeridianThemeColors[_G.MeridianThemeName]) or Color3.fromRGB(90, 160, 255)
    themeGlow.Parent = box

    local txt = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    txt.Name = name .. "Text"
    txt.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    txt.Text = label
    txt.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 170, 178)
    txt.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0)
    txt.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.22
    txt.TextSize = 11
    txt.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    txt.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
    txt.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    txt.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    txt.ZIndex = 8
    txt.Parent = box

    local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
    btn.Name = name .. "Click"
    btn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    btn.Text = ""
    btn.AutoButtonColor = false
    btn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    btn.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    btn.ZIndex = 10
    btn.Parent = box
    return txt, btn, box, boxStroke, themeGlow
end

local jumpText, jumpClick, jumpBox, jumpStroke, jumpGlow = makeDropTab("DropJump", 0, "JUMP")
local standText, standClick, standBox, standStroke, standGlow = makeDropTab("DropStand", 1, "STAND")

local function refreshDropModeTheme()
    local tint = (_G.MeridianThemeColors and _G.MeridianThemeColors[_G.MeridianThemeName]) or Color3.fromRGB(90, 160, 255)
    jumpGlow.Color = tint
    standGlow.Color = tint
end
_G.MeridianDropModeGlowRefreshFns = _G.MeridianDropModeGlowRefreshFns or {}
table.insert(_G.MeridianDropModeGlowRefreshFns, refreshDropModeTheme)

local function setDropMode(mode)
if mode ~= "Stand" then
    mode = "Jump"
end
_G.MeridianDropMode = mode

for _, pair in ipairs({
    {jumpText, jumpBox, jumpStroke, jumpGlow, mode == "Jump"},
    {standText, standBox, standStroke, standGlow, mode == "Stand"},
}) do
    local text, box, strokeObj, glowObj, isActive = pair[1], pair[2], pair[3], pair[4], pair[5]
    tween(box, {
        BackgroundColor3 = isActive and Color3.fromRGB(58, 58, 64) or Color3.fromRGB(24, 24, 28),
        BackgroundTransparency = isActive and 0.02 or 0.08,
    }, 0.14)
    tween(strokeObj, {
        Color = isActive and Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 245) or Color3.fromRGB(90, 90, 98),
        Transparency = isActive and 0.08 or 0.35,
    }, 0.14)
    tween(glowObj, {
        Transparency = isActive and 0.15 or 1,
    }, 0.18)
    tween(text, {
        TextColor3             = isActive and COLORS.white or Color3.fromRGB(170, 170, 178),
        TextTransparency       = isActive and 0    or 0.18,
        TextStrokeTransparency = isActive and 0.2  or 0.38,
    }, 0.14)
end

if saveMeridianConfig then pcall(saveMeridianConfig) end
end

jumpClick.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setDropMode("Jump") end)
standClick.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setDropMode("Stand") end)

refreshDropModeTheme()
setDropMode(_G.MeridianDropMode)

return holder
end

section(Movement, "AUTO PLAY", 100)

do
    local duelModeRow = baseRow(Movement, "Duel Mode", 101)
    duelModeRow.Size = UDim2.new(1,-4,0,42)

    local lbl = duelModeRow:FindFirstChild("Label")
    if lbl then
        lbl.Text = "Duel Mode"
        lbl.Size = UDim2.new(0.35,0,1,0)
    end

    local halfBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
    halfBtn.Name = "HalfButton"
    halfBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0,58,0,28)
    halfBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1,-134,0.5,-14)
    halfBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    halfBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
    halfBtn.Text = "HALF"
    halfBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    halfBtn.TextSize = 10
    halfBtn.ZIndex = 20
    halfBtn.Parent = duelModeRow
    corner(halfBtn,6)
    stroke(halfBtn,COLORS.strokeSoft,1,0.45)

    local fullBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
    fullBtn.Name = "FullButton"
    fullBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0,58,0,28)
    fullBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1,-70,0.5,-14)
    fullBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    fullBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
    fullBtn.Text = "FULL"
    fullBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    fullBtn.TextSize = 10
    fullBtn.ZIndex = 20
    fullBtn.Parent = duelModeRow
    corner(fullBtn,6)
    stroke(fullBtn,COLORS.strokeSoft,1,0.45)

    local function refreshDuelBtns()
        halfBtn.TextColor3 = (_G.MeridianDuelMode == "half")
            and Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
            or Color3.fromRGB(140,140,140)

        fullBtn.TextColor3 = (_G.MeridianDuelMode == "full")
            and Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
            or Color3.fromRGB(140,140,140)

        if _G.MeridianAutoMoveCarrySpeedBox then
            local row = _G.MeridianAutoMoveCarrySpeedBox.Parent
            if row then
                row.Visible = (_G.MeridianDuelMode == "full")
            end
        end
    end

    _G.MeridianRefreshDuelModeButtons = refreshDuelBtns

    halfBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        _G.MeridianDuelMode = "half"
        refreshDuelBtns()
        if saveMeridianConfig then pcall(saveMeridianConfig) end
    end)

    fullBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        _G.MeridianDuelMode = "full"
        refreshDuelBtns()
        if saveMeridianConfig then pcall(saveMeridianConfig) end
    end)

    refreshDuelBtns()
end

function startPlayerESP()
if PlayerESP.enabled then return end
PlayerESP.enabled = true
function cleanup(plr)
local d=PlayerESP.playerData[plr]; if not d then return end
pcall(function() if d.highlight then d.highlight:Destroy() end end)
pcall(function() if d.billboard then d.billboard:Destroy() end end)
if d.conns then for _,c in ipairs(d.conns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end end
PlayerESP.playerData[plr]=nil
end
function setup(plr,char)
if not PlayerESP.enabled or plr==LP then return end
cleanup(plr)
local hrp=char and (char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart",5))
local head=char and (char:FindFirstChild("Head") or char:WaitForChild("Head",5))
if not hrp or not head then return end
local hl=Instance.new("Highlight")
hl.Name="MeridianDuelsESP"; hl.Adornee=char; hl.FillColor=(_G.MeridianThemeColors and _G.MeridianThemeColors[_G.MeridianThemeName]) or THEME_ACCENT; hl.FillTransparency=0.72
hl.OutlineColor=(_G.MeridianThemeColors and _G.MeridianThemeColors[_G.MeridianThemeName]) or THEME_ACCENT; hl.OutlineTransparency=0; hl.DepthMode=Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop; hl.Parent=char
local bb=Instance.new("BillboardGui")
bb.Name="MeridianDuelsESPTag"; bb.Adornee=head; bb.Size=UDim2.new(0,170,0,42); bb.StudsOffset=Vector3.new(0,2.8,0); bb.AlwaysOnTop=true; bb.LightInfluence=0; bb.Parent=head
local box=Instance.new("Frame",bb); box.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,1,0); box.BackgroundTransparency=1; box.BorderSizePixel=0
Instance.new("UICorner",box).CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,9)
local n=Instance.new("TextLabel",box); n.Size=UDim2.new(1,-8,0,19); n.Position=UDim2.new(0,4,0,1); n.BackgroundTransparency=1; n.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255); n.TextStrokeColor3=Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0); n.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBlack; n.TextSize=13; n.TextStrokeTransparency=0.08
local sub=Instance.new("TextLabel",box); sub.Size=UDim2.new(1,-8,0,17); sub.Position=UDim2.new(0,4,0,19); sub.BackgroundTransparency=1; sub.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255); sub.TextStrokeColor3=Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0); sub.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBlack; sub.TextSize=11; sub.TextStrokeTransparency=0.08
local conn=RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if not PlayerESP.enabled or not hrp.Parent then return end
local v=hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity or hrp.Velocity
n.Text=string.format("Speed: %.1f", Vector3.new(v.X,0,v.Z).Magnitude); sub.Text=plr.Name
end)
PlayerESP.playerData[plr]={highlight=hl,billboard=bb,conns={conn}}
end
for _,plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do if plr~=LP then if plr.Character then setup(plr,plr.Character) end; table.insert(PlayerESP.conns, plr.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(c) task.defer(setup,plr,c) end)) end end
table.insert(PlayerESP.conns, Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(plr) if plr~=LP then table.insert(PlayerESP.conns, plr.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(c) task.defer(setup,plr,c) end)) end end))
table.insert(PlayerESP.conns, Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(cleanup))
end
function stopPlayerESP()
PlayerESP.enabled=false
for _,c in ipairs(PlayerESP.conns or {}) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
PlayerESP.conns={}
for plr,d in pairs(PlayerESP.playerData or {}) do pcall(function() if d.highlight then d.highlight:Destroy() end end); pcall(function() if d.billboard then d.billboard:Destroy() end end) end
PlayerESP.playerData={}
end
function _aceEspColor()
return THEME_ACCENT or Color3.fromRGB(230,230,230)
end
function _safeDrawing(kind, props)
if not Drawing or not Drawing.new then return nil end
local ok, obj = pcall(function() return Drawing.new(kind) end)
if not ok or not obj then return nil end
for k,v in pairs(props or {}) do pcall(function() obj[k]=v end) end
return obj
end
function _cleanupBoxedESPPlayer(player)
local data = BoxedESPData[player]
if not data then return end
for _,obj in pairs(data) do
pcall(function()
obj.Visible = false
if obj.Remove then obj:Remove() end
end)
end
BoxedESPData[player] = nil
end
function _cleanupBoxedESP()
for player,_ in pairs(BoxedESPData) do _cleanupBoxedESPPlayer(player) end
end
function _updateBoxedESP()
local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
if not cam then return end
local anyOn = BoxedESPOptions.box or BoxedESPOptions.tracer
if not anyOn then
_cleanupBoxedESP()
return
end
for _,player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
if player == LP then continue end
local char = player.Character
local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
local head = char and char:FindFirstChild("Head")
if not root or not head then
_cleanupBoxedESPPlayer(player)
continue
end
local rootPos,onScreen = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(root.Position)
local headPos = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(head.Position + Vector3.new(0,0.55,0))
local data = BoxedESPData[player]
if not data then
data = {
box = _safeDrawing("Square",{Thickness=2,Filled=false,Transparency=1,Color=_aceEspColor()}),
tracer = _safeDrawing("Line",{Thickness=2,Transparency=1,Color=_aceEspColor()}),
}
BoxedESPData[player] = data
end
local color = _aceEspColor()
local height = math.abs(headPos.Y - rootPos.Y) * 2.15
if height < 20 or height ~= height then height = 65 end
local width = height / 2.15
local view = cam.ViewportSize
local centerX, centerY = view.X/2, view.Y/2
local targetX, targetY = rootPos.X, rootPos.Y + height/2
local targetVisible = onScreen and rootPos.Z > 0
if not targetVisible then
local dx = rootPos.X - centerX
local dy = rootPos.Y - centerY
if rootPos.Z <= 0 then
dx = -dx
dy = -dy
end
if math.abs(dx) < 1 and math.abs(dy) < 1 then
local rel = cam.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(root.Position)
dx = rel.X
dy = -rel.Y
if rootPos.Z <= 0 then
dx = -dx
dy = -dy
end
end
local edgePad = 10
local scaleX = (dx ~= 0) and ((view.X/2 - edgePad) / math.abs(dx)) or math.huge
local scaleY = (dy ~= 0) and ((view.Y/2 - edgePad) / math.abs(dy)) or math.huge
local scale = math.min(scaleX, scaleY)
if scale == math.huge or scale ~= scale then scale = 1 end
targetX = math.clamp(centerX + dx * scale, edgePad, view.X - edgePad)
targetY = math.clamp(centerY + dy * scale, edgePad, view.Y - edgePad)
end
if data.box then
data.box.Color = color
data.box.Size = Vector2.new(width,height)
data.box.Position = Vector2.new(rootPos.X - width/2, rootPos.Y - height/2)
data.box.Visible = false
end
if data.tracer then
data.tracer.Color = color
local localChar = LP.Character
local localRoot = localChar and localChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
local localHead = localChar and localChar:FindFirstChild("Head")
local fromX, fromY
if localRoot then
local localScreen = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(localRoot.Position)
fromX = localScreen.X
fromY = localScreen.Y + 15
end
if not fromX or not fromY then
fromX = cam.ViewportSize.X/2
fromY = cam.ViewportSize.Y - 88
end
data.tracer.From = Vector2.new(fromX, fromY)
data.tracer.To = Vector2.new(targetX, targetY)
data.tracer.Visible = BoxedESPOptions.tracer == true
end
end
end
function refreshBoxedESP()
local anyOn = BoxedESPOptions.box or BoxedESPOptions.tracer
if anyOn and not BoxedESPConn then
BoxedESPConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(_updateBoxedESP)
elseif (not anyOn) and BoxedESPConn then
BoxedESPConn:Disconnect()
BoxedESPConn = nil
_cleanupBoxedESP()
end
end
Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(_cleanupBoxedESPPlayer)

local function _vC3(t)
    return Color3.fromRGB(t[1], t[2], t[3])
end

function _v4mpClearSky()
    for _, child in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
        if child:GetAttribute("_MeridianDuelsSky") then
            pcall(function() child:Destroy() end)
        end
    end

    local terrain = workspace:FindFirstChildOfClass("Terrain")
    if terrain then
        for _, child in ipairs(terrain:GetChildren()) do
            if child:GetAttribute("_MeridianDuelsSky") then
                pcall(function() child:Destroy() end)
            end
        end
    end
end

function applyCustomSky(mode)
    _v4mpClearSky()

    local preset = SKY_PRESETS[mode]
    if not preset or preset.kind == "off" then
        Lighting.ClockTime = 14
        Lighting.Brightness = 2
        Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = Color3.fromRGB(127,127,127)
        Lighting.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(127,127,127)
        Lighting.FogEnd = 100000
        Lighting.GlobalShadows = true
        skyTheme = "Off"
        return
    end

    Lighting.FogStart = 0
    Lighting.FogEnd = 100000
    Lighting.FogColor = Color3.fromRGB(200,200,200)
    Lighting.ColorShift_Top = Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0)
    Lighting.ColorShift_Bottom = Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0)
    Lighting.GlobalShadows = true

    Lighting.ClockTime = preset.clock or 14
    Lighting.Brightness = preset.brightness or 2

    if preset.outAmb then
        Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = _vC3(preset.outAmb)
    end

    if preset.ambient then
        Lighting.Ambient = _vC3(preset.ambient)
    end

    if preset.sky then
        local skyInst = Instance.new("Sky")
        skyInst:SetAttribute("_MeridianDuelsSky", true)

        if preset.sky.stars then skyInst.StarCount = preset.sky.stars end
        if preset.sky.moon then skyInst.MoonAngularSize = preset.sky.moon end
        if preset.sky.sun then skyInst.SunAngularSize = preset.sky.sun end
        if preset.sky.moonTex then skyInst.MoonTextureId = "rbxasset://sky/moon.jpg" end

        skyInst.Parent = Lighting
    end

    if preset.atm then
        local atm = Instance.new("Atmosphere")
        atm:SetAttribute("_MeridianDuelsSky", true)
        atm.Density = preset.atm.dens or 0.3
        atm.Color = _vC3(preset.atm.color)
        atm.Decay = _vC3(preset.atm.decay)
        atm.Glare = preset.atm.glare or 1
        atm.Haze = preset.atm.haze or 1
        atm.Parent = Lighting
    end

    local terrain = workspace:FindFirstChildOfClass("Terrain")
    if preset.clouds and terrain then
        local clouds = Instance.new("Clouds")
        clouds:SetAttribute("_MeridianDuelsSky", true)
        clouds.Cover = preset.clouds.cover or 0.5
        clouds.Density = preset.clouds.dens or 0.5
        clouds.Color = _vC3(preset.clouds.color)
        clouds.Parent = terrain
    end

    skyTheme = mode
end

_G.MeridianStretchValue = 0.70
_G.MeridianStretchPreset = "Medium"

_G.MeridianStretchPresets = {
    ["Light"] = 0.85,
    ["Medium"] = 0.70,
    ["Strong"] = 0.55,
    ["Extreme"] = 0.40,
}

function enableStretchRez()
    fpsBoostEnabled = true
    if stretchRezConn then stretchRezConn:Disconnect(); stretchRezConn=nil end

    stretchRezConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if not fpsBoostEnabled then
            if stretchRezConn then stretchRezConn:Disconnect(); stretchRezConn=nil end
            return
        end

        local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera or workspace.CurrentCamera
        if cam then
            local sv = 0.70

            cam.CFrame = cam.CFrame * CFrame.new(
                0,0,0,
                1,0,0,
                0,sv,0,
                0,0,1
            )
        end
    end)
end

function disableStretchRez()
    fpsBoostEnabled = false
    if stretchRezConn then
        stretchRezConn:Disconnect()
        stretchRezConn = nil
    end
end

function _G.MeridianSetStretchPreset(name)
    local v = _G.MeridianStretchPresets[name]
    if not v then return end
    _G.MeridianStretchPreset = name
    _G.MeridianStretchValue = v
end

function disableCustomFov() fovEnabled=false; if customFovConn then customFovConn:Disconnect(); customFovConn=nil end; workspace.CurrentCamera.FieldOfView=fpsBoostEnabled and 107 or 70 end
_G.MeridianZombieAntiLag = _G.MeridianZombieAntiLag or {
    conn=nil,
    active=false,
    defBrightness=nil,
    defFog=nil,
    defDiffuse=nil,
    defSpecular=nil,
}

function _applyAntiLagObj(obj)
    pcall(function()
        if obj:IsA("BasePart") then
            obj.Material = Enum.Material.Plastic
            obj.Reflectance = 0
            obj.CastShadow = false
        elseif obj:IsA("Decal") or obj:IsA("Texture") then
            obj.Transparency = 1
        elseif obj:IsA("ParticleEmitter")
            or obj:IsA("Trail")
            or obj:IsA("Beam")
            or obj:IsA("Fire")
            or obj:IsA("Smoke")
            or obj:IsA("Sparkles") then
            obj.Enabled = false
        elseif obj:IsA("AnimationController") or obj:IsA("Animator") then
            for _,tr in ipairs(obj:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
                pcall(function() tr:Stop(0) end)
            end
        end
    end)
end

function enableAntiLag()
    antiLagVisualEnabled = true
    local S = _G.MeridianZombieAntiLag
    S.active = true
    S.defBrightness = S.defBrightness or Lighting.Brightness
    S.defFog = S.defFog or Lighting.FogEnd
    S.defDiffuse = S.defDiffuse or Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale
    S.defSpecular = S.defSpecular or Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale

    Lighting.GlobalShadows = false
    Lighting.FogEnd = 1e10
    Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale = 0
    Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale = 0

    for _,e in pairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
        pcall(function()
            if e:IsA("BlurEffect")
                or e:IsA("SunRaysEffect")
                or e:IsA("ColorCorrectionEffect")
                or e:IsA("BloomEffect")
                or e:IsA("DepthOfFieldEffect") then
                e.Enabled = false
            end
        end)
    end

    for _,obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
        _applyAntiLagObj(obj)
    end

    if S.conn then S.conn:Disconnect() end
    S.conn = workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
        if S.active then _applyAntiLagObj(obj) end
    end)
    antiLagDescConn = S.conn
end

function disableAntiLag()
    antiLagVisualEnabled = false
    local S = _G.MeridianZombieAntiLag
    S.active = false

    if S.conn then
        S.conn:Disconnect()
        S.conn = nil
    end
    antiLagDescConn = nil

    pcall(function()
        Lighting.GlobalShadows = true
        if S.defBrightness then Lighting.Brightness = S.defBrightness end
        if S.defFog then Lighting.FogEnd = S.defFog end
        if S.defDiffuse then Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale = S.defDiffuse end
        if S.defSpecular then Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale = S.defSpecular end

        for _,e in pairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
            pcall(function()
                if e:IsA("BlurEffect")
                    or e:IsA("SunRaysEffect")
                    or e:IsA("ColorCorrectionEffect")
                    or e:IsA("BloomEffect")
                    or e:IsA("DepthOfFieldEffect") then
                    e.Enabled = true
                end
            end)
        end
    end)
end

function applyKTMOptimization()
    enableAntiLag()
end

function enableNukeOptimizer()
nukeOptimiserEnabled=true; _aceNukeOn=true; applyKTMOptimization(); applyCustomSky("Off")
for _,c in ipairs(_aceNukeConns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end; _aceNukeConns={}
table.insert(_aceNukeConns, workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(o) if nukeOptimiserEnabled then _applyAntiLagObj(o) end end))
task.spawn(function() while nukeOptimiserEnabled do pcall(function() setfpscap(240) end); task.wait(3) end end)
end
function disableNukeOptimizer() nukeOptimiserEnabled=false; _aceNukeOn=false; for _,c in ipairs(_aceNukeConns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end; _aceNukeConns={} end
function enableNoCamCollision()
noCamCollisionEnabled=true; if noCamCollisionConn then noCamCollisionConn:Disconnect() end
noCamCollisionConn=RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
if not noCamCollisionEnabled then return end
local cam=workspace.CurrentCamera; local char=LP.Character; local hrp=char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"); if not cam or not hrp then return end
local params=RaycastParams.new(); params.FilterType=Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude; params.FilterDescendantsInstances=workspace.CurrentCamera and workspace.CurrentCamera:FindFirstChild("_MeridianAvatarOverlay") and {char,workspace.CurrentCamera:FindFirstChild("_MeridianAvatarOverlay")} or {char}; params.IgnoreWater=true
local res=workspace:Raycast(cam.CFrame.Position,(hrp.Position+Vector3.new(0,1.5,0))-cam.CFrame.Position,params)
local hit={}
if res and res.Instance and res.Instance:IsA("BasePart") then hit[res.Instance]=true; if noCamCollisionParts[res.Instance]==nil then noCamCollisionParts[res.Instance]=res.Instance.LocalTransparencyModifier end; res.Instance.LocalTransparencyModifier=1 end
for part,orig in pairs(noCamCollisionParts) do if not hit[part] then pcall(function() if part and part.Parent then part.LocalTransparencyModifier=orig end end); noCamCollisionParts[part]=nil end end
end)
end
function disableNoCamCollision() noCamCollisionEnabled=false; if noCamCollisionConn then noCamCollisionConn:Disconnect(); noCamCollisionConn=nil end; for p,orig in pairs(noCamCollisionParts) do pcall(function() if p and p.Parent then p.LocalTransparencyModifier=orig end end) end; noCamCollisionParts={} end
function enableCustomFont() customFontVisualEnabled=false; if V then V.customFontEnabled=false end end
function disableCustomFont() customFontVisualEnabled=false; if V then V.customFontEnabled=false end end
end

function enableNoCamCollision()
noCamCollisionEnabled = true
if noCamCollisionConn then noCamCollisionConn:Disconnect() end
noCamCollisionConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
if not noCamCollisionEnabled then
if noCamCollisionConn then noCamCollisionConn:Disconnect();noCamCollisionConn=nil end
return
end
local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
local char = LP.Character
if not cam or not char then return end
local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if not hrp then return end
local camPos = cam.CFrame.Position
local charPos = hrp.Position + Vector3.new(0,1.5,0)
local toChar = charPos - camPos
if toChar.Magnitude < 0.3 then return end
local params = RaycastParams.new()
params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
params.FilterDescendantsInstances = workspace.CurrentCamera and workspace.CurrentCamera:FindFirstChild("_MeridianAvatarOverlay") and {char, workspace.CurrentCamera:FindFirstChild("_MeridianAvatarOverlay")} or {char}
params.IgnoreWater = true
local hit = {}
local origin = camPos
local remaining = toChar
for _ = 1,12 do
if remaining.Magnitude < 0.2 then break end
local res = workspace:Raycast(origin,remaining,params)
if not res then break end
local part = res.Instance
if part and part:IsA("BasePart") and not part:IsDescendantOf(char) then
hit[part] = true
if noCamCollisionParts[part] == nil then noCamCollisionParts[part] = part.LocalTransparencyModifier end
part.LocalTransparencyModifier = 1
end
origin = res.Position + remaining.Unit * 0.02
remaining = charPos - origin
end
for part,orig in pairs(noCamCollisionParts) do
if not hit[part] then
pcall(function() if part and part.Parent then part.LocalTransparencyModifier = orig end end)
noCamCollisionParts[part] = nil
end
end
end)
end
function disableNoCamCollision()
noCamCollisionEnabled = false
if noCamCollisionConn then noCamCollisionConn:Disconnect();noCamCollisionConn=nil end
for part,orig in pairs(noCamCollisionParts) do
pcall(function() if part and part.Parent then part.LocalTransparencyModifier = orig end end)
end
noCamCollisionParts = {}
end
