    showNamed(Movement,"Anti Ragdoll",17)
    if _G.MeridianAntiRagdollSelector then
        _G.MeridianAntiRagdollSelector.Visible = false
        _G.MeridianAntiRagdollSelector.Size = UDim2.new(1,-4,0,0)
    end
    if _G.MeridianAntiRagdollArrow then
        _G.MeridianAntiRagdollArrow.Visible = false
    end

    if not byName(Combat,"BAT AIMBOT") then section(Combat,"BAT AIMBOT",5) end
    if aimbotMainRow then
        aimbotMainRow.LayoutOrder = 6
        local l = aimbotMainRow:FindFirstChild("Label")
        if l then l.Text = "Bat Aimbot" end
    end
    if aimbotSpeedRow then
        aimbotSpeedRow.LayoutOrder = 7
        local l = aimbotSpeedRow:FindFirstChild("Label")
        if l then l.Text = "Auto Bat Speed" end
    end
    if _G.MeridianNormalAutoSwingRow then _G.MeridianNormalAutoSwingRow.LayoutOrder = 8 end
    if _G.MeridianMirrorTPDownRow then
        _G.MeridianMirrorTPDownRow.LayoutOrder = 9
        local l = _G.MeridianMirrorTPDownRow:FindFirstChild("Label")
        if l then l.Text = "Mirror TP" end
    end

    if not byName(Combat,"TP Bat") then
        local r,setV = _G.MeridianActionToggleRow(Combat,"TP Bat",_G.MeridianAntiDesyncAimbotOn==true,11)
        local b = r and r:FindFirstChild("ToggleButton")
        if b then
            b.Activated:Connect(function()
                if _G.MeridianToggleAntiDesyncAimbot then pcall(_G.MeridianToggleAntiDesyncAimbot) end
                if setV then setV(_G.MeridianAntiDesyncAimbotOn==true) end
            end)
        end
    end

    if not byName(Combat,"AUTO PATH") then section(Combat,"AUTO PATH",12) end
    if not byName(Combat,"Auto Left") then
        local r,setV = _G.MeridianActionToggleRow(Combat,"Auto Left",autoLeftEnabled,13)
        local b=r and r:FindFirstChild("ToggleButton")
        if b then b.Activated:Connect(function()
            if _G.MeridianSetAutoLeft then _G.MeridianSetAutoLeft(not autoLeftEnabled) end
            if setV then setV(autoLeftEnabled) end
        end) end
    end
    if not byName(Combat,"Auto Right") then
        local r,setV = _G.MeridianActionToggleRow(Combat,"Auto Right",autoRightEnabled,14)
        local b=r and r:FindFirstChild("ToggleButton")
        if b then b.Activated:Connect(function()
            if _G.MeridianSetAutoRight then _G.MeridianSetAutoRight(not autoRightEnabled) end
            if setV then setV(autoRightEnabled) end
        end) end
    end

    if not byName(Combat,"BODY LOCK") then section(Combat,"BODY LOCK",19) end

    hideAll(Combat)
    showNamed(Combat,"AUTO STEAL",1,"STEAL CONFIGURATION")
    showNamed(Combat,"Auto Steal",4)
    if _G.MeridianStealSelector then
        _G.MeridianStealSelector.Visible = true
        _G.MeridianStealSelector.LayoutOrder=4.1
    end

    if selectedStealMode == "Vynx" then
        showObj(_G.MeridianStealRow1,4.2)
        showObj(_G.MeridianStealRow2,4.3)
    elseif selectedStealMode == "Auto Steal V3" then
        showObj(_G.MeridianStealRow3,4.4)
    end

    showNamed(Combat,"BAT AIMBOT",5)
    showObj(aimbotMainRow,6)
    if _G.MeridianAimbotVersionRow then
        _G.MeridianAimbotVersionRow.Visible = true
        _G.MeridianAimbotVersionRow.LayoutOrder = 6.5
    end
    showObj(aimbotSpeedRow,7)
    showObj(laggerAimbotSpeedRow,7.5)
    showObj(_G.MeridianNormalAutoSwingRow,8)
    showObj(_G.MeridianMirrorTPDownRow,9)

    showNamed(Combat,"TP BAT",10)
    showNamed(Combat,"TP Bat",11)
    if _G.MeridianTPBatVersionRow then
        _G.MeridianTPBatVersionRow.Visible = true
        _G.MeridianTPBatVersionRow.LayoutOrder = 11.5
    end
    showNamed(Combat,"AUTO PATH",12)
    showNamed(Combat,"Auto Left",13)
    showNamed(Combat,"Auto Right",14)
    showNamed(Combat,"COUNTERS",15)
    showNamed(Combat,"Bat Counter",16)
    showNamed(Combat,"Med Counter",17,"Medusa Counter")
    showNamed(Combat,"Auto Reset On Med Fling",18,"Reset After Med")
    showNamed(Combat,"Auto Instant Reset",18.5,"Insta Reset On Death")
    showNamed(Combat,"Safe Mode",18.8)
    showNamed(Combat,"Unwalk",19.5)
    showNamed(Combat,"No Player Collision",19.8)
    showNamed(Combat,"BODY LOCK",19)
    showNamed(Combat,"Body Lock",20)
    if _G.MeridianBodyLockSettings then
        _G.MeridianBodyLockSettings.LayoutOrder=21
        local l=_G.MeridianBodyLockSettings:FindFirstChild("Label")
        if l then l.Text="Lock Radius" end
    end

    hideAll(Keybinds)
    showNamed(Keybinds,"MOVEMENT KEYBINDS",1)
    showNamed(Keybinds,"Carry Speed Key",2)
    showNamed(Keybinds,"Lagger Mode Key",3)
    showNamed(Keybinds,"Lagger Carry Key",3.5)
    showNamed(Keybinds,"Drop Brainrot",4,"Drop Key")
    showNamed(Keybinds,"TP Down",5,"TP Down Key")
    showNamed(Keybinds,"COMBAT KEYBINDS",6)
    showNamed(Keybinds,"Normal Aimbot",7,"Bat Aimbot Key")
    showNamed(Keybinds,"TP BAT",8,"TP Bat Key")
    showNamed(Keybinds,"Auto Left",9,"Auto Left Key")
    showNamed(Keybinds,"Auto Right",10,"Auto Right Key")
    showNamed(Keybinds,"Instant Reset",11,"Insta Reset Key")

    if not byName(Keybinds,"INTERFACE KEYBINDS") then section(Keybinds,"INTERFACE KEYBINDS",12) end
    showNamed(Keybinds,"INTERFACE KEYBINDS",12)

    local uiToggleRow = byName(Settings,"Toggle UI")
    if uiToggleRow then
        uiToggleRow.Parent = Keybinds
        uiToggleRow.LayoutOrder = 13
        uiToggleRow.Visible = true
        local l=uiToggleRow:FindFirstChild("Label")
        if l then l.Text="UI Toggle Key" end
    end

    local bgPicker = byName(MenuCustomize,"Background Image Picker")
    local btnPicker = byName(MenuCustomize,"Mobile Button Image Picker")
    local colorPicker = byName(MenuCustomize,"ColorThemePicker")
    local barSize = byName(MenuCustomize,"Progress Bar Size")
    if bgPicker then bgPicker.Parent=Settings end
    if btnPicker then btnPicker.Parent=Settings end
    if colorPicker then colorPicker.Parent=Settings end
    if barSize then barSize.Parent=Settings end

    hideAll(MenuCustomize)

    local controllerBindButtons = {}
    _G.MeridianControllerBinds = _G.MeridianControllerBinds or {}
    local CB = _G.MeridianControllerBinds
    local _ctrlListeningBtn = nil
    local function ctrlBind(label, order, bindKey)
        local r=baseRow(MenuCustomize,label,order)

        local b=Instance.new("TextButton")
        b.Name="ControllerKeybindButton"
        b.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(8,8,12)
        b.BackgroundTransparency=0.18
        b.BorderSizePixel=0
        b.Text = (bindKey and CB[bindKey]) and tostring(CB[bindKey]):gsub("Enum.KeyCode.","") or "NONE"
        b.TextColor3=COLORS.white
        b.TextSize=12
        b.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBlack
        b.AutoButtonColor=false
        b.Size=UDim2.new(0,56,0,22)
        b.Position=UDim2.new(1,-64,0.5,-11)
        b.ZIndex=8
        b.Parent=r
        corner(b,7)
        stroke(b,COLORS.strokeSoft,1,0.45)

        local clear=Instance.new("TextButton")
        clear.Name="ClearControllerKeybindButton"
        clear.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(8,8,12)
        clear.BackgroundTransparency=0.08
        clear.BorderSizePixel=0
        clear.Text=""
        clear.AutoButtonColor=false
        clear.Size=UDim2.new(0,18,0,18)
        clear.Position=UDim2.new(1,-86,0.5,-9)
        clear.ZIndex=9
        clear.Parent=r
        corner(clear,999)
        stroke(clear,COLORS.strokeSoft,1,0.45)

        clear.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            b.Text="NONE"
            if bindKey then CB[bindKey]=nil end
            if saveMeridianConfig then pcall(saveMeridianConfig) end
        end)

        if bindKey then
            b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                _ctrlListeningBtn = b
                b.Text="..."
            end)
        end

        table.insert(controllerBindButtons,b)
        return r,b
    end

    -- Meridian Duels -- gamepad button picker: while a bind button is
    -- "listening" (just clicked), the next Gamepad1 button press is
    -- captured as that action's controller bind.
    UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gpe)
        if not _ctrlListeningBtn then return end
        if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad1 then return end
        local kc = input.KeyCode
        if kc == Enum.KeyCode.Unknown then return end
        _ctrlListeningBtn.Text = tostring(kc):gsub("Enum.KeyCode.","")
        for k, b in pairs(_G.MeridianControllerBindButtonMap or {}) do
            if b == _ctrlListeningBtn then
                CB[k] = kc
            end
        end
        _ctrlListeningBtn = nil
        if saveMeridianConfig then pcall(saveMeridianConfig) end
    end)

    local resetCtrl=baseRow(MenuCustomize,"RESET ALL CONTROLLER",1)
    resetCtrl:FindFirstChild("Label").Text="RESET ALL CONTROLLER"
    local resetCtrlClick=Instance.new("TextButton")
    resetCtrlClick.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,1,0)
    resetCtrlClick.BackgroundTransparency=1
    resetCtrlClick.Text=""
    resetCtrlClick.ZIndex=20
    resetCtrlClick.Parent=resetCtrl
    resetCtrlClick.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        for _,b in ipairs(controllerBindButtons) do b.Text="NONE" end
        for k in pairs(CB) do CB[k]=nil end
        if saveMeridianConfig then pcall(saveMeridianConfig) end
    end)

    section(MenuCustomize,"MOVEMENT CONTROLLER",2)
    _G.MeridianControllerBindButtonMap = _G.MeridianControllerBindButtonMap or {}
    local _r_speed, _b_speed = ctrlBind("Carry Speed Key",3,"SpeedToggle")
    _G.MeridianControllerBindButtonMap.SpeedToggle = _b_speed
    local _r_lagger, _b_lagger = ctrlBind("Lagger Mode Key",4,"LaggerToggle")
    _G.MeridianControllerBindButtonMap.LaggerToggle = _b_lagger
    local _r_laggerCarry, _b_laggerCarry = ctrlBind("Lagger Carry Key",4.5,"LaggerCarryToggle")
    _G.MeridianControllerBindButtonMap.LaggerCarryToggle = _b_laggerCarry
    local _r_drop, _b_drop = ctrlBind("Drop Key",5,"DropBrainrot")
    _G.MeridianControllerBindButtonMap.DropBrainrot = _b_drop
    local _r_tpdown, _b_tpdown = ctrlBind("TP Down Key",6,"TPDown")
    _G.MeridianControllerBindButtonMap.TPDown = _b_tpdown
    section(MenuCustomize,"COMBAT CONTROLLER",7)
    local _r_bataim, _b_bataim = ctrlBind("Bat Aimbot Key",8,"Aimbot")
    _G.MeridianControllerBindButtonMap.Aimbot = _b_bataim
    local _r_tpbat, _b_tpbat = ctrlBind("TP Bat Key",9,"AntiDesyncAimbot")
    _G.MeridianControllerBindButtonMap.AntiDesyncAimbot = _b_tpbat
    local _r_autoleft, _b_autoleft = ctrlBind("Auto Left Key",10,"AutoLeft")
    _G.MeridianControllerBindButtonMap.AutoLeft = _b_autoleft
    local _r_autoright, _b_autoright = ctrlBind("Auto Right Key",11,"AutoRight")
    _G.MeridianControllerBindButtonMap.AutoRight = _b_autoright
    local _r_instareset, _b_instareset = ctrlBind("Insta Reset Key",12,"InstaReset")
    _G.MeridianControllerBindButtonMap.InstaReset = _b_instareset
    section(MenuCustomize,"INTERFACE CONTROLLER",13)
    local _r_uitoggle, _b_uitoggle = ctrlBind("UI Toggle Key",14,"ToggleUI")
    _G.MeridianControllerBindButtonMap.ToggleUI = _b_uitoggle

    if not byName(Utility,"Animation Pack") then
        animationPackRow(Utility,7)
    end

    if not byName(Utility,"Show Tracer") then
        local r,setV=toggleRow(Utility,"Show Tracer",showTracerEnabled,3)
        local b=r and r:FindFirstChild("ToggleButton")
        if b then b.Activated:Connect(function()
            showTracerEnabled=not showTracerEnabled
            if BoxedESPOptions then BoxedESPOptions.tracer=showTracerEnabled end
            if refreshBoxedESP then pcall(refreshBoxedESP) end
            if setV then setV(showTracerEnabled) end
        end) end
        setTracerESPVisual=setV
    end

    if not byName(Utility,"Ragdoll Countdown") then
        local r,setV=toggleRow(Utility,"Ragdoll Countdown",ragdollCountdownEnabled,4)
        local b=r and r:FindFirstChild("ToggleButton")
        if b then b.Activated:Connect(function()
            ragdollCountdownEnabled=not ragdollCountdownEnabled
            if ragdollCountdownEnabled then pcall(hookRagdollCountdown,LP.Character) else pcall(stopRagdollCountdown) end
            if setV then setV(ragdollCountdownEnabled) end
        end) end
        setRagdollCountdownVisual=setV
    end

    if not byName(Utility,"Try Hard Animation") then
        local r,setV=toggleRow(Utility,"Try Hard Animation",hitHarderAnimEnabled,8)
        local b=r and r:FindFirstChild("ToggleButton")
        if b then b.Activated:Connect(function()
            hitHarderAnimEnabled=not hitHarderAnimEnabled
            if hitHarderAnimEnabled then pcall(enableHitHarderAnim) else pcall(disableHitHarderAnim) end
            if setV then setV(hitHarderAnimEnabled) end
        end) end
    end

    hideAll(Utility)
    showNamed(Utility,"ESP",1,"PLAYERS")

    for _,o in ipairs(Utility:GetChildren()) do
        if o.Name=="ESP" then o.Visible=true end
    end
    showNamed(Utility,"Show Tracer",3)
    showNamed(Utility,"Ragdoll Countdown",4)
    if not byName(Utility,"VISUAL") then section(Utility,"VISUAL",5) end
    showNamed(Utility,"VISUAL",5)
    showNamed(Utility,"Sky Theme",6,"Custom Sky")
    showNamed(Utility,"Animation Pack",7,"Anim Pack")
    showNamed(Utility,"Try Hard Animation",8)
    showNamed(Utility,"PERFORMANCE",9)
    showNamed(Utility,"Stretch Rez",10,"Stretch Res")
    showNamed(Utility,"Anti Lag",11,"Anti-Lag")
    showNamed(Utility,"FOV",12,"FOV Change")
    showNamed(Utility,"FOV Value",13)

    if not byName(Settings,"Circle Buttons") then
        local r,setV=_G.MeridianActionToggleRow(Settings,"Circle Buttons",_G.MeridianMobileButtonShape=="CIRCLE",2)
        local b=r and r:FindFirstChild("ToggleButton")
        if b then b.Activated:Connect(function()
            _G.MeridianMobileButtonShape=(_G.MeridianMobileButtonShape=="CIRCLE") and "ROUNDED" or "CIRCLE"
            if _G.MeridianApplyMobileButtonShape then _G.MeridianApplyMobileButtonShape() end
            if setV then setV(_G.MeridianMobileButtonShape=="CIRCLE") end
        end) end
    end

    if not byName(Settings,"Intro Song") then
        toggleRow(Settings,"Intro Song",false,8)
    end
    if not byName(Settings,"Background Color") then
        toggleRow(Settings,"Background Color",false,15)
    end

    hideAll(Settings)
    showNamed(Settings,"MOBILE BUTTONS",1)
    showNamed(Settings,"Circle Buttons",2)
    showNamed(Settings,"Hide Mobile Buttons",3,"Hide Mob Buttons")
    showNamed(Settings,"Mobile Buttons Size",4,"Button Size %")
    showNamed(Settings,"Move Buttons",5)
    showNamed(Settings,"Reset Mobile Buttons",6,"Reset Buttons")

    if not byName(Settings,"INTERFACE") then section(Settings,"INTERFACE",7) end
    showNamed(Settings,"INTERFACE",7)
    showNamed(Settings,"Intro Song",8)
    showNamed(Settings,"Intro",9)

    if not byName(Settings,"BACKGROUND") then section(Settings,"BACKGROUND",10) end
    showNamed(Settings,"BACKGROUND",10)
    if bgPicker then showObj(bgPicker,11) end

    if not byName(Settings,"BUTTONS IMAGE") then section(Settings,"BUTTONS IMAGE",12) end
    showNamed(Settings,"BUTTONS IMAGE",12)
    if btnPicker then showObj(btnPicker,13) end
    if colorPicker then showObj(colorPicker,14) end
    showNamed(Settings,"Background Color",15)

    if not byName(Settings,"UI SCALE") then section(Settings,"UI SCALE",16) end
    showNamed(Settings,"UI SCALE",16)
    showNamed(Settings,"GUI Scale",17,"UI Scale")
    if barSize then
        showObj(barSize,18,"Steal Bar Size")
    end
    showNamed(Settings,"Save Config Holder",19)
    showNamed(Settings,"Reset All Settings Holder",20)
end)
end)

UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
local isControllerInput = tostring(input.UserInputType):find("Gamepad") ~= nil
if gameProcessed and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard and not listeningForSpeedKey and not listeningForTPDownKey then return end
if UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then return end
if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard and not isControllerInput then return end
if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Unknown then return end

-- Meridian Duels -- gamepad controller binds (set in CTRL tab) trigger
-- the same toggle functions as their keyboard counterparts.
if isControllerInput and _G.MeridianControllerBinds then
local CBinds = _G.MeridianControllerBinds
if CBinds.SpeedToggle and input.KeyCode == CBinds.SpeedToggle then
toggleCarryMode()
return
end
if CBinds.LaggerToggle and input.KeyCode == CBinds.LaggerToggle then
toggleLaggerMode()
return
end
if CBinds.LaggerCarryToggle and input.KeyCode == CBinds.LaggerCarryToggle then
toggleLaggerCarryMode()
return
end
if CBinds.DropBrainrot and input.KeyCode == CBinds.DropBrainrot then
runDropBrainrot()
return
end
if CBinds.TPDown and input.KeyCode == CBinds.TPDown then
runTPFloor()
return
end
if CBinds.Aimbot and input.KeyCode == CBinds.Aimbot then
if _G.MeridianSafeModeIsLocked and _G.MeridianSafeModeIsLocked() then
if _G.MeridianSafeModeForceStop then _G.MeridianSafeModeForceStop("SAFE MODE LOCK") end
return
end
if _G.MeridianToggleSelectedAimbot then
_G.MeridianToggleSelectedAimbot()
elseif _G.MeridianStartNormalAimbot and _G.MeridianStopNormalAimbot then
if _G.MeridianNormalAimbotOn then _G.MeridianStopNormalAimbot() else _G.MeridianStartNormalAimbot() end
end
if _G.MeridianRefreshAimbotVisual then _G.MeridianRefreshAimbotVisual() end
return
end
if CBinds.AntiDesyncAimbot and input.KeyCode == CBinds.AntiDesyncAimbot then
if _G.MeridianSafeModeIsLocked and _G.MeridianSafeModeIsLocked() then
if _G.MeridianSafeModeForceStop then _G.MeridianSafeModeForceStop("SAFE MODE LOCK") end
return
end
if _G.MeridianToggleAntiDesyncAimbot then
_G.MeridianToggleAntiDesyncAimbot()
elseif _G.MeridianStartAntiDesyncAimbot and _G.MeridianStopAntiDesyncAimbot then
if _G.MeridianAntiDesyncAimbotOn then _G.MeridianStopAntiDesyncAimbot() else _G.MeridianStartAntiDesyncAimbot() end
end
return
end
if CBinds.AutoLeft and input.KeyCode == CBinds.AutoLeft then
if _G.MeridianSetAutoLeft then _G.MeridianSetAutoLeft(not autoLeftEnabled) end
return
end
if CBinds.AutoRight and input.KeyCode == CBinds.AutoRight then
if _G.MeridianSetAutoRight then _G.MeridianSetAutoRight(not autoRightEnabled) end
return
end
if CBinds.InstaReset and input.KeyCode == CBinds.InstaReset then
if _G.MeridianInstantReset then _G.MeridianInstantReset() end
return
end
if CBinds.ToggleUI and input.KeyCode == CBinds.ToggleUI then
if Main.Visible then
    _G.__MeridianDuelsMinimized = true
    MiniFrame.Visible = true
    _G.MeridianSlideClose()
else
    _G.__MeridianDuelsMinimized = false
    MiniFrame.Visible = false
    _G.MeridianSlideOpen(function()
        if _G.MeridianRefreshStealModeSettings then pcall(_G.MeridianRefreshStealModeSettings) end
    end)
end
return
end
end

if speedKeybinds.ToggleUI and input.KeyCode == speedKeybinds.ToggleUI then
if Main.Visible then
    _G.__MeridianDuelsMinimized = true
    MiniFrame.Visible = true
    _G.MeridianSlideClose(function()
        if _G.__MeridianMiniDashIn then pcall(_G.__MeridianMiniDashIn) end
    end)
else
    _G.__MeridianDuelsMinimized = false
    MiniFrame.Visible = false
    _G.MeridianSlideOpen(function()
        if _G.MeridianRefreshStealModeSettings then pcall(_G.MeridianRefreshStealModeSettings) end
    end)
end
pcall(saveMeridianConfig)
do end
return
end
if listeningForSpeedKey then
if tick() - (keybindListenStartedAt or 0) < 0.18 then return end
local targetKey = listeningForSpeedKey
if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Escape then
listeningForSpeedKey = nil
refreshAllSpeedKeybinds()
return
end
if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Backspace or input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Delete then
speedKeybinds[targetKey] = nil
else
for otherKeyId, boundKey in pairs(speedKeybinds) do
if otherKeyId ~= targetKey and boundKey == input.KeyCode then
speedKeybinds[otherKeyId] = nil
end
end
if tpDownKeybind == input.KeyCode then
tpDownKeybind = nil
refreshTPDownKeybind()
end
speedKeybinds[targetKey] = input.KeyCode
end
listeningForSpeedKey = nil
refreshAllSpeedKeybinds()
do end
return
end
if listeningForTPDownKey then
if tick() - (keybindListenStartedAt or 0) < 0.18 then return end
if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Escape then
listeningForTPDownKey = false
refreshTPDownKeybind()
return
end
if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Backspace or input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Delete then
tpDownKeybind = nil
else
for keyId, boundKey in pairs(speedKeybinds) do
if boundKey == input.KeyCode then
speedKeybinds[keyId] = nil
end
end
tpDownKeybind = input.KeyCode
end
listeningForTPDownKey = false
refreshAllSpeedKeybinds()
refreshTPDownKeybind()
do end
return
end
if speedKeybinds.SpeedToggle and input.KeyCode == speedKeybinds.SpeedToggle then
toggleCarryMode()
return
end
if speedKeybinds.LaggerToggle and input.KeyCode == speedKeybinds.LaggerToggle then
toggleLaggerMode()
return
end
if speedKeybinds.LaggerCarryToggle and input.KeyCode == speedKeybinds.LaggerCarryToggle then
toggleLaggerCarryMode()
return
end
if speedKeybinds.Aimbot and input.KeyCode == speedKeybinds.Aimbot then
if _G.MeridianSafeModeIsLocked and _G.MeridianSafeModeIsLocked() then
if _G.MeridianSafeModeForceStop then _G.MeridianSafeModeForceStop("SAFE MODE LOCK") end
return
end
if _G.MeridianToggleSelectedAimbot then
_G.MeridianToggleSelectedAimbot()
elseif _G.MeridianStartNormalAimbot and _G.MeridianStopNormalAimbot then
if _G.MeridianNormalAimbotOn then _G.MeridianStopNormalAimbot() else _G.MeridianStartNormalAimbot() end
end
if _G.MeridianRefreshAimbotVisual then _G.MeridianRefreshAimbotVisual() end
return
end
if speedKeybinds.AntiDesyncAimbot and input.KeyCode == speedKeybinds.AntiDesyncAimbot then
if _G.MeridianSafeModeIsLocked and _G.MeridianSafeModeIsLocked() then
if _G.MeridianSafeModeForceStop then _G.MeridianSafeModeForceStop("SAFE MODE LOCK") end
return
end
if _G.MeridianToggleAntiDesyncAimbot then
_G.MeridianToggleAntiDesyncAimbot()
elseif _G.MeridianStartAntiDesyncAimbot and _G.MeridianStopAntiDesyncAimbot then
if _G.MeridianAntiDesyncAimbotOn then _G.MeridianStopAntiDesyncAimbot() else _G.MeridianStartAntiDesyncAimbot() end
end
return
end
if speedKeybinds.DropBrainrot and input.KeyCode == speedKeybinds.DropBrainrot then
runDropBrainrot()
return
end
if speedKeybinds.AutoLeft and input.KeyCode == speedKeybinds.AutoLeft then
if _G.MeridianSetAutoLeft then _G.MeridianSetAutoLeft(not autoLeftEnabled) end
return
end
if speedKeybinds.AutoRight and input.KeyCode == speedKeybinds.AutoRight then
if _G.MeridianSetAutoRight then _G.MeridianSetAutoRight(not autoRightEnabled) end
return
end
if tpDownKeybind and input.KeyCode == tpDownKeybind then
runTPFloor()
return
end
end)
task.defer(function()
task.wait(0.15)
pcall(function()
for _, pageName in ipairs({"MOVEMENT","COMBAT"}) do
local page = pages[pageName]
if page then
for _, holder in ipairs(page:GetDescendants()) do
if holder:IsA("Frame") and holder:FindFirstChild("SelectedSlide") then
holder.Size = UDim2.new(1,-4,0,34)
holder.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0)
holder.BackgroundTransparency = 0.3

local slide = holder:FindFirstChild("SelectedSlide")
if slide then
slide.Size = UDim2.new(0.5,-4,1,-8)
slide.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
slide.BackgroundTransparency = 0.85
local s = slide:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
if s then
s.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
s.Thickness = 1
s.Transparency = 0.2
end
end

for _, child in ipairs(holder:GetChildren()) do
if child:IsA("TextLabel") or child:IsA("TextButton") then
child.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
child.TextSize = 12
end
end
end
end

for _, obj in ipairs(page:GetDescendants()) do
if obj:IsA("TextButton") and obj.Name == "ArrowButton" then
obj.Position = UDim2.new(1,-108,0.5,-13)
obj.Size = UDim2.new(0,38,0,26)
obj.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(8,8,12)
obj.BackgroundTransparency = 0.18
obj.Text = "▼"
obj.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
obj.TextSize = 22
obj.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
local c = obj:FindFirstChildOfClass("UICorner")
if c then c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,7) end
end
end
end
end
end)
end)

_G.__MeridianDuelsSetupStealBar = function()
local RunService   = game:GetService("RunService")
local UIS = UserInputService
local TS = TweenService
local Stats        = game:GetService("Stats")
local existingStealBar = LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui") and LP.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("StealBarGui")
if existingStealBar then existingStealBar:Destroy() end
local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
gui.Name = "StealBarGui"
gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
gui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
gui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
safeParentGui(gui)
_G.MeridianInfoBarGui = gui
function drag(frame)
local dragging, dragStart, startPos = false, nil, nil
frame.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
if _G.MeridianGuiLocked == true then return end
if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
dragging = true
dragStart = input.Position
startPos = frame.Position
input.Changed:Connect(function()
if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then dragging = false end
end)
end
end)
UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
if dragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
local delta = input.Position - dragStart
frame.Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + delta.X, startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y)
end
end)
end

local function normalizeStealUIImage(value)
value = tostring(value or "")
if value == "" then return "" end
if value:match("^rbxassetid://%d+$") then return value end
local id = value:match("(%d+)")
return id and ("rbxassetid://" .. id) or ""
end
function _G.MeridianApplyStealUIImage()
local image = normalizeStealUIImage(_G.MeridianStealUIImage)
stealBgImage.Image = image
stealBgImage.ImageColor3 = _G.MeridianThemeColors[_G.MeridianThemeName] or _G.MeridianThemeColors.WHITE
stealBgImage.Visible = image ~= ""
pbFrame.BackgroundTransparency = image ~= "" and 0.18 or 0
end
_G.MeridianApplyStealUIImage()
Instance.new("UICorner", pbFrame).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 15)
local pbSt = Instance.new("UIStroke", pbFrame)
pbSt.Color = Color3.fromRGB(55, 55, 55)
pbSt.Thickness = 1
pcall(function() _G.MeridianAddRotatingGlow(pbSt, 2.2) end)
drag(pbFrame)
pbFrame:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Position"):Connect(function()
savedProgressBarPositionTable = udim2ToTable(pbFrame.Position)
end)

-- Animated flowing gradient layer (primary effect — no flat color)
local progressFillGrad = Instance.new("UIGradient", progressFill)
progressFillGrad.Name = "MeridianFillGrad"
progressFillGrad.Rotation = 0
progressFillGrad.Color = ColorSequence.new({
    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0,   Color3.fromRGB(180, 180, 200)),
    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.3, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)),
    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.7, Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 220)),
    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1,   Color3.fromRGB(160, 160, 180)),
})
progressFillGrad.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0,    0.15),
    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.25, 0.02),
    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.75, 0.02),
    NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1,    0.15),
})

-- Animate the gradient offset so it flows left-to-right continuously
task.spawn(function()
    local t = 0
    while progressFill and progressFill.Parent do
        t = (t + 0.016) % 2
        pcall(function()
            progressFillGrad.Offset = Vector2.new(math.sin(t * math.pi) * 0.4, 0)
        end)
        task.wait(0.016)
    end
end)

-- Helper: current theme color
local function _getFillThemeColor()
    return (_G.MeridianThemeColors and _G.MeridianThemeColors[_G.MeridianThemeName])
        or Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 235)
end

-- Helper: lighten a Color3
local function _lightenColor(c, amount)
    amount = amount or 0.35
    return Color3.new(
        math.min(1, c.R + amount),
        math.min(1, c.G + amount),
        math.min(1, c.B + amount)
    )
end

-- Helper: darken a Color3
local function _darkenColor(c, amount)
    amount = amount or 0.2
    return Color3.new(
        math.max(0, c.R - amount),
        math.max(0, c.G - amount),
        math.max(0, c.B - amount)
    )
end

-- Apply current theme to fill gradient
local function _applyFillTheme()
    local tc    = _getFillThemeColor()
    local light = _lightenColor(tc, 0.3)
    local dark  = _darkenColor(tc, 0.15)
    progressFillGrad.Color = ColorSequence.new({
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0,   light),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, dark),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1,   light),
    })
    progressFill.BackgroundColor3 = tc
    tipGlow.BackgroundColor3      = light
    progressShine.BackgroundColor3 = light
end
_applyFillTheme()

-- Shimmer loop: sweeps while bar is active
local _shimmerConn = nil
local function _startShimmer()
    if _shimmerConn then return end
    local t = 0
    _shimmerConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
        if not (progressFill and progressFill.Parent) then
            if _shimmerConn then _shimmerConn:Disconnect() end
            _shimmerConn = nil
            return
        end
        t = t + dt * 0.72
        local x = (t % 1) * 1.55 - 0.55
        progressShimmer.Position = UDim2.new(x, 0, 0, 0)
        local pulse = 0.25 + 0.2 * math.sin(tick() * 5)
        tipGlow.BackgroundTransparency = pulse
    end)
end
local function _stopShimmer()
    if _shimmerConn then
        _shimmerConn:Disconnect()
        _shimmerConn = nil
    end
    progressShimmer.Position = UDim2.new(-0.55, 0, 0, 0)
    tipGlow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
end

-- Theme switcher hook
_G.MeridianRefreshFillBarTheme = function()
    _applyFillTheme()
end

local progressPct = Instance.new("TextLabel", pbFrame)
progressPct.Visible = false
progressPct.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
progressPct.BackgroundTransparency = 1
progressPct.Text = ""

local barState = "IDLE"
function setBarState(state)
    barState = state
    local tc = _getFillThemeColor()
    if state == "STEALING" then
        _startShimmer()
        _applyFillTheme()
        TS:Create(stealLbl, TweenInfo.new(0.18), {TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)}):Play()
        TS:Create(pbSt, TweenInfo.new(0.22), {
            Color       = tc,
            Transparency = 0.05,
            Thickness   = 1.8
        }):Play()
        TS:Create(pbgStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.22), {
            Color       = tc,
            Transparency = 0.2,
        }):Play()
        TS:Create(stealWhiteGlow, TweenInfo.new(0.28, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
            BackgroundTransparency = 0.72
        }):Play()
        TS:Create(progressRadLbl, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {TextColor3 = tc}):Play()
        progressRadLbl.Text = "STEALING"
    elseif state == "READY" then
        _startShimmer()
        _applyFillTheme()
        TS:Create(stealLbl, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)}):Play()
        TS:Create(pbSt, TweenInfo.new(0.25), {
            Color       = Color3.fromRGB(90, 90, 90),
            Transparency = 0,
            Thickness   = 1
        }):Play()
        TS:Create(pbgStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.25), {
            Color       = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
            Transparency = 0.65,
        }):Play()
        TS:Create(stealWhiteGlow, TweenInfo.new(0.3), {BackgroundTransparency = 1}):Play()
        TS:Create(progressRadLbl, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)}):Play()
        progressRadLbl.Text = "SEARCHING"
    elseif state == "STOLE" then
        _stopShimmer()
        _applyFillTheme()
        TS:Create(stealLbl, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)}):Play()
        TS:Create(pbSt, TweenInfo.new(0.22), {
            Color       = tc,
            Transparency = 0.05,
            Thickness   = 1.8
        }):Play()
        TS:Create(progressRadLbl, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {TextColor3 = tc}):Play()
        progressRadLbl.Text = "STOLE!"
        -- brief flash then restore stroke
        task.delay(1.2, function()
            if barState == "STOLE" then
                TS:Create(pbSt, TweenInfo.new(0.4), {
                    Color       = Color3.fromRGB(55, 55, 55),
                    Transparency = 0,
                    Thickness   = 1
                }):Play()
            end
        end)
    else
        _stopShimmer()
        TS:Create(stealLbl, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 150)}):Play()
        TS:Create(pbSt, TweenInfo.new(0.25), {
            Color       = Color3.fromRGB(55, 55, 55),
            Transparency = 0,
            Thickness   = 1
        }):Play()
        TS:Create(pbgStroke, TweenInfo.new(0.25), {
            Color       = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
            Transparency = 0.65,
        }):Play()
        TS:Create(stealWhiteGlow, TweenInfo.new(0.3), {BackgroundTransparency = 1}):Play()
        TS:Create(progressRadLbl, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 150)}):Play()
        progressRadLbl.Text = "IDLE"
    end
end

local _executorNameCache = nil
local function getExecutorNameShort()
if not _executorNameCache then
pcall(function()
local name = nil
if identifyexecutor then
local a, b = identifyexecutor()
name = a or b
end
if (not name or name == "") and getexecutorname then name = getexecutorname() end
if not name or name == "" then
if syn then name = "Synapse" end
if fluxus then name = "Fluxus" end
if is_sirhurt_closure then name = "Sirhurt" end
if secure_load then name = "Sentinel" end
if KRNL_LOADED then name = "Krnl" end
if getgenv and getgenv().IS_VORB_LOADED then name = "Vorb" end
end
if type(name) ~= "string" or name == "" then name = "Unknown" end
name = tostring(name):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
if #name > 12 then name = name:sub(1, 12) end
_executorNameCache = name
end)
_executorNameCache = _executorNameCache or "Unknown"
end
return _executorNameCache
end

local function getUniverseClockShort()
local t = os.date("*t")
local h = t.hour
local m = t.min
local ampm = "AM"
if h >= 12 then
ampm = "PM"
if h > 12 then h = h - 12 end
end
if h == 0 then h = 12 end
return string.format("%d:%02d %s", h, m, ampm)
end

task.spawn(function()
local _fpsFrames, _fpsAccum, _fpsValue = 0, 0, 60
local _fpsConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
_fpsFrames = _fpsFrames + 1
_fpsAccum = _fpsAccum + dt
if _fpsAccum >= 0.5 then
_fpsValue = math.floor(_fpsFrames / _fpsAccum + 0.5)
_fpsFrames, _fpsAccum = 0, 0
end
end)
while pbFrame and pbFrame.Parent do
local ping = 0
pcall(function()
local stat = Stats.Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]
if stat then ping = tonumber(stat:GetValue()) or 0 end
end)
stealLbl.Text = string.format("%dms \xe2\x94\x83%dfps", math.floor(ping + 0.5), _fpsValue)
task.wait(0.5)
end
if _fpsConn then _fpsConn:Disconnect() end
end)

local StealBar = {}
function StealBar.SetProgress(p)
    p = math.clamp(p, 0, 1)
    -- Smooth tween the fill width
    TS:Create(progressFill, TweenInfo.new(0.12, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
        Size = UDim2.new(p, 0, 1, 0)
    }):Play()
    -- Refresh theme color every tick so switching themes live-updates the bar
    _applyFillTheme()
    if barState == "STEALING" then
        stealWhiteGlow.BackgroundTransparency = 0.84 - (p * 0.26)
        progressFill.BackgroundTransparency   = 0.08 - (p * 0.06)
        pbSt.Transparency = math.max(0.02, 0.18 - (p * 0.16))
        -- scale shimmer speed proportional to fill progress
        -- (handled in loop via t increment - no change needed)
    end
end
function StealBar.Reset()
    StealBar.SetProgress(0)
    setBarState("IDLE")
end
function StealBar.SetState(state)
    setBarState(state)
end
setBarState("IDLE")
_G.StealBar = StealBar
end
_G.__MeridianDuelsSetupStealBar()
if _G.MeridianAutoStealSync then task.defer(_G.MeridianAutoStealSync) end
_G.__MeridianDuelsSetupMinimizeToggle = function()
_G.__MeridianDuelsMinimized = false

-- Meridian Duels -- Bounce-dash EXIT for the main GUI: when closed via
-- the (-) button, the whole window dashes out toward whichever side of
-- the screen (left/right) is closer, mirroring the mini pill's dash-IN
-- entrance effect but in reverse (a small anticipation pull-back, then
-- a fast dash off-screen in the correct direction).
local function playMainDashOut(onComplete)
    local startX      = Main.Position.X.Offset
    local startXScale = Main.Position.X.Scale
    local startY      = Main.Position.Y
    local screenW     = Gui.AbsoluteSize.X > 0 and Gui.AbsoluteSize.X or 800
    local mainW       = Main.AbsoluteSize.X > 0 and Main.AbsoluteSize.X or 356
    local startAbsX   = (startXScale * screenW) + startX
    local toLeft      = startAbsX <= (screenW - startAbsX - mainW)

    -- Straight dash off-screen — no rotation, no squish, no cartoon
    local exitOffsetX = toLeft
        and (startX - (startAbsX + mainW + 40))
        or  (startX + (screenW - startAbsX + 40))

    -- Meridian Duels -- PERF FIX: the old version tweened every single
    -- descendant (ImageLabel/TextLabel/Frame/ScrollingFrame) inside
    -- Main on every close - hundreds of Tween objects created at once,
    -- which stalled the client and made the first click feel like it
    -- needed two taps. Sliding Main itself off-screen already hides
    -- everything inside it, so only Main needs to move/fade - nothing
    -- inside it needs its own tween.
    TweenService:Create(Main,
        TweenInfo.new(0.16, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In),
        {
            Position = UDim2.new(startXScale, exitOffsetX, startY.Scale, startY.Offset),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        }
    ):Play()

    task.delay(0.17, function()
        -- Reset position for next open. No per-descendant transparency
        -- reset needed since we never touched their transparency.
        Main.Position = UDim2.new(startXScale, startX, startY.Scale, startY.Offset)
        Main.Rotation = 0
        Main.BackgroundTransparency = 0
        if onComplete then onComplete() end
    end)
end
_G.__MeridianMainDashOut = playMainDashOut

-- Meridian Duels -- Bounce-dash entrance for the mini pill: when the
-- GUI is minimized, the pill flies in from whichever side of the
-- screen (left/right) is closer to its landing spot, overshoots past
-- its target, then springs back - instead of just popping into place.
local function playMiniDashIn()
    local landX = MiniFrame.Position.X.Offset
    local landXScale = MiniFrame.Position.X.Scale
    local landY = MiniFrame.Position.Y
    local screenW = Gui.AbsoluteSize.X > 0 and Gui.AbsoluteSize.X or 800
    local pillW = MiniFrame.AbsoluteSize.X > 0 and MiniFrame.AbsoluteSize.X or 148

    -- Figure out the pill's absolute landing X, then decide whether the
    -- nearer edge is the left or right side of the screen.
    local landAbsX = (landXScale * screenW) + landX
    local distToLeft = landAbsX
    local distToRight = screenW - (landAbsX + pillW)
    local fromLeft = distToLeft <= distToRight

    local startOffsetX = fromLeft
        and (landX - (landAbsX + pillW + 40))
        or  (landX + (screenW - landAbsX + 40))
    local overshootOffsetX = fromLeft and (landX + 18) or (landX - 18)

    MiniFrame.Position = UDim2.new(landXScale, startOffsetX, landY.Scale, landY.Offset)
    MiniFrame.Rotation = fromLeft and -8 or 8
    MiniFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.08

    -- Fast dash to just past the landing point...
    local dashTween = TweenService:Create(MiniFrame,
        TweenInfo.new(0.16, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
        {
            Position = UDim2.new(landXScale, overshootOffsetX, landY.Scale, landY.Offset),
            Rotation = fromLeft and 4 or -4,
        }
    )
    dashTween:Play()

    dashTween.Completed:Connect(function()
        -- ...then a springy bounce-back settle into the final resting spot.
        TweenService:Create(MiniFrame,
            TweenInfo.new(0.38, Enum.EasingStyle.Elastic, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
            {
                Position = UDim2.new(landXScale, landX, landY.Scale, landY.Offset),
                Rotation = 0,
            }
        ):Play()
    end)
end
_G.__MeridianMiniDashIn = playMiniDashIn

local _closeClickBusy = false
Close.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
if _closeClickBusy then return end
_closeClickBusy = true
_G.__MeridianDuelsMinimized = not _G.__MeridianDuelsMinimized
if _G.__MeridianDuelsMinimized then
    MiniFrame.Visible = true
    _G.MeridianSlideClose(function() _closeClickBusy = false end)
else
    MiniFrame.Visible = false
    _G.MeridianSlideOpen(function()
        _closeClickBusy = false
        if _G.MeridianRefreshStealModeSettings then pcall(_G.MeridianRefreshStealModeSettings) end
    end)
end
-- Save menu state
pcall(saveMeridianConfig)
end)
end

    frame.BackgroundTransparency = 0

    layer1.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    layer1.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    layer1.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop

    layer1.Parent = frame

    local layer2 = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    layer2.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    layer2.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    layer2.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop
    layer2.ZIndex = 1
    layer2.ImageTransparency = 1
    layer2.Parent = frame

    layer1.Image = Images[1]
    layer2.Image = Images[1]

    local continueText = Instance.new("TextLabel", screenGui)
    continueText.Size = UDim2.new(0, 300, 0, 60)
    continueText.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -150, 0.5, -30)

    continueText.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
    continueText.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.5
    continueText.ZIndex = 100
    continueText.Visible = true

    local skipRequested = false
    local sound = nil
    pcall(function()
        local url = "https://files.catbox.moe/iyw1cb.mp3"
        local fileName = "MeridianNewIntroSong.mp3"
        if not isfile(fileName) then

        end
        sound = Instance.new("Sound")
        sound.SoundId = getcustomasset(fileName)
        sound.Volume = 1
        sound.Parent = workspace
        sound:Play()
    end)

    local function finishIntro()
        if skipRequested then return end
        skipRequested = true
        if sound then
            pcall(function() sound:Stop() end)
            pcall(function() sound:Destroy() end)
        end
        pcall(function() screenGui:Destroy() end)
        Main.Size = FULL_MAIN_SIZE
        Main.Visible = not wasMinimizedBeforeIntro
        MiniFrame.Visible = wasMinimizedBeforeIntro
    end

    local tapCatcher = Instance.new("TextButton")
    tapCatcher.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    tapCatcher.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    tapCatcher.Text = ""
    tapCatcher.AutoButtonColor = false
    tapCatcher.ZIndex = 50
    tapCatcher.Parent = screenGui
    tapCatcher.MouseButton1Click:Connect(finishIntro)

    task.delay(9, function()
        if not skipRequested then finishIntro() end
    end)

    local FPS = 24
    local currentIdx = 1
    local total = #Images
    local frameDelay = 1 / FPS
    local fadeTime = frameDelay * 0.9

    while not skipRequested do
        local nextIdx = currentIdx + 1
        if nextIdx > total then nextIdx = 1 end

        local behind, front
        if layer1.ZIndex == 1 then
            behind, front = layer1, layer2
        else
            behind, front = layer2, layer1
        end

        behind.Image = Images[nextIdx]
        behind.ImageTransparency = 1
        behind.ZIndex = 2
        front.ZIndex = 1

        TweenService:Create(behind, TweenInfo.new(fadeTime, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {ImageTransparency = 0}):Play()

        currentIdx = nextIdx

        local startTime = tick()
        while tick() - startTime < frameDelay do
            if skipRequested then break end
            task.wait(0.02)
        end
    end
end

if setFOVVisual then setFOVVisual(fovEnabled) end
if fovEnabled then enableCustomFov() else disableCustomFov() end
if setNoCamCollisionVisual then setNoCamCollisionVisual(noCamCollisionEnabled) end
if noCamCollisionEnabled then enableNoCamCollision() else disableNoCamCollision() end
if type(applyCustomSky) == "function" then
applyCustomSky((skyTheme and skyTheme ~= "") and skyTheme or "Off")
end
if skyValueLabel then skyValueLabel.Text = skyTheme or "Off" end
end)
do end
end

task.wait(0.35)
if type(applyCustomSky) == "function" then
pcall(function() applyCustomSky((skyTheme and skyTheme ~= "") and skyTheme or "Off") end)
end
if skyValueLabel then skyValueLabel.Text = skyTheme or "Off" end

_G.MeridianAutoTPRestoreWanted = _G.MeridianAutoTPRestoreWanted or false
_G.MeridianAutoTPRestoreBlockedUntil = _G.MeridianAutoTPRestoreBlockedUntil or 0
function aceAnyAimbotActive()
return (_G.MeridianNormalAimbotOn == true) or (_G.MeridianAntiDesyncAimbotOn == true)
end
_G.MeridianStopAutoTPForAction = function()
if autoTPEnabled then
_G.MeridianAutoTPRestoreWanted = true
_G.MeridianAutoTPRestoreBlockedUntil = tick() + 0.35
stopAutoTP()
if setAutoTPVisual then setAutoTPVisual(false) end
end
end
function aceTryRestoreAutoTP()
if not _G.MeridianAutoTPRestoreWanted then return end
if tick() < (_G.MeridianAutoTPRestoreBlockedUntil or 0) then return end
if aceAnyAimbotActive() then return end
if dropBrainrotActive then return end
_G.MeridianAutoTPRestoreWanted = false
startAutoTP()
if setAutoTPVisual then setAutoTPVisual(true) end
do end
end
RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(aceTryRestoreAutoTP)
_G._oldMeridianStopNormalAimbot = _G.MeridianStopNormalAimbot
_G.MeridianStopNormalAimbot = function(...)
local r = {_G._oldMeridianStopNormalAimbot(...)}
_G.MeridianAutoTPRestoreBlockedUntil = tick() + 0.05
task.delay(0.08, aceTryRestoreAutoTP)
return unpack(r)
end
_G.MeridianNormalAimbotStop = _G.MeridianStopNormalAimbot
_G._oldMeridianStopAntiDesyncAimbot = _G.MeridianStopAntiDesyncAimbot
_G.MeridianStopAntiDesyncAimbot = function(...)
local r = {_G._oldMeridianStopAntiDesyncAimbot(...)}
_G.MeridianAutoTPRestoreBlockedUntil = tick() + 0.05
task.delay(0.08, aceTryRestoreAutoTP)
return unpack(r)
end
task.spawn(function()
local wasDropping = false
while task.wait(0.05) do
if dropBrainrotActive then
wasDropping = true
elseif wasDropping then
wasDropping = false
_G.MeridianAutoTPRestoreBlockedUntil = tick() + 0.05
task.delay(0.08, aceTryRestoreAutoTP)
end
end
end)
function aceRepairKeybinds()
for keyId, defaultKey in pairs(DEFAULT_SPEED_KEYBINDS) do
if speedKeybinds[keyId] == nil or speedKeybinds[keyId] == Enum.KeyCode.Unknown then
speedKeybinds[keyId] = defaultKey
end
end

if speedKeybinds.AntiDesyncAimbot == nil then
speedKeybinds.AntiDesyncAimbot = Enum.KeyCode.V
end
if tpDownKeybind == Enum.KeyCode.Unknown then tpDownKeybind = DEFAULT_TP_DOWN_KEYBIND end
if refreshAllSpeedKeybinds then refreshAllSpeedKeybinds() end
if refreshTPDownKeybind then refreshTPDownKeybind() end
end
_G._oldSaveMeridianConfigStable = saveMeridianConfig
saveMeridianConfig = function()
aceRepairKeybinds()
return _G._oldSaveMeridianConfigStable()
end
aceRepairKeybinds()
task.defer(function()
task.wait(0.2)
aceRepairKeybinds()
do end
end)
task.defer(function()
task.wait(0.35)
local TS = game:GetService("TweenService")
for _, oldName in ipairs({"MeridianMobileButtons", "MeridianMobileButtons"}) do
local old = PlayerGui:FindFirstChild(oldName)
if old then old:Destroy() end
end
local mobileGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
mobileGui.Name = "MeridianMobileButtons"
mobileGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
mobileGui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
mobileGui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
mobileGui.DisplayOrder = 1000
safeParentGui(mobileGui)
_G.MeridianMobileGui = mobileGui
mobileGui.Enabled = not (_G.MeridianHideMobileButtons == true)

-- Re-apply the saved custom font now that the mobile/floating buttons
-- GUI actually exists. The earlier restore call (right after intro,
-- before this GUI was built) skipped this root entirely since
-- _G.MeridianMobileGui was still nil at that point - the floating
-- buttons were never included in that pass and always rendered with
-- the default font regardless of what was saved.
if (_G.MeridianCustomFontSelected or "None") ~= "None" then
    task.defer(function()
        pcall(function() _G.MeridianApplyCustomFont(_G.MeridianCustomFontSelected) end)
    end)
end
_G.MeridianMobileButtonRefs = {}
local mobileButtons = _G.MeridianMobileButtonRefs
function _G.MeridianApplyMobileButtonsHidden()
local shouldHide = (_G.MeridianHideMobileButtons == true)

local g = nil
pcall(function()
    g = PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("MeridianMobileButtons")
end)

if not g then
    pcall(function()
        local cg = game:GetService("CoreGui")
        g = cg and cg:FindFirstChild("MeridianMobileButtons")
    end)
end

if not g and gethui then
    pcall(function()
        local hui = gethui()
        g = hui and hui:FindFirstChild("MeridianMobileButtons")
    end)
end

if not g and mobileGui and mobileGui.Parent then
    g = mobileGui
end

if g then
    g.Enabled = not shouldHide
end

if setHideMobileButtonsVisual then
    pcall(setHideMobileButtonsVisual, shouldHide)
end
end
local function normalizeMobileImage(value)
value = tostring(value or ""):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
if value == "" then return "" end
local id = value:match("^(%d+)$")
if id then return "rbxassetid://" .. id end
local embedded = value:match("[?&]id=(%d+)") or value:match("/(%d+)[/%?]?")
if embedded then return "rbxassetid://" .. embedded end
return value
end
function _G.MeridianApplyMobileButtonImage()
local image = normalizeMobileImage(_G.MeridianMobileButtonImage)
for _, entry in pairs(mobileButtons) do
local btn = entry and entry.btn
local img = btn and btn:FindFirstChild("MobileBackgroundImage")
if img then
img.Image = image
img.ImageColor3 = _G.MeridianThemeColors[_G.MeridianThemeName] or _G.MeridianThemeColors.WHITE
img.Visible = image ~= ""
end
end
end
function _G.MeridianApplyMobileButtonShape()
local shape = tostring(_G.MeridianMobileButtonShape or "ROUNDED"):upper()
if shape ~= "CIRCLE" and shape ~= "ROUNDED" then shape = "ROUNDED" end
_G.MeridianMobileButtonShape = shape
for key, entry in pairs(mobileButtons) do
local btn = entry and entry.btn
local holder = entry and entry.holder
if btn and holder then
holder.Size = UDim2.new(0,58,0,58)
btn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
btn.TextSize = 10
local textOverlay = btn:FindFirstChild("MobileButtonText")
if textOverlay then
textOverlay.Font = btn.Font
textOverlay.TextSize = btn.TextSize
end
local radius = UDim.new(0,10)
if shape == "CIRCLE" then radius = UDim.new(0.5, 0) end -- true circle
local c = btn:FindFirstChildOfClass("UICorner")
if c then c.CornerRadius = radius end
local img = btn:FindFirstChild("MobileBackgroundImage")
local ic = img and img:FindFirstChildOfClass("UICorner")
if ic then ic.CornerRadius = radius end
local noneBg = btn:FindFirstChild("ButtonNoneBackground")
local nc = noneBg and noneBg:FindFirstChildOfClass("UICorner")
if nc then nc.CornerRadius = radius end
local glow = holder:FindFirstChild("Glow")
local gc = glow and glow:FindFirstChildOfClass("UICorner")
if gc then gc.CornerRadius = radius end
local dot = btn:FindFirstChild("Dot")
if dot then dot.Visible = false end
end
end
end
function _G.MeridianApplyMobileButtonSize()
_G.MeridianMobileButtonScale = math.clamp(tonumber(_G.MeridianMobileButtonScale) or 1.00, 0.30, 6.00)
for _, entry in pairs(mobileButtons) do
local holder = entry and entry.holder
if holder then
local sc = holder:FindFirstChild("MobileButtonScale") or Instance.new("UIScale")
sc.Name = "MobileButtonScale"
sc.Scale = _G.MeridianMobileButtonScale
sc.Parent = holder
end
end
pcall(function()
local gui = PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("MeridianDuelsUI") or PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("MeridianDuelsHub") or PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("CyberHub")
local root = gui or PlayerGui
for _, obj in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
if obj.Name == "Mobile Buttons Size" then
local valueBox = obj:FindFirstChild("Value")
if valueBox and valueBox:IsA("TextLabel") then
valueBox.Text = string.format("%.2f", _G.MeridianMobileButtonScale)
end
end
end
end)
end
local function setActive(btn, state)
if not btn then return end
local pressed = btn:GetAttribute("MeridianMobilePressed") == true
state = (state == true) or pressed
local visualState = state and "on" or "off"
if btn:GetAttribute("MeridianMobileVisualState") == visualState then return end
btn:SetAttribute("MeridianMobileVisualState", visualState)
local holder = btn.Parent
local glow = holder and holder:FindFirstChild("Glow")
local st = btn:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
local textOverlay = btn:FindFirstChild("MobileButtonText")
local noneBg = btn:FindFirstChild("ButtonNoneBackground")
local noneGrad = noneBg and noneBg:FindFirstChild("ButtonNoneGradient")
local dot = btn:FindFirstChild("Dot")
if noneGrad then noneGrad.Enabled = not state end
if noneBg then
TS:Create(noneBg,TweenInfo.new(0.18),{
BackgroundColor3 = state and Color3.fromRGB(220,220,220) or Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
}):Play()
end
if textOverlay then
TS:Create(textOverlay, TweenInfo.new(0.18), {
TextColor3 = state and Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0) or Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255),
}):Play()
end
if st then
TS:Create(st, TweenInfo.new(0.18), {
Color = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255),
Thickness = 1.1,
Transparency = state and 0.35 or 0.55,
}):Play()
end
if dot and dot.Visible then
TS:Create(dot, TweenInfo.new(0.16), {
BackgroundColor3 = state and Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0) or Color3.fromRGB(80,80,95),
}):Play()
end
if glow then
glow.Visible = false
glow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
local gs = glow:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
if gs then gs.Transparency = 1 end
end
end
local function pulse(btn)
if not btn then return end
btn:SetAttribute("MeridianMobilePressed", true)
setActive(btn, true)
task.delay(0.18, function()
if btn and btn.Parent then
btn:SetAttribute("MeridianMobilePressed", false)
setActive(btn, false)
end
end)
end
local function makeButton(key, label, pos, onPress, customSize)
local holder = Instance.new("Frame")
holder.Name = "MBH_" .. key
holder.Size = customSize or UDim2.new(0,58,0,58)
local savedPos = _G.MeridianMobileButtonPositions and _G.MeridianMobileButtonPositions[key]
holder.Position = (type(savedPos) == "table") and tableToUDim2(savedPos, pos) or pos
holder.BackgroundTransparency = 1
holder.BorderSizePixel = 0
holder.ZIndex = 1000
holder.Active = true
holder.Parent = mobileGui
local glow = Instance.new("Frame", holder)
glow.Name = "Glow"
glow.Size = UDim2.new(1, 4, 1, 4)
glow.Position = UDim2.new(0, -2, 0, -2)
glow.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
glow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
glow.BorderSizePixel = 0
glow.ZIndex = 1000
Instance.new("UICorner", glow).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 13)
local glowStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", glow)
glowStroke.Name = "MeridianPressFlash"
glowStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
glowStroke.Thickness = 0.8
glowStroke.Transparency = 1
glowStroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
local btn = Instance.new("TextButton", holder)
btn.Name = "MB_" .. key
btn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
btn.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
btn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
btn.BorderSizePixel = 0
btn.Text = label
btn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
btn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
btn.TextSize = 11
btn.TextWrapped = true
btn.AutoButtonColor = false
btn.ZIndex = 1002
btn.Active = true
Instance.new("UICorner", btn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,10)

local noneBg = Instance.new("ImageLabel", btn)
noneBg.Name = "ButtonNoneBackground"
noneBg.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,1,0)
noneBg.Position = UDim2.new(0,0,0,0)
noneBg.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
noneBg.BorderSizePixel = 0
noneBg.ZIndex = btn.ZIndex
noneBg.Parent = btn
Instance.new("UICorner", noneBg).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,10)

local noneGrad = Instance.new("UIGradient", noneBg)
noneGrad.Name = "ButtonNoneGradient"
noneGrad.Rotation = 25
noneGrad.Color = ColorSequence.new({
ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0,Color3.fromRGB(22,22,25)),
ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.18,Color3.fromRGB(2,2,3)),
ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.82,Color3.fromRGB(2,2,3)),
ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1,Color3.fromRGB(22,22,25))
})

local bgImage = Instance.new("ImageLabel", btn)
bgImage.Name = "MobileBackgroundImage"
bgImage.BackgroundTransparency = 1
bgImage.BorderSizePixel = 0
bgImage.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
bgImage.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
bgImage.Image = normalizeMobileImage(_G.MeridianMobileButtonImage)
bgImage.ImageColor3 = _G.MeridianThemeColors[_G.MeridianThemeName] or _G.MeridianThemeColors.WHITE
bgImage.ImageTransparency = 0.18
bgImage.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop
bgImage.Visible = bgImage.Image ~= ""
bgImage.ZIndex = btn.ZIndex
local bgCorner = Instance.new("UICorner", bgImage)
bgCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0,10)
local textOverlay = Instance.new("TextLabel", btn)
textOverlay.Name = "MobileButtonText"
textOverlay.BackgroundTransparency = 1
textOverlay.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 1, -8)
textOverlay.Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0, 4)
textOverlay.Text = label
textOverlay.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
textOverlay.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0)
textOverlay.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.22
textOverlay.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
textOverlay.TextSize = 11
textOverlay.TextWrapped = true
textOverlay.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
textOverlay.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
textOverlay.ZIndex = btn.ZIndex + 1
local mobileTextStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", textOverlay)
mobileTextStroke.Name = "MeridianTextOutline"
mobileTextStroke.Thickness = 1.4
mobileTextStroke.Transparency = 0
btn.TextTransparency = 1
local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", btn)
stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
stroke.Thickness = 1.1
stroke.Transparency = 0.55
stroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
pcall(function() _G.MeridianAddRotatingGlow(stroke, 1.8) end)
local pressing, dragging = false, false
local pressPos, holderStart = nil, nil
btn.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
pressing = true
dragging = false
pressPos = i.Position
holderStart = holder.Position
-- Only a squish/press animation here — do NOT flip the "on" visual
-- yet. setActive(true) used to fire immediately on first touch
-- contact, so a graze or a finger sliding past the button flashed
-- it "active" before any tap/drag decision was made. The real
-- active-state visual is set below in InputEnded (via onPress) or
-- restored from the actual feature flag (keepOn), never from touch
-- contact alone.
pcall(function()
TS:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.08, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 4),
Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -4)
}):Play()
end)
end
end)
btn.InputEnded:Connect(function(i)
if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
local delta = pressPos and (i.Position - pressPos) or Vector3.zero
local moved = math.abs(delta.X) > 12 or math.abs(delta.Y) > 12
if pressing and not dragging and not moved and _G.MeridianGuiLocked == true then pcall(onPress, btn) end
if dragging then do end end
pressing = false
dragging = false
btn:SetAttribute("MeridianMobilePressed", false)
pcall(function()
TS:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.10, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),
Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
}):Play()
end)
task.delay(0.08, function()
if btn and btn.Parent then
local keepOn = false
if key == "autoLeft" then keepOn = autoLeftEnabled == true
elseif key == "autoRight" then keepOn = autoRightEnabled == true
elseif key == "aimbot" then keepOn = (_G.MeridianNormalAimbotOn == true)
elseif key == "antiDesync" then keepOn = _G.MeridianAntiDesyncAimbotOn == true
elseif key == "carry" then keepOn = currentSpeedMode == "Carry"
elseif key == "laggerNormal" then keepOn = currentSpeedMode == "Lagger"
elseif key == "laggerCarry" then keepOn = currentSpeedMode == "Lagger Carry"
end
setActive(btn, keepOn)
end
end)
end
end)
UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
if _G.MeridianGuiLocked == true or not pressing then return end
if i.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement and i.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
local delta = i.Position - pressPos
if not dragging and (math.abs(delta.X) > 15 or math.abs(delta.Y) > 15) then dragging = true end
if dragging then
holder.Position = UDim2.new(holderStart.X.Scale, holderStart.X.Offset + delta.X, holderStart.Y.Scale, holderStart.Y.Offset + delta.Y)
end
end)
mobileButtons[key] = {holder = holder, btn = btn, setActive = function(state) setActive(btn, state) end}
return btn
end

-- Global (not local) so MeridianAntiDieWatch, defined earlier in the file,
-- can check this to know when Insta Reset is actively running and yield
-- to it (skip health-restore / Motor6D-fix while a reset is in progress,
-- since Insta Reset intentionally drops Health to 0 and changes Humanoid
-- state as its own reset mechanism -- Anti-Die would otherwise immediately
-- fight that by healing/re-stabilizing the character mid-reset).
_G.MeridianResetCooldown = false
local MeridianResetThread = nil
local MeridianResetCharacter = nil
local MeridianResetSuccessful = false
local MeridianStopResetSequence = false
local MeridianCameraLocked = false
local MeridianLockedCameraCFrame = nil
local MeridianResetMaxDuration = 0.05

function _G.MeridianInstantReset()
if _G.MeridianResetCooldown then return end
_G.MeridianResetCooldown = true
MeridianResetSuccessful = false
MeridianStopResetSequence = false
MeridianCameraLocked = false

local character = LP.Character
if not character then
_G.MeridianResetCooldown = false
return
end

local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
if not humanoid then
_G.MeridianResetCooldown = false
return
end

local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
if camera then
MeridianLockedCameraCFrame = camera.CFrame
MeridianCameraLocked = true
camera.CFrame = MeridianLockedCameraCFrame
end

MeridianResetCharacter = character
local isRespawning = false

MeridianResetThread = task.spawn(function()
local attempts = 0
local maxAttempts = 40
local originalHipHeight = humanoid.HipHeight

while character and character.Parent and humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0 and not isRespawning and not MeridianStopResetSequence do
if LP.Character ~= character then
isRespawning = true
break
end

pcall(function()
humanoid.HipHeight = 1e30
humanoid.AutoRotate = true

local rootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if rootPart then
rootPart.CanCollide = false
end

for _, part in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do
if part:IsA("BasePart") and part.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
part.CanCollide = false
end
end
end)

if not character or not character.Parent or not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 or LP.Character ~= character then
MeridianResetSuccessful = true
break
end

attempts += 1
if attempts >= maxAttempts then break end
task.wait(MeridianResetMaxDuration)
end

if not MeridianResetSuccessful and character and character.Parent and humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0 and not isRespawning then
pcall(function()
humanoid.Health = 0
end)
task.wait(0.1)
if not character.Parent or humanoid.Health <= 0 then
MeridianResetSuccessful = true
end
end

if not MeridianResetSuccessful and character and character.Parent and humanoid then
pcall(function()
humanoid.HipHeight = originalHipHeight
local rootPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
if rootPart then
rootPart.CanCollide = true
end
for _, part in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do
if part:IsA("BasePart") and part.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
part.CanCollide = true
end
end
end)
end

MeridianCameraLocked = false
_G.MeridianResetCooldown = false
MeridianResetThread = nil
MeridianResetCharacter = nil
MeridianStopResetSequence = false
end)
end

function _G.MeridianStopInstantReset()
MeridianStopResetSequence = true
if MeridianResetThread then
pcall(task.cancel, MeridianResetThread)
MeridianResetThread = nil
end
_G.MeridianResetCooldown = false
MeridianResetCharacter = nil
MeridianCameraLocked = false
end

LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
_G.MeridianStopInstantReset()
MeridianResetSuccessful = false
MeridianStopResetSequence = false
MeridianCameraLocked = false
end)

_G.MeridianCamLockConn = _G.MeridianCamLockConn or nil
if _G.MeridianCamLockConn then pcall(function() _G.MeridianCamLockConn:Disconnect() end) end
_G.MeridianCamLockConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
if MeridianCameraLocked and MeridianLockedCameraCFrame and workspace.CurrentCamera then
workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame = MeridianLockedCameraCFrame
end
end)

local defaults = {
drop         = UDim2.new(1,-132,0.5,-194),
autoRight    = UDim2.new(1,-66, 0.5,-194),
aimbot       = UDim2.new(1,-132,0.5,-128),
autoLeft     = UDim2.new(1,-66, 0.5,-128),
tp           = UDim2.new(1,-132,0.5,-62),
laggerNormal = UDim2.new(1,-66, 0.5,-62),
carryLagger  = UDim2.new(1,-132,0.5,4),
antiDesync   = UDim2.new(1,-66, 0.5,4),
carry        = UDim2.new(1,-132,0.5,70),
instaReset   = UDim2.new(1,-66, 0.5,70),
}

function _G.MeridianResetMobileButtons()
_G.MeridianMobileButtonScale = MeridianAutoMobile and 0.90 or 1.00
_G.MeridianMobileButtonImage = ""
_G.MeridianHideMobileButtons = false
_G.MeridianMobileButtonShape = "ROUNDED"
for key, defaultPos in pairs(defaults) do
local entry = mobileButtons[key]
local holder = entry and entry.holder
if holder then
holder.Position = defaultPos
end
end
_G.MeridianApplyMobileButtonSize()
_G.MeridianApplyMobileButtonsHidden()
_G.MeridianApplyMobileButtonShape()
_G.MeridianApplyMobileButtonImage()
_G.MeridianApplyMobileButtonShape()
do end
end

makeButton("drop", "DROP\nBRAINROT", defaults.drop, function(btn)
if runDropBrainrot then runDropBrainrot() elseif runDrop then runDrop() end
pulse(btn)
end)

local instantResetBtn = makeButton("instaReset", "INSTANT\nRESET", defaults.instaReset, function(btn)
if _G.MeridianInstantReset then
_G.MeridianInstantReset()
end
pulse(btn)
end)

if instantResetBtn then
instantResetBtn.Name = "Instant Reset"
instantResetBtn.Visible = true
local instantHolder = instantResetBtn.Parent
if instantHolder then
instantHolder.Name = "Instant Reset Holder"
instantHolder.Visible = true
instantHolder.Size = UDim2.new(0,58,0,58)
-- Meridian Duels -- don't force the default position here: makeButton()
-- above already restored the saved custom position (if one exists) via
-- _G.MeridianMobileButtonPositions. Overwriting it with defaults.instaReset
-- unconditionally was why a dragged Instant Reset position never stuck.
local savedInstaPos = _G.MeridianMobileButtonPositions and _G.MeridianMobileButtonPositions.instaReset
instantHolder.Position = (type(savedInstaPos) == "table") and tableToUDim2(savedInstaPos, defaults.instaReset) or defaults.instaReset
instantHolder.ZIndex = 1000
end
end

-- Exclusive toggle: only 1 of Auto Left/Auto Right/Bat Aimbot/TP Bat on at a time
local function exclusiveOff()
    if autoLeftEnabled then
        if _G.MeridianSetAutoLeft then _G.MeridianSetAutoLeft(false) end
    end
    if autoRightEnabled then
        if _G.MeridianSetAutoRight then _G.MeridianSetAutoRight(false) end
    end
    if _G.MeridianNormalAimbotOn then
        selectedAimbotMode = "Normal"
        if _G.MeridianToggleSelectedAimbot then _G.MeridianToggleSelectedAimbot() end
    end
    if _G.MeridianAntiDesyncAimbotOn then
        if _G.MeridianStopAntiDesyncAimbot then _G.MeridianStopAntiDesyncAimbot() end
    end
end

makeButton("autoLeft", "AUTO LEFT", defaults.autoLeft, function()
if not autoLeftEnabled then exclusiveOff() end
if _G.MeridianSetAutoLeft then _G.MeridianSetAutoLeft(not autoLeftEnabled) end
end)

makeButton("aimbot", "BAT AIMBOT", defaults.aimbot, function(btn)
if _G.MeridianSafeModeIsLocked and _G.MeridianSafeModeIsLocked() then return end
if _G.MeridianNormalAimbotOn then
    -- STOP: route through correct version
    if _G.MeridianAimbotVersion == "V3" and _G.MeridianStopV3Aimbot then
        _G.MeridianStopV3Aimbot()
    elseif _G.MeridianAimbotVersion == "V2" and _G.MeridianStopAimbotV2 then
        _G.MeridianStopAimbotV2()
    elseif _G.MeridianStopNormalAimbot then
        _G.MeridianStopNormalAimbot()
    end
else
    -- START: stop exclusives first, then route through correct version
    exclusiveOff()
    selectedAimbotMode = "Normal"
    if _G.MeridianAimbotVersion == "V3" and _G.MeridianStartV3Aimbot then
        _G.MeridianStartV3Aimbot()
    elseif _G.MeridianAimbotVersion == "V2" and _G.MeridianStartAimbotV2 then
        _G.MeridianStartAimbotV2()
    elseif _G.MeridianStartNormalAimbot then
        _G.MeridianStartNormalAimbot()
    end
end
if _G.MeridianRefreshAimbotVisual then _G.MeridianRefreshAimbotVisual() end
task.delay(0.03, function()
    setActive(btn, _G.MeridianNormalAimbotOn == true)
end)
end)

makeButton("autoRight", "AUTO RIGHT", defaults.autoRight, function()
if not autoRightEnabled then exclusiveOff() end
if _G.MeridianSetAutoRight then _G.MeridianSetAutoRight(not autoRightEnabled) end
end)



makeButton("laggerNormal", "LAGGER\nMODE", defaults.laggerNormal, function(btn)
if setSpeedMode then
if currentSpeedMode == "Lagger" then
setSpeedMode("Normal")
else
setSpeedMode("Lagger")
end
end
task.delay(0.03, function()
setActive(btn, currentSpeedMode == "Lagger")
end)
end)

makeButton("carryLagger", "CARRY\nLAGGER", defaults.carryLagger, function(btn)
if setSpeedMode then
if currentSpeedMode == "Lagger Carry" then
setSpeedMode("Normal")
else
setSpeedMode("Lagger Carry")
end
end
task.delay(0.03,function()
setActive(btn,currentSpeedMode=="Lagger Carry")
end)
end)

makeButton("antiDesync", "TP BAT", defaults.antiDesync, function(btn)
if _G.MeridianSafeModeIsLocked and _G.MeridianSafeModeIsLocked() then return end
if not _G.MeridianAntiDesyncAimbotOn then exclusiveOff() end
if _G.MeridianToggleAntiDesyncAimbot then
_G.MeridianToggleAntiDesyncAimbot()
end
task.delay(0.03, function()
setActive(btn, _G.MeridianAntiDesyncAimbotOn == true)
end)
end)

makeButton("tp", "TP DOWN", defaults.tp, function(btn)
if runTPFloor then runTPFloor() end
pulse(btn)
end)

makeButton("carry", "CARRY SPEED", defaults.carry, function()
if setSpeedMode then
setSpeedMode(currentSpeedMode == "Carry" and "Normal" or "Carry")
end
end)

task.defer(function()
task.wait(0.15)
local entry = mobileButtons and mobileButtons.instaReset
local holder = entry and entry.holder
if holder then
holder.Visible = true
holder.Size = UDim2.new(0,58,0,58)
-- Meridian Duels -- same fix as above: respect the saved custom
-- position instead of forcing defaults.instaReset every time this
-- deferred block runs (which happens ~0.15s after every button setup,
-- silently undoing any drag the user did).
local savedInstaPos2 = _G.MeridianMobileButtonPositions and _G.MeridianMobileButtonPositions.instaReset
holder.Position = (type(savedInstaPos2) == "table") and tableToUDim2(savedInstaPos2, defaults.instaReset) or defaults.instaReset
holder.ZIndex = 1000
end
local btn = entry and entry.btn
if btn then
btn.Visible = true
btn.Name = "Instant Reset"
end
end)

_G.MeridianApplyMobileButtonSize()
_G.MeridianApplyMobileButtonsHidden()

task.defer(function()
    task.wait(0.15)
    if mobileGui then
        mobileGui.Enabled = not (_G.MeridianHideMobileButtons == true)
    end
    pcall(_G.MeridianApplyMobileButtonsHidden)
end)

_G.MeridianMobileBtnConn = _G.MeridianMobileBtnConn or nil
if _G.MeridianMobileBtnConn then pcall(function() _G.MeridianMobileBtnConn:Disconnect() end) end
_G.MeridianMobileBtnConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
if mobileButtons.autoLeft then mobileButtons.autoLeft.setActive(autoLeftEnabled == true) end
if mobileButtons.autoRight then mobileButtons.autoRight.setActive(autoRightEnabled == true) end
if mobileButtons.aimbot then mobileButtons.aimbot.setActive(_G.MeridianNormalAimbotOn == true) end
if mobileButtons.antiDesync then mobileButtons.antiDesync.setActive(_G.MeridianAntiDesyncAimbotOn == true) end
if mobileButtons.carry then mobileButtons.carry.setActive(currentSpeedMode == "Carry") end
if mobileButtons.laggerNormal then mobileButtons.laggerNormal.setActive(currentSpeedMode == "Lagger") end
if mobileButtons.carryLagger then mobileButtons.carryLagger.setActive(currentSpeedMode == "Lagger Carry") end
end)
end)

task.defer(function()
task.wait(1)
pcall(function()
_G.MeridianApplyMovingEdgeLights(PlayerGui)
end)
end)

-- Meridian Duels -- Ensure every gradient/stroke built during initial
-- GUI construction (edges, pill borders, floating buttons) actually
-- picks up the current theme color, not just gradients created after
-- a theme is manually re-picked in Settings.
task.defer(function()
task.wait(0.3)
pcall(function()
if _G.MeridianRefreshGlowTheme then
_G.MeridianRefreshGlowTheme()
end
end)
end)

task.spawn(function()
while task.wait(5) do
if canSaveConfig and saveMeridianConfig then
pcall(saveMeridianConfig)
end
end
end)
